# 101: Chapter 101: Dylan, She's Your Childhood Friend!

A Champion-level Dragon-type Trainer!

Hearing Zinnia's words, Ilene and Lillie were both very surprised. A Champion-level trainer is at the top of the ranks in any region. Such an opponent is undoubtedly extremely formidable.

The news that Dylan and Zinnia would battle quickly spread through Meteor Village, attracting not only the elder but also many of the remaining Meteor villagers who came to watch.

In the open space in front of the village.

"Then I'll be the referee."

Itto stepped forward without hesitation. Unfortunately, Meteor Village was quite primitive and lacked scoreboards or similar facilities, but that wasn't a big issue.

"Now the battle between Dylan and Zinnia begins!"

"The battle rules are 6 vs 6! Both sides can switch Pokémon, and the battle ends when one side's six Pokémon are all unable to continue. The other side wins!"

"The battle starts!"

Itto waved a small flag and then stepped off the battlefield.

"Dylan."

Zinnia took out a Poké Ball, enlarging it and pointing it at Dylan, her face showing a bright and fearless smile, full of heroic spirit.

"Since Aster left, I have been honing myself and my Pokémon day by day, and I have finally become who I am now!"

"The fangs and claws of Dragons are their most powerful weapons! Can you withstand them?"

Seeing that Dylan remained unfazed, Zinnia threw the Poké Ball with force.

"Goodra!"

The Poké Ball opened, and a bipedal Dragon appeared before Zinnia.

It was a tall and robust figure, mostly purple, with very smooth skin, a thick and massive tail, and a layer of slime on its body.

This was Goodra, the final evolution of Goomy.

'Drip~ Goodra, Dragon-type, Dragon Pokémon

Ability: Hydration

Signature Moves: Draco Meteor, Muddy Water, Body Slam, Dragon Pulse, Rain Dance, Dragon Breath, Dragon Claw, Thunderbolt…

Description: This Pokémon is very affectionate toward people. It gets lonely when its trainer is not around and cries sticky tears.'

The first Pokémon was Goodra…

Lillie, who wasn't a trainer herself, clicked her tongue in admiration as she read the Pokédex information on Goodra. Although she wasn't a trainer, she knew that being called a 'late-blooming Pokémon' was extremely rare and valuable.

"That's really strong," Ilene said, her lips pressed together, her brows slightly furrowed.

Just from Goodra's aura, one could sense that its strength was likely very high.

On the battlefield.

"This Goodra is probably at least level 65 or higher."

Dylan carefully observed Goodra, pondering.

He had a Goodra himself, but his Goodra was probably not as high a level as Zinnia's.

In that case, he would need to slightly adjust his strategy. Although he had Articuno as backup, Zinnia's strength was higher than anticipated.

"Incineroar, prepare for battle."

Dylan threw out Incineroar's Poké Ball.

Incineroar landed, opening its arms and roaring fiercely at Goodra, showing a menacing pose.

"Oh~ Is this the evolved form of the Litten I sent?" Itto's eyes lit up as he took out his Pokédex to check Incineroar's information.

He nodded in satisfaction as he read, clearly proud of his son.

"Incineroar?" Zinnia recalled Incineroar's type.

Though she had never seen an Incineroar before, knowing its type was enough.

"Incineroar!"

Incineroar roared at Goodra, and under its imposing presence, Goodra's physical attack was lowered by one stage.

Intimidate ability!

"Goodra, use Muddy Water!"

Zinnia remained unperturbed and decisively commanded Goodra. Her Goodra specialized in special attacks.

Goodra let out a dragon's roar, and water-blue energy began to shine from its body.

Water-type Muddy Water!

"Fake Out!"

The black and red figure dashed toward Goodra with lightning speed and slapped Goodra hard on the face.

Goodra's Muddy Water was interrupted, and it staggered back, covering its face and retreating a few steps.

"Hmm?" Seeing this, Zinnia tilted her head slightly.

"Parting Shot!"

Incineroar, with one hand on its waist and the other pointing at Goodra, began to insult Goodra, causing its eyes to become increasingly fierce, its breathing heavy, and it was clear that Incineroar was cursing harshly.

After the taunt, Incineroar returned to Dylan's Poké Ball in a flash of white light.

Goodra's white light dissipated, and its physical and special attacks were further reduced by one stage.

"…" Zinnia looked somewhat confused.

Though her Pokémon was very strong, her experience in battling outsiders was limited; she mostly sparred with elders from her tribe.

Dragon-type battles were usually straightforward, but she was a bit taken aback by this tactic.

"Sableye! Prepare for battle!"

Sableye appeared before Dylan.

Zinnia recognized Sableye; it was a Pokémon of relatively low strength, and she wondered why Dylan chose it as his main.

Nevertheless, even though Goodra had been weakened twice by Incineroar, its power was still far beyond what Sableye could handle!

Goodra gathered dragon energy in its mouth.

"Fake Out."

The small Sableye dashed at Goodra at high speed and swiped its claws at Goodra's face.

Goodra's Dragon Pulse was interrupted again, and it covered its face, staring blankly at Sableye.

Zinnia was silent, and Sableye used Fake Out once more.

"Taunt!"

Sableye's eyes sparkled with an eerie light as it showed off, extending its small claws and making a show of bravado.

Goodra's forehead veins bulged, and its eyes seemed to flicker with flames. Its anger had reached its peak.

This…

Zinnia frowned, sensing that something was off.

"Goodra, Dragon Pulse!"

But no matter what, they needed to attack.

Goodra, usually gentle, was already extremely angry after two slaps and one taunt, and its dragon energy was now fully charged.

Just then, a beam of light struck Sableye, and it was returned to Dylan's Poké Ball by him.

After recalling Sableye, Dylan fell into deep thought.

Zinnia tightened her lips, her hand unconsciously clenching into a fist.

But after waiting for a while, Dylan still hadn't sent out his Pokémon. Acting as the referee, Itto immediately warned Dylan.

"What's wrong, Dylan? Delaying the battle like this is against the rules."

Everyone, including Zinnia, looked at Dylan in confusion.

"I was thinking, if I send out Incineroar again and then repeatedly switch with Sableye, that shouldn't be against the rules, right?"

Dylan hesitated for a moment but decided to voice his thoughts.

What the heck?!

Hearing Dylan's words, Itto and the others were stunned. They instantly understood what Dylan meant.

Yes, Dylan could indeed send out Incineroar again, using Intimidate plus Fake Out, then swap to Sableye, repeating the cycle.

It wouldn't take long before Goodra's physical and special attacks would be reduced to the minimum, turning it into a powerless opponent.

Moreover, once Dylan switched to Sableye for the second time and used Mean Look, Goodra would struggle to continue the battle.

"???"

Zinnia's eyes widened, her pupils dilating as she stared in shock at her childhood friend Dylan.

No way! How could you do this? You weren't like this before, Dylan!

"Technically, that's not against the rules, but Dylan..."

As the referee, Itto hesitated, trying to phrase it as gently as possible.

"Zinnia is your friend. You understand what that means, right, Dylan?"

The underlying message was clear: even if you want to play dirty, you shouldn't completely disregard basic decency! Plus, this is in her own village!

The silent elder: "..."

The embarrassed Lillie: "Hehe..."

Ilene: (—)

"I understand." Dylan sighed with some regret.

Indeed, since they hadn't seen each other for so long, he should at least give Zinnia some battle experience and not be too inhumane.

In that case...

"Drapion! Prepare for battle."

Dylan threw out his third Poké Ball.

Seeing that it wasn't Incineroar, Zinnia secretly breathed a sigh of relief. Though the strategy was a bit annoying, Dylan was still a good person! Just as gentle as before!

"A Poison/Dark-type Drapion, Dylan, are you a Dark-type specialist?" Zinnia recognized Drapion and curiously asked Dylan, noting that he had already used three Dark-type Pokémon.

"That's right. I'm using a Dark-type team." Dylan nodded.

"I see."

Zinnia understood why Dylan's strategy was so... unique. With a Dark-type team, no tactic would be too surprising, given their nature.

"Let's continue then!" Zinnia, now reinvigorated, flashed that spirited smile once again.

"Goodra! Dragon Pulse!"

For the third time, Goodra opened its mouth to gather dragon energy, and this time, it finally succeeded!

Goodra was practically in tears; this was the first time it had successfully unleashed a move in the entire battle.

"Toxic Spikes."

???

Zinnia looked at Dylan in confusion. Toxic Spikes?

A dragon-shaped wave of energy shot toward Drapion, hitting it directly and causing an explosion, but the Dragon Pulse didn't have a type advantage over Drapion.

Moreover, Goodra's special attack had already been weakened once.

Drapion let out a pained cry but withstood the attack, using its pincers to scatter the Toxic Spikes into the air. The spikes burst in midair, and a layer of purple fluid sank into the ground, causing the surface to glow with a dark purple light before returning to normal.

Zinnia's frown deepened. Unlike in Kanto, Toxic Spikes weren't well-known in Hoenn, especially to Zinnia, who had only recently left Meteor Village.

But Zinnia knew that Dylan wasn't a simple opponent, so those Toxic Spikes definitely had some trick behind them!

"It's here! Dylan's specialty, the Toxic Spikes strategy!" Ilene's eyes lit up, her tone excited.

"Sister Ilene, are Toxic Spikes really that powerful?" Lillie asked innocently.

"Very powerful! Dylan was the pioneer of this strategy! Now, the Toxic Spikes tactic is famous throughout Kanto!"

Though not for the best reasons, and Dylan has been cursed behind his back more times than one could count for it.

"Dylan is amazing!" Lillie gasped, covering her mouth in astonishment. Famous throughout Kanto, wow!

"Just watch, Lillie. This Goodra is already destined to lose."

Ilene's beautiful face was full of confidence as she envisioned Goodra's inevitable defeat.

(End of Chapter)

---

---

---

# 102: Chapter 102: My Dragon, Defeated Just Like That?

"Zinnia has been completely outplayed."

Itto stroked the stubble on his chin thoughtfully. He knew just how much people in Kanto hated the Toxic Spikes strategy.

"This child has exceptional talent, but she doesn't have much experience battling with others," the elder sighed. Zinnia's potential as a trainer was just as great as Aster's.

However, because she had been focused solely on training and raising her Pokémon, Zinnia lacked battle experience. The elder had agreed with Itto to let Zinnia battle Dylan partly for this reason.

Meanwhile, on the battlefield...

Zinnia was considering how to attack. Drapion had only one weakness—Ground-type moves. Goodra's physical attacks were decent enough.

However, after being hit twice by Incineroar's Intimidate and Parting Shot, Goodra's physical damage output was now far from ideal.

"Goodra, use Earthquake!" Zinnia commanded decisively.

Even if it wasn't at its best, a super-effective Earthquake would definitely deal more damage than a regular special attack.

Goodra raised a foot and stomped down hard, but just as its foot touched the ground, Goodra's movements froze, a look of pain crossing its face.

A strange, deep purple toxic light spread across Goodra's body—it was the effect of Toxic Spikes!

"What's this?"

Zinnia was stunned, her brow furrowing immediately.

"Pin Missile!"

A barrage of white Pin Missiles rained down, relentlessly striking Goodra, who had just finished enduring the effects of Toxic Spikes.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

The sound of continuous explosions filled the air, mingled with Goodra's pained cries.

Even though Pin Missile, a Bug-type move, wasn't super effective against Goodra, it still dealt solid damage, and it really hurt!

As the bombardment of Pin Missiles ended and the dust began to settle, Goodra reappeared, now covered in numerous wounds.

"It seems like Dylan has the upper hand," Lillie blinked her large eyes.

So far, Goodra had hardly touched Dylan's Pokémon, yet it had been attacked several times.

"Well, it's Dylan after all," Ilene smiled brightly.

Zinnia didn't know what the spectators were thinking, but she was definitely feeling the pressure, and she was unwilling to back down.

After all, her Goodra was supposed to be strong!

"Goodra, Earthquake!"

Zinnia urged Goodra, thinking that she had to land at least one hit.

Goodra lifted its foot and stomped down hard, causing the ground to shake violently. The shockwaves raced toward Drapion.

"Earthquake," Dylan chuckled lightly.

Drapion raised its tail and slammed it down on the ground, sending its own shockwaves forward.

The two Earthquake waves clashed violently, canceling each other out, and the battlefield returned to silence.

"Dragon Pulse!" Realizing that Earthquake wasn't going to work, Zinnia decided to try a long-range attack to create an opening.

"Drapion, charge in," Dylan ordered after a moment of thought.

Goodra began to gather pure dragon energy in its mouth, launching a swift Dragon Pulse.

But Drapion moved its legs, dodging the Dragon Pulse with surprising speed for its large size, and rushed toward Goodra.

Drapion was actually quite fast!

"Goodra, retreat and use Muddy Water!" Zinnia remained calm.

Goodra started to back away, but after only two steps, it froze again as the deep purple toxic light appeared on its body, causing it to wince in pain from the Toxic Spikes.

Again with the Toxic Spikes!

Zinnia's eyes widened. How could this be? Could the Toxic Spikes activate multiple times?

But in that moment, Drapion had already reached Goodra, its lightning-fast tail shooting out and wrapping around Goodra's throat, slamming it to the ground.

"Goodra!"

Zinnia stared in shock at Drapion's outstretched tail.

Goodra struggled desperately to break free from Drapion's grip, but the tail was like an unbreakable iron shackle, choking it and causing immense pain.

"Goodra! Use Thunder!"

"Cross Poison, interrupt it!"

Goodra's body began to crackle with golden electricity, but Drapion was quicker. Its tail tightened around Goodra's throat, lifting it into the air.

Its pincers glowed with a purple light, and a cross-shaped energy blade slashed out.

Boom!

The Cross Poison struck Goodra squarely, exploding on impact. Goodra let out a pained cry, the electricity around it dimming.

"Goodra!"

Zinnia was already reaching for Goodra's Poké Ball, ready to recall it. This wasn't going well.

"Drapion!"

"Dra~!"

Drapion let out a vicious growl, lifting Goodra higher with its tail before slamming it down hard.

Boom!

Goodra hit the ground with a heavy thud, the impact causing the ground to tremble. Goodra lay there with its eyes closed, enduring the pain.

But the next moment...

"Dra~!"

The deep purple toxic light flared up on Goodra's body for the third time. Goodra cried out in agony, its body writhing as it struggled to escape Drapion's grip.

But Drapion's tail held it down like an unbreakable iron clamp, pinning Goodra to the ground.

"Ah…"

Lillie covered her mouth in horror, looking at the pitiful state of Goodra. She felt goosebumps all over her body.

Poor Goodra!

"Earthquake!"

Drapion lifted its two front legs and slammed them down, using Earthquake!

Boom!

The physical shockwaves of Earthquake rippled out, hitting Goodra's body hard. The helpless Goodra, pressed against the ground, endured the full force of the Earthquake, and finally, its body went limp, its eyes swirling in defeat.

Seeing this, Zinnia, who had already taken out the Poké Ball, frowned deeply.

"Goodra is unable to battle. Drapion wins," Itto announced the battle result.

"It felt like Goodra was just taking hits the whole time…" Lillie murmured, watching as Zinnia recalled Goodra.

But this was supposed to be a fully grown 'late-blooming Pokémon,' so it should have been very powerful.

"No need to just feel it; that's exactly what happened." Lillie shook her head.

"Zinnia's Goodra is strong, but it never had a chance to show it. Dylan completely targeted it."

"Part of it is also because Zinnia isn't familiar with Dylan's battle style."

A proud pseudo-legendary Pokémon, and it didn't even get to do anything before going down…

Itto clicked his tongue lightly twice, thinking, "As expected of my son! Even a Champion-level ace was completely outplayed."

On the field.

Zinnia looked at the Poké Ball in her hand containing Goodra for a long time. My dragon… was defeated just like that?

Zinnia then raised her head to look at Dylan and his Drapion on the other side. After a while, a smile reappeared on her pretty face.

"Dylan, you're really strong," Zinnia remarked, though the intensity in her eyes only grew stronger.

"The battle has only just begun, right, Zinnia?" Dylan chuckled softly. In terms of levels, his Drapion certainly wasn't as strong as that Goodra.

But Goodra's physical defense was lacking, and after being weakened by Incineroar's Intimidate and subjected to Toxic Spikes, Drapion was able to shine as it did in the recent battle.

Yes, it nearly took down a Dragon-type pseudo-legendary without a scratch!

"That's right! The battle has only just begun!"

Zinnia took out a second Poké Ball and threw it high.

"My second dragon is this one! Altaria!"

With a melodious cry, a stunningly beautiful Altaria floated in front of Zinnia.

Its sky-blue body and fluffy wings, which resembled clouds, covered most of its form, with two ribbon-like feathers fluttering atop its head. It was indeed a very beautiful Pokémon.

'Beep~ Altaria, Dragon/Flying-type, the Humming Pokémon

Ability: Natural Cure

Moves: Dragon Pulse, Sky Attack, Perish Song, Moonblast, Cotton Guard, Safeguard, Defog (egg move)…

Description: It flies gracefully through the sky while singing a beautiful melody, captivating all who hear it.'

"This Altaria seems really strong~ Even stronger than Mom's."

Lillie blinked her bright eyes as she looked at the Pokédex entry for Altaria.

"Silly child, I can't compare to Zinnia."

Amelia didn't know whether to feel happy or amused when she heard this. How could her Altaria compare to Zinnia's? That was giving her way too much credit.

"Um…" Lillie seemed to realize she'd said something silly, so she touched her lips with a dazed expression, trying to use her cuteness to cover it up.

"A dragon-type traitor, huh? So it inherited Defog as well."

Dylan watched Altaria with great interest.

One can't deny it—Altaria is indeed very beautiful, and even more so after it Mega Evolves.

"Altaria, use Defog," Zinnia ordered decisively.

Altaria chirped sweetly as its cotton-like wings flapped, stirring up a powerful gust that swept across the field, clearing away all the Toxic Spikes on the ground.

"This is a bit troublesome."

Dylan pondered how to deal with Altaria. In reality, Altaria isn't an offensively strong Pokémon.

Even at the Champion level, it remains that way. The main problem now is that Altaria can fly, and it has high defenses.

Unlike Goodra, whose physical defense is weak, Altaria's defenses are quite balanced. The most important thing is that Altaria has a wide range of moves, including Safeguard, which protects it from status conditions. Moreover…

"Altaria, use Cotton Guard!" Zinnia ordered Altaria once again.

"Rock Slide!" Dylan didn't hesitate.

Drapion growled at Altaria as chunks of rock formed and shot towards the Altaria in the sky.

Rock-type Rock Slide!

Altaria's fluffy cotton wings suddenly puffed up even more, enveloping it like a cloud.

The rocks from Rock Slide hit Altaria dead on, and it cried out in pain as its body was pushed back. However, due to the fluffiness of its wings absorbing the impact, the damage it took was limited.

Just like Blastoise's shell or Drapion's flexible pincers, Altaria's cotton wings effectively absorb physical attacks—a feature that isn't represented in the games.

In the next moment, the 'cotton' returned to normal, and Altaria's body glowed with a white light.

The Grass-type move Cotton Guard, which sharply raises the user's defense by three stages!

Dylan raised an eyebrow. Zinnia really is choosing to power up Altaria. Well, it makes sense—if Altaria doesn't power up, its offensive capability is pretty mediocre.

"Altaria, use Dragon Dance!"

But after boosting its defense, Zinnia ordered Altaria to use Dragon Dance.

Altaria sang a beautiful melody as it flapped its cotton wings, moving its body in a mystical, ancient dance. Its body began to glow with a faint blue light.

This is the Dragon-type move Dragon Dance! It boosts the user's speed and attack.

And Drapion could only look up, glaring at Altaria as it performed its dance in the sky.

Alright, fine! So greedy, huh? You're not even considering my Drapion as a threat, are you?

Dylan hesitated, wondering if he should have Drapion start powering up as well or just bring out Articuno to finish off this showy, flashy dragon-type traitor.

(End of Chapter)

---

# 103: Chapter 103: He is Also a Draconid!

Zinnia! You forced me to do this!

Dylan's expression didn't change at all, but his hands moved swiftly, retrieving Drapion into its Poké Ball.

"Toxapex! Get ready for battle!"

Articuno was his secret weapon, so he had to save it for later. But that's okay! He still had his cute, shy, and harmless little Toxapex!

Like a domed tent, Toxapex landed in front of Dylan, its two tentacles slowly rising, revealing its true form hidden behind them.

"Oh~ it's that mischievous Toxapex," Itto's eyes lit up. He remembered it very well.

This Toxapex was the evolved form of the Mareanie he had sent to Dylan the first time. Back then, he had sent Dylan a Goomy, and this Toxapex was just sent along for the ride.

But now, it seems that the Goomy, which he had put so much effort into sending, was just an afterthought?

"Toxapex, huh..." Lillie recalled, Toxapex was something she had heard of before, after all, it was a Pokémon native to their Alola region.

"Let's see how well Dylan has raised it."

Itto was very interested in seeing what moves this Toxapex had learned.

'Baneful Bunker, Toxic, Recover, Toxic Spikes, Iron Defense, Pain Split...'

Itto's eyelid began to twitch uncontrollably. What the... how did his son turn out to be so... sinister?

Fortunately, as he looked further, Toxapex had indeed learned quite a few normal battle moves, which allowed Itto to breathe a sigh of relief.

"My son is just so healthy~" Itto sighed with emotion.

On the field.

"A Poison/Water-type Toxapex?"

Zinnia looked at Dylan with confusion. Wasn't he supposed to have a Dark-type team?

"Toxapex is special, so I added it to my team, especially since you're so strong, Zinnia."

Dylan smiled calmly in response. Having one or two Pokémon that don't quite fit the team type is a normal thing.

"I see," Zinnia understood. That made sense, considering how powerful she was! But since it was Toxapex, it probably knew a few Ice-type moves as well.

Zinnia's sharp eyes narrowed slightly. Water and Ice often go hand in hand, but that's okay. Now that her Altaria had been strengthened, it was very powerful!

And in the air, Altaria had completed its Dragon Dance, its aura becoming even more imposing.

"Altaria, use Dragon Pulse!"

"Light Screen."

Zinnia decisively ordered Altaria, while Dylan immediately commanded Toxapex. He didn't have it use Haze to eliminate Altaria's buffs.

Because Altaria's Cotton Guard increased its Defense, it didn't matter.

As for making Toxapex use Blizzard or Ice Beam...

There was really no need right now. Even if he used Ice Beam, it would just be shattered by Altaria's Dragon Pulse. Toxapex's offensive power was pitiful, even weaker than Altaria's.

Altaria opened its mouth and unleashed a fierce Dragon Pulse, while a semi-transparent Light Screen appeared in front of Toxapex, forming.

Boom!

The dragon-shaped pulse struck Toxapex hard and exploded. Toxapex grunted, but that was all. The semi-transparent Light Screen then merged into Toxapex's body.

The Light Screen that reduces special attack damage was completed!

"Altaria, use Sky Attack!"

The Light Screen's damage reduction for special attacks was too high, so Zinnia could only use physical attacks. Fortunately, Altaria had already used Dragon Dance once.

Altaria spread its wings wide, and a red aura began to surround its body before it suddenly dove down.

A Flying-type Sky Attack!

During the flight, the red aura 'boiled and burned,' eventually forming a majestic divine bird.

"Gotcha," Dylan chuckled. Zinnia was so 'straightforward.'

Coo~

The divine bird's cry echoed as the imposing Sky Attack descended from the sky, closing in on Toxapex.

"Toxapex, use Baneful Bunker."

Toxapex let out a low hum, spreading its two tentacles wide, and an eerie, deep purple barrier enveloped it.

Toxapex's signature move, the Poison-type Baneful Bunker!

"Hmm?" Seeing the strange deep purple barrier, Zinnia felt a bad premonition rising in her heart.

Something was wrong! But it was too late for regrets now.

Boom!

Altaria's Sky Attack slammed heavily into the Baneful Bunker barrier, the divine bird's aura fiercely striking. Ripples spread across the barrier, but it remained solid.

At the same time, purple fluid began to seep onto Altaria's body.

Soon, the power of the Sky Attack was exhausted. As Altaria retreated, its aura dissipating, a sinister purple glow began to flicker across its body.

Altaria suddenly froze, a pained expression appearing on its face.

It was poisoned?! Zinnia stared in shock at the poisoned Altaria. How could this be? Was it from that move that looked like Protect?

Zinnia furrowed her brow. She had underestimated her opponent! She should have used Safeguard on Altaria before attacking!

"Ice Beam."

Dylan, however, didn't hesitate to take advantage of the situation, pressing his attack while Altaria was vulnerable.

Toxapex raised its tentacles, aiming at Altaria, as icy blue energy coalesced. A jagged, winding beam of ice shot toward Altaria.

An Ice-type Ice Beam!

"Altaria, dodge quickly!" Zinnia's pupils contracted; Ice Beam's damage against Altaria would be too high! But how could it dodge? Altaria, struggling with the poison's encroaching pain, tried to flap its wings to evade, but it was a step too slow.

The Ice Beam struck Altaria's wing joint, and it let out a pained scream. The originally melodious voice now sounded sharp from the pain.

A quadruple-effective Ice Beam! Combined with Merciless's effect, even with Toxapex's low special attack, it was a massive blow to Altaria.

It wasn't knocked out only due to the level difference, Toxapex's low special attack, and Altaria's high special defense.

Even so, Altaria was severely injured, especially its wing, which was now drooping as its body plummeted.

"Another Ice Beam!" Dylan quickly ordered Toxapex to continue the assault.

"Come back, Altaria!" Zinnia wasn't foolish; she immediately recalled the heavily injured Altaria.

Altaria's ability is Natural Cure, so as long as it is recalled, it can heal its status conditions.

"You're amazing, big brother! You almost knocked out another one!" Lillie was thrilled, as her big brother was performing wonderfully! Even though Dylan's Pokémon were odd-looking and not very attractive, they were really strong! That champion-level successor, Zinnia, was completely suppressed!

"Dylan has really become strong!" Amelia also smiled with relief, her tone filled with pride.

"Amelia, Dylan isn't just good with Dark-types, is he?" The old lady's cloudy eyes showed some thought as she asked Amelia.

"Yes, Grandma, Dylan is also extremely proficient with Poison-types," Amelia replied.

"Just as I thought," the old lady nodded. She could tell from the way Dylan skillfully used Drapion and this Toxapex. Dylan is truly unique… mastering both Dark and Poison types…

But the old lady was still happy. After all, Dylan is Amelia's child, so he could be considered half a descendant of the Draconid people, right?

"Dylan is actually skilled with multiple types, but his other teams aren't fully assembled yet," Ilene quickly added. She believed Dylan's talent went far beyond what he had shown so far.

And wasn't Dylan already preparing a Ghost-type team? She believed his Ghost-type team would amaze everyone too!

"Does Dylan use Dragon-type Pokémon?" the old lady asked, looking at the elegant young woman.

"Hmm… I haven't seen him use them in battle, but Dylan does have a Goodra and a Dragonite as his main Dragon-types," Ilene thought for a moment before responding.

"I see…" The old lady fell into thought. If Dylan is really proficient with Dragon-types… No, considering Dylan's current battle strategies, it doesn't make sense for him not to use Dragon-types.

After all, there's not much to say about Dragon-type tactics; Dragons are simply superior in strength and physique compared to other types! Dragon-type Pokémon are powerful for no other reason than that they are Dragons!

However, Dragon-type Pokémon are difficult to train, and few trainers can truly master them.

"Since he has the blood of the Draconid people, he is naturally connected to Dragons," the old lady's kind face broke into a smile as she had an idea.

As a half-descendant of the Draconid people, how could he not have a Dragon-type team? Especially since Dylan already has a Goodra and a Dragonite, both of which are powerful Dragons.

Yes, Dylan must train Dragons! Dragons are the strongest type! The Draconid people revere their Dragon god (Rayquaza), which is why they have an unparalleled passion for Dragon-type Pokémon. They firmly believe that Dragons are the strongest Pokémon! Because in their eyes, their Dragon god is the strongest, isn't he?!

On the battlefield.

Zinnia's smile had slowly disappeared, and she stared solemnly at the 'ugly' Toxapex.

This creature… is difficult to deal with.

The Light Screen it has up is the main issue, and the fact that Altaria suddenly got poisoned is bothering Zinnia. She strongly suspects it was because of that purple version of Protect.

Otherwise, it doesn't make sense for Altaria to get poisoned so suddenly.

"Tyrantrum!"

After some thought, Zinnia sent out her third Pokémon.

Accompanied by a fierce roar, a dragon with an extremely ferocious appearance landed in front of Zinnia.

It looked like a huge, bipedal, dark red Tyrannosaurus Rex, with a strong and burly build. There were orange rock-like protrusions around its eyes and tail, giving it a savage and powerful aura.

It was Tyrantrum!

'Beep~ Tyrantrum, Rock/Dragon-type, the Despot Pokémon.

Ability: Strong Jaw Moves: Head Smash, Giga Impact, Horn Drill, Earthquake, Dragon Claw, Crunch, Rock Slide, Rock Polish (inherited)…

Description: A Pokémon from about 100 million years ago. Though it's brutal, its dignified manner gives it a slight royal demeanor.'

It looked incredibly fierce!

Lillie read the description on her Pokédex and thought that this Tyrantrum was probably very formidable.

But big brother will surely be fine!

"Tyrantrum, huh? Still relying on brute force. But Zinnia, using sheer power alone won't take down my Toxapex," Dylan raised an eyebrow and reminded Zinnia with a smile.

Although his Toxapex was weak in direct confrontations, it was incredibly difficult to deal with. Tyrantrum was indeed very powerful, but too bad, Bunker Down is perfect for countering such close-range brute force.

"Hmph~ Dylan, I'll show you that Dragon's power isn't something some petty tricks can defeat!"

Though Zinnia was at a disadvantage, she dramatically flipped her cape, looking incredibly cool.

The only reason she struggled earlier was that she didn't understand this Toxapex. This time, she would show Dylan that a mere Toxapex couldn't stop a Dragon!

Yes, Zinnia had already decided that if Tyrantrum couldn't deal with this small creature, she would immediately send out her ace, Salamence, to Mega Evolve and obliterate it!

As the heir of the Draconid people and a champion-level Dragon-type trainer, how could she not defeat a mere Gym Leader?!

How could she face others as the heir of the Draconid people if she couldn't? She might as well hand over her heir status to Dylan.

(End of chapter)

# 104: Chapter 104: Grandfather Must Assemble

Winning means being the successor; losing is just being a defeated dog!

Zinnia's eyes seemed to burn with flames. That's right, she would definitely win! Dylan was just a Gym Leader!

She wouldn't lose! She couldn't lose! She would win!

"Tyrantrum! Use Earthquake!"

Zinnia commanded with great momentum. She would prove that her Dragons were not some strange and meaningless little creatures that could be defeated!

Tyrantrum let out a deep, fierce roar, lifted a massive leg, and stomped down hard.

Earthquake!

Boom! Boom!

The entire battlefield shook violently, even affecting the spectators, including Lillie.

A wave of tangible Earthquake shockwaves surged forward, rapidly approaching Toxapex.

"Toxic Spikes."

Dylan glanced at Tyrantrum and gave the order.

Toxapex's two tentacles rose, and purple energy spheres began to form.

Zinnia's eyes narrowed. This Toxic Spikes again.

The Earthquake shockwave passed through Toxapex's body. The base of Toxapex's tentacles showed signs of pain; Earthquake was super effective against it. Even though Toxapex had an extremely high defense, the damage was still significant.

Tyrantrum's physical attack was also very high.

But the Toxic Spikes were not interrupted. Toxapex released the Toxic Spikes into the air.

The Toxic Spikes burst in the air like fireworks, with streams of purple liquid sinking into the ground. The ground flashed with a layer of purple eerie light before returning to normal.

"Tyrantrum!" Zinnia contemplated for a moment and signaled Tyrantrum. Toxic Spikes are intermittent triggers, and this unpredictability is too much.

It's better to have Tyrantrum trigger the Toxic Spikes now than to be suddenly eroded by them later during the attack.

Tyrantrum stepped forward, and with that step, deep purple toxic light covered its entire body. The erosion from the Toxic Spikes made Tyrantrum feel very uncomfortable.

"Recover."

Toxapex took the opportunity to recover, a white glow gathering around it. Recover is a Normal-type move that can restore a large amount of health.

After the recovery, Toxapex had almost completely healed from the damage caused by Earthquake.

Zinnia's expression was not so good. This thing could even recover? In that case, she had to attack continuously and defeat it in a short time.

But this meant she would have to fight up close. What about the Fortress?

"Tyrantrum, Dig."

Zinnia quickly thought through her options. She had to press the attack!

Tyrantrum quickly dug a hole, leaving only a hole in its place.

"Iron Defence." Dylan commanded decisively.

A metallic sheen covered Toxapex. Reflect, a Pyschic-type move, significantly boosts the user's defense.

Before Reflect was completed, the ground beneath Toxapex began to crack. Tyrantrum, surrounded by yellowish energy, crashed into Toxapex, sending it flying.

Toxapex cried out in pain. The Dig damage was also high, but Reflect had already been completed, increasing Toxapex's physical defense by two levels.

"Wild Charge!" Zinnia commanded without hesitation.

Tyrantrum roared and charged forward in a frenzied assault, using Wild Charge!

Dylan squinted slightly. Was this a genuine attack or a feint? Zinnia couldn't possibly be unaware that the previous Altaria's poisoning was due to Fortress.

Dylan hesitated. If he used Beneful Bunker now and Tyrantrum stopped attacking at the last moment, he would be deceived, and the situation could collapse.

However, seeing Tyrantrum's increasing speed, Dylan thought he might be overthinking it.

Zinnia was simply going all out.

"Benefull Bunker!"

The deep purple barrier appeared in front of Toxapex. Fortress!

Bang!

Tyrantrum's Wild Charge collided heavily with the Fortress barrier, and a violent gust of wind spread out. Purple fluid from the Fortress spread into Tyrantrum.

Zinnia, noticing this, realized it was a Toxic version of Protect! What a sneaky move!

Wild Charge ended, and the Fortress dissipated into light particles.

Tyrantrum took a step back, and toxic light shone from its body. Tyrantrum roared in pain. It was poisoned! And with the added erosion from Toxic Spikes, the dual poisoning made Tyrantrum suffer greatly.

"Ice Beam"

Toxapex wasn't idle either. Its tentacles raised and aimed at Tyrantrum, shooting a beam of icy blue energy.

Crack! Crack!

The Ice Beam hit Tyrantrum's face, the sound of frost forming incessant. Tyrantrum roared even louder.

Ice Beam is double of effectiveness against Tyrantrum, and with the Merciless ability, Toxapex's attack hits critical points.

Although Toxapex's Special Attack isn't high, Tyrantrum's Special Defense is also average, so the damage from Ice Beam was quite significant.

"Tyrantrum! Hang in there! Use Psychic Fangs!" Zinnia grit her teeth and shouted at Tyrantrum.

Everything was going as expected! With both poisoning and Toxic Spikes erosion triggered, Tyrantrum could use this time to defeat Toxapex in one go!

Tyrantrum, with its frost-covered, fierce face, roared angrily. The sonic waves from the roar made Toxapex feel like it wanted to cover its mouth and nose. This guy has such a strong stench!

The next moment, Tyrantrum lunged at Toxapex, its colorful fangs biting down hard on Toxapex's tentacles, lifting and shaking it violently.

Psychic Fangs of the Psychic-type!

Toxapex cried out in pain, its translucent barrier visible, being destroyed by the Psychic Fangs.

Psychic is effective against Poison, and with Tyrantrum's Strong Jaw ability, the power of Psychic Fangs was increased by 50%, dealing heavy damage to Toxapex!

"Just a pure brute," Dylan observed Tyrantrum's aggressive attacks with a raised eyebrow.

This guy was relying on sheer force for an all-out assault.

"Toxapex, Ice Beam!"

Toxapex, enduring the intense pain, raised its tentacles again, aiming at Tyrantrum's face, gathering icy blue energy.

"Tyrantrum! Shake it off!"

Tyrantrum threw Toxapex heavily onto the ground. The impact made Toxapex grunt, and its tentacles lost their aim due to the weight.

"Earthquake!"

Tyrantrum's roar was deafening as it lifted its strong foot and stomped down on Toxapex.

Earthquake!

Lillie nervously clasped her hands over her chest. Could Toxapex withstand this Earthquake?

"Ice Beam!"

Dylan commanded without hesitation. It was coming right up!

Toxapex struggled to raise its tentacles, gathering icy blue energy.

Just then, Tyrantrum's descending foot halted. Its face showed pain and horror as toxic light erupted.

The poison was eroding now!

Zinnia's pupils contracted. Tyrantrum was in a very bad state. If Toxapex wasn't finished now, there would be no chance left!

"Tyrantrum!!"

"Roar!!"

Hearing Zinnia's voice, Tyrantrum endured the pain from the toxins and stomped down with its powerful foot!

Crack! Crack!

Boom!!

Toxapex's Ice Beam struck Tyrantrum's face, and Tyrantrum roared in pain again. Its massive, fearsome body began to tilt and fall.

Thud~ Tyrantrum fell to the ground, unable to rise.

But the Earthquake had also been successful, even directly stomping on Toxapex.

Toxapex curled up in pain. The Earthquake shockwaves raged, shaking Toxapex violently.

"How is it?"

"Toxapex can't possibly hold on, right?"

"Tyrantrum seems to have lost its fighting ability in the end…"

"Dylan is really impressive, managing to fight so well against Zinnia."

The Draconid Tribe watching the battle murmured among themselves. Compared to the previous battles, this one was undoubtedly much more intense and thrilling.

The wild Tyrantrum's frenzied attack also provided a visual spectacle.

The Earthquake gradually subsided.

"Toxapex and Tyrantrum have both lost their fighting abilities."

Itto observed the two Pokémon and confirmed the result.

They ended up taking each other down…

Lillie and the others were somewhat surprised but also found it understandable.

"Thank you for your hard work. You'll be well rewarded after this," Dylan gently reassured Toxapex.

Perhaps Ice Beam shouldn't have been used in the end, but Recover? No, after Recover, Tyrantrum could still use Psychic Fangs.

Toxapex did well to take down Tyrantrum.

"Indeed, Zinnia, your Tyrantrum is very strong."

Dylan looked at Zinnia and smiled, praising her.

Although relying solely on brute force might seem a bit rough to Dylan, it had to be said that it was quite effective at times, just like Tyrantrum earlier.

It managed to take down Toxapex.

"This is only natural, Dylan. My power is not limited to this!"

Zinnia's face broke into a fearless, confident smile again. She knew her Dragons wouldn't let her down! The battle was exhilarating!

Even though it took 2vs1 to take down Toxapex, and Altaria hadn't contributed much damage, winning was still a proof! Toxapex was out of commission!

"Oh? Then I'll have to continue watching."

Dylan smiled and elegantly adjusted his collar.

Honestly, battling Zinnia was easier than he had imagined. He wasn't sure if it was because Zinnia's battle style was too straightforward or because his own strength was actually higher than he had expected.

Clearly, Zinnia's Pokémon strength levels were definitely not an issue.

The problem seemed to lie with Zinnia. Although her raw strength was fine, her command was slightly problematic.

Perhaps it wouldn't be noticeable against other trainers who also preferred direct confrontations, but against someone who plays tricks like Dylan, Zinnia had some trouble.

But that's understandable. Zinnia had only recently left the Meteor Village and was still growing.

"Hmph~ Then watch closely! My fourth Dragon is this one!"

Zinnia dramatically threw her fourth Poké Ball.

"Hydreigon!"

With a flash of white light, a fearsome Hydreigon appeared in front of Zinnia.

Its body was predominantly blue, covered in black fur, with three pairs of wings on its back, and the most unusual feature was its three heads!

"This is… Hydreigon!"

Lillie's eyes widened. First, Altaria, and now Hydreigon?!

'Ding~ Hydreigon, Dark/Dragon type, Brutal Pokémon

Ability: Levitate Moves: Dark Pulse, Draco Meteor, Dragon Pulse, Trick, Roar, Dragon Rush, Tri Attack, Dark Pulse...

Description: It bites anything that moves. Many legends mention Hydreigon destroying villages.'

Such a powerful Hydreigon!

"There's even a Hydreigon? That seems right."

Dylan watched the intimidating and powerful Hydreigon with a twinkle in his eye. Impressive!

Indeed, the Meteor People's heir had incredibly rich resources: Goodra, Hydreigon, and the ace Dragonite—three Dragon-type quasi-legends!

It was truly extravagant.

No matter what, after this battle, Dylan must insist that Zinnia take him to Meteor Falls. After coming this far, he must bring back a Dragon-type 'specialty'.

That's right, he meant Salamance!

Seeing Zinnia's lineup, Dylan decided that he would definitely assemble a team of six Dragon-type quasi-legends to face challengers.

Goodra, Dragonite, Salamence, Garchomp, Hydreigon, and Dragapult. Well, if he could replace Goodra with Dracovish, that would be even better.

Perfect!

No, Dylan is cheating. How could he only focus on quasi-legends? He must create the most luxurious Dragon-type lineup!

(End of Chapter)

# 105: Chapter 105: How could you have this thing in your team?

"Incineroar."

After a moment of thought, Dylan decided to send out Incineroar first.

The "Strongest Incineroar in the Universe" made its grand appearance once again, letting out a menacing roar at Hydreigon, whose three heads gave a cold, fierce glare.

Intimidate activated!

Under the invisible pressure, Hydreigon's white glow dimmed, and its physical attack was lowered by one stage.

"Incineroar is really useful!"

Itto couldn't help but exclaim. Throughout this battle, he realized that Incineroar was an exceptionally excellent Pokémon.

The strategy of combining Intimidate, Fake Out, and Parting Shot directly reduces the opponent's physical attack twice and special attack by one stage. It's a combination that fits any team.

Most importantly, although Dylan didn't have Incineroar face opponents directly, Itto knew very well that Incineroar itself is actually quite powerful!

After all, being one of the starter Pokémon of the Alola region, its combat power couldn't possibly be weak.

"Again with this Incineroar." Zinnia's face darkened, and her brows furrowed.

Just the sight of this fierce and muscular Incineroar made her sick! It's such a waste for it to be so big.

It's a pity Hydreigon wouldn't chase after it, otherwise, she'd definitely make it taste defeat!

There wasn't much else to say after that. With Fake Out followed by Parting Shot, under Hydreigon's cold and angry gaze, Incineroar retreated back into Dylan's Poké Ball.

Hydreigon ended up with its physical attack lowered by two stages and special attack by one, significantly reducing its combat power.

Zinnia didn't withdraw Hydreigon. There was no point; as long as Incineroar wasn't dealt with, any Pokémon that came out would have to endure this combo.

Right now, she wanted to see what Pokémon Dylan would use to counter her Hydreigon.

"Meowha!"

Dylan decisively threw out his fifth Poké Ball.

"Meow~"

With a lazy, magnetic purr, a humanoid, light green Pokémon elegantly stood up, arms spread in a haughty pose.

It was Dylan's Meowscarada!

"Oh~ This is Dylan's starter Pokémon."

Itto's eyes lit up as he observed the Meowscarada, whose lazy demeanor masked a deep sense of malice and arrogance.

He knew, of course, that Dylan's starter Pokémon was a Sprigatito, which had clearly evolved into this Meowscarada.

"Eh? Brother's starter Pokémon?"

Lillie's bright eyes also lit up. As everyone knows, for a trainer, the starter Pokémon holds a truly unique significance.

It's the first partner that accompanies you on your journey as a trainer. Regardless of its strength, its existence is extraordinary.

So, for most trainers, the starter Pokémon is either their ace or a key member of their team, or at the very least, their most important friend and partner.

Even if it eventually becomes less powerful than the rest of your team, its significance remains unparalleled.

Beep~ Meowscarada, Grass/Dark type, Magician Pokémon

Ability: Overgrow

Moves: Flower Trick, Leaf Storm, Grassy Terrain, Play Rough, Energy Ball, Knock Off, U-turn, Sucker Punch, Frenzy Plant…

Description: It skillfully distracts its enemies and then detonates a pollen-filled flower bomb it secretly planted.

Amazing!!

Lillie curiously watched the mischievous and arrogant Meowscarada. She wasn't sure how strong it was, but it certainly seemed powerful.

"A Grass/Dark type Meowscarada," Zinnia squinted slightly. "That type combination is four times weak to Bug moves."

However, Hydreigon only knew U-turn as a Bug-type move. Still, that wasn't a big issue.

Even if Hydreigon was weakened, there's no way it would lose to just a Grass-type cat!

"Hydreigon! Use Flamethrower!" Zinnia quickly launched her attack.

Hydreigon growled, and the mouths of its three heads filled with fire.

Indeed, Hydreigon might only have consciousness and a brain in its central head, but its left and right heads can still attack, though they lack the power of the central head. This is one of Hydreigon's natural advantages.

The next moment, Hydreigon flew to the front and above Meowscarada, firing three streams of flames toward the ground.

A Fire-type Flamethrower!

"Sucker Punch," Dylan said with a faint smile.

A sinister black light flickered in Meowscarada's eyes, and before the flames could hit, its light green figure vanished.

A Dark-type priority move, Sucker Punch!

"So fast!" Zinnia's eyes sharpened.

You could faintly see the extremely swift light green figure sprinting, then leaping high behind Hydreigon.

With its superior jumping ability and the acceleration of Sucker Punch, Meowscarada was behind Hydreigon in the blink of an eye.

"Hydreigon, it's behind you, use Flamethrower!"

Zinnia quickly commanded Hydreigon.

Hydreigon's two side heads immediately turned backward, red flames gathering, and it used Flamethrower!

"Meow~!"

But Meowscarada did a backflip, its right foot surrounded by a dark aura, landing a heavy kick on Hydreigon's back.

A Dark-type Sucker Punch!

Hydreigon grunted; the damage from Sucker Punch wasn't much, but the impact knocked it down slightly in altitude.

But that slight drop meant that Hydreigon's double Flamethrower missed Meowscarada, shooting past below it.

Meowscarada landed right on Hydreigon's back.

Hydreigon sensed something on its back, glancing back at Meowscarada with an angry roar.

"Hydreigon! Ascend rapidly!"

Zinnia's expression grew grim. She needed to shake off Meowscarada, or Hydreigon would have trouble attacking.

Hydreigon suddenly shot upwards, trying to use high-speed maneuvers to shake off Meowscarada from its back.

"Play Rough!" Dylan slowly raised a grin.

What?!

Zinnia's pupils contracted. This Grass cat actually knew Play Rough?!

At this moment, Meowscarada clung tightly to the black fur on Hydreigon's neck with one paw, while its other arm glowed with pink energy.

A Fairy-type Play Rough!

And Hydreigon, a Dark/Dragon type, is four times weak to Fairy moves!

"Hydreigon! Dive down!" Zinnia shouted urgently.

Hydreigon quickly spun around and began a high-speed dive.

Hydreigon's speed kept increasing, and Meowscarada found it increasingly difficult to maintain its balance, almost falling off. Fortunately, it managed to grab onto the fur on Hydreigon's neck.

Meanwhile, Meowscarada's left paw kept hammering at Hydreigon.

Play Rough was very painful. Hydreigon roared in agony, its body wobbling and struggling to fly straight!

Even if Meowscarada couldn't attack with full strength in this state, Play Rough was still too much for Hydreigon.

After the fourth hit from Meowscarada, which essentially amounted to a full Play Rough attack, Hydreigon finally couldn't hold on any longer and fell with a wail.

Boom~!

Under Zinnia's grim expression, the Hydreigon crashed heavily to the ground. Dust billowed up, and the ground trembled slightly, showing just how hard it had fallen.

"Play Rough!"

But Dylan didn't wait for the dust to settle before ordering Meowscarada to attack again.

The previous Play Rough had severely injured Hydreigon; another hit would be perfect.

Through the hazy dust, Meowscarada remained on top of Hydreigon, its sharp claws and fangs, glowing with a pink aura, continually striking at Hydreigon.

Hydreigon's pained growls echoed again. When the dust finally began to clear, Meowscarada gracefully stood up and walked back to Dylan with elegant cat-like steps.

At the bottom of the pit, Hydreigon was already unconscious.

Meowscarada's unsuccessful initial surprise attack, the heavy impact from the fall, and two quadruple-effective Play Roughs—Hydreigon simply couldn't withstand it.

Silence! The crowd fell completely silent!

The Draconid people stared at the defeated Hydreigon with strange looks in their eyes. Zinnia's Hydreigon was her strongest Dragon-type after her ace, Salamence!

Yet now it had been defeated without accomplishing anything... well, it did do something: it spewed fire twice.

It was clear that the main reason was that the agile Meowscarada found an opportunity to get on Hydreigon's back, but regardless of the process, the outcome was that Hydreigon was utterly defeated.

Zinnia was also completely silent, staring at Hydreigon, lost in thought.

"Hydreigon is unable to battle. Meowscarada wins."

Itto announced the result of the battle.

"Yay! Meowscarada is amazing!" Lillie cheered, waving her tiny fists. As expected of her brother's first Pokémon! Who cares about Hydreigon, the pseudo-legendary? Haha, we took it down easily!

Zinnia recalled Hydreigon and stared at its Poké Ball for a long time, taking a deep breath to calm her turbulent emotions.

Zinnia's gaze toward Dylan had subtly changed as she took out another Poké Ball and threw it.

"Salamence!"

"Roar~!"

A dragon's roar echoed through the sky as crimson wings spread wide. A fearsome, incredibly fierce dragon looked down at Meowscarada with its savage eyes.

A four-legged dragon Pokémon with striking, blood-red wings, a long tail, and a deep blue body.

It was none other than Salamence, the pseudo-legendary Dragon Pokémon! The aura of dragon's majesty spread, and even Lillie could sense something was off. This Salamence was clearly stronger than Zinnia's previous Pokémon!

"Another pseudo-legendary Dragon-type..."

Lillie took out her Pokédex and pointed it at Salamence.

"Beep~ Salamence, Dragon/Flying type, the Dragon Pokémon.

Ability: Intimidate

Moves: Double-Edge, Flamethrower, Zen Headbutt, Dragon Claw, Dragon Rush, Hyper Beam, Hurricane...

Description: With wings that finally grew, it soars in the vast sky, spewing flames in excitement and turning the ground into a burning field."

No doubt, an extremely powerful Salamence!

But what happened next shocked Lillie and Ilene beyond words.

"Salamence!"

Zinnia raised her arm, and the Mega Bracelet on her leg lit up with a brilliant light. Almost simultaneously, the Mega Evolution Stone on Salamence's neck shone, enveloping it in a thick aura of evolutionary energy.

Mega Evolution, a step beyond evolution!

"Is that an evolution?"

"How? Salamence can evolve further?"

Lillie and Ilene watched in astonishment as Salamence underwent another evolution mid-air. How was this possible?!!

Soon, the light of evolution faded, revealing a completely new form of Salamence to everyone.

The most notable difference from its regular form was the magnificent, blood-red wings. They twisted and merged to form a massive crescent shape, a spectacular and awe-inspiring sight.

Its head, neck, and body were sleeker than before, and its limbs had shortened, but its tail had grown longer and more slender.

This was Mega Salamence!

Meowscarada's beautiful cat-like eyes focused on Mega Salamence, and its expression grew serious. As a Pokémon, it could more directly sense the immense and overwhelming pressure.

This opponent was incredibly strong!

"An impressive Mega Salamence, indeed. But, Zinnia?"

Dylan observed the Mega Salamence for a while before looking toward Zinnia.

"???"

Zinnia returned a puzzled look.

"Could it be that you're getting serious just because Hydreigon got taken down?"

Dylan thought for a moment and chose his words carefully to ask Zinnia. Why else would she suddenly bring out her ace and Mega Evolve without saying a word?

"Of course not." Zinnia's expression stiffened, but then she waved her hand dismissively, pretending to be nonchalant.

"Battles are normal in Pokémon battles. I don't take them to heart. I'm not that kind of person."

A bright smile reappeared on Zinnia's face.

"Well then, Dylan, this Mega Salamence is my strongest ace! Please experience its power to the fullest!"

"I can feel it. To show my respect, I must use my own ace!"

Dylan recalled Meowscarada and smiled as he took out a Premier Ball.

"Oh?" Zinnia was quite curious. Dylan's ace, huh?

Then...

"Yiao~"

A clear, melodious cry rang out, and a legendary ice-blue bird gracefully flapped its wings, floating in front of Mega Salamence. A soft blue light shimmered across its feathers.

The legendary Articuno!

"???"

Zinnia's expression froze, staring at Dylan with a face full of question marks.

It's one thing to have a Sableye on your Dark-type team, but you even added this thing?

(End of chapter)

# 106: Chapter 106: The Dragon Type Doesn't Seem That Strong

In the sky, a graceful and magnificent Articuno faced off against a fierce and intimidating Mega Salamence.

"Articuno is so beautiful~"

Lillie's eyes sparkled like little stars, and she clasped her hands to her chest, gazing longingly at the Articuno that was slowly flapping its wings in the air, her girlish heart bursting with admiration.

Oh wow~ She really loves this elegant, noble, and magnificent Ice bird! It's just too beautiful!

"Right? And when Articuno flaps its wings, frost falls down. When it's hot outside, just a couple of wing flaps will make it super cool!"

Ilene, feeling like she had the right to speak, smiled and said.

"That's amazing!!" Lillie felt even more in love.

But in reality, apart from these two, everyone else had extremely strange expressions at this moment, especially the Draconid people, whose gazes toward Dylan were starting to change.

"Dylan, something's off with your team, isn't it? Weren't you supposed to be using a Dark-type lineup?" After a moment of silence, Zinnia finally couldn't hold back and questioned Dylan.

People shouldn't deceive each other like this! What happened to the trust between people?

You clearly said you were using a Dark-type team, and we even dealt with your Toxapex earlier, but now you're bringing in an Articuno?!!

Isn't this targeting? This is obviously targeting, right?! This is totally targeting, you jerk!!!

Even Zinnia was starting to panic at this moment. Even with a Mega Evolution, Mega Salamence has a quadruple weakness to Ice.

And it's not just any Ice-type Pokémon; it's Articuno, the legendary Ice-type Pokémon!

It would have been one thing if you were just targeting me with an Ice-type, but you're targeting me with a legendary Ice-type Pokémon? Is this how you treat a childhood friend?

We haven't seen each other in years! We even played in the mud together when we were kids!

"That's right, so my team has four Dark-type Pokémon," Dylan shrugged with an innocent look.

I said it was a Dark-type team, but I never said it was purely Dark-type. There's nothing wrong with that.

"Zinnia, it's mainly because you're so strong, you know? I had no choice but to use my trump card, Articuno."

"Too bad I haven't captured the Ancient Blade Leopard yet. Otherwise, I wouldn't need to use Articuno."

Yes, the Ancient Blade Leopard, one of Paldea's legendary 'Treasures of Ruin,' is a Dark/Ice-type legendary Pokémon.

One of the few major C-rank Pokémon among Ice-types. Purely in terms of combat strength, the Ancient Blade Leopard is much stronger than Articuno and could still fit into a Dark-type team.

"???"

Zinnia was utterly baffled, feeling like her face was full of question marks. Is this even human speech?

"Fine, fine, fine!!"

Zinnia gritted her teeth and finally squeezed out those three words through clenched teeth.

Dylan, well done!

As expected, the kind Dylan I remembered has been swept away by the wind. The one before me now is the wicked, shameless Gym Leader Dylan!

"Zinnia, you're the heir of the Draconid people. Can't you overcome this little setback? Or do you always act like this when you encounter Ice-types?"

Dylan sighed and spoke to Zinnia in a disappointed tone.

Zinnia was startled by his words.

She suddenly realized that Dylan was right. Why was she so emotionally shaken at the sight of Articuno? She was the heir of the Draconid people, a noble Dragon-type Trainer!

Even against Ice-type Pokémon, she must not back down! An Articuno is nothing to be afraid of!

"Hmph! Dylan, you're underestimating me!"

Zinnia flashed her characteristic bright and fearless smile, tossing her cape back with renewed vigor.

"I am Zinnia! The heir Zinnia!"

At this moment, Zinnia's clear voice was full of spirit—so cool!

"You didn't disappoint me," Dylan smiled, offering praise.

"Of course! I'm coming for you, Dylan!"

"I'm ready. Bring it on, Zinnia!"

"Salamence, Stone Edge!"

Zinnia swung her arm with force. Even if it's Articuno, it couldn't possibly defeat her Mega Salamence!

The bond between her and Salamence wouldn't be shattered by a mere Ice-type! Moreover, Articuno is quadruple weak to Rock, and her Salamence knows a Rock-type move, so who's really got the upper hand here?

Yes, we can win!

Mega Salamence roared, a sound that echoed through the heavens. Sharp stones materialized in front of it and then shot towards Articuno at high speed.

This is the Rock-type move, Stone Edge!

"Agility," Dylan responded with a calm smile.

Articuno let out a high-pitched cry, and its body began to move at high speed. As it moved, white light kept flashing across its body, and its speed continued to increase, easily dodging the incoming stones.

The Psychic-type move, Agility, can increase the user's speed.

"Blizzard!"

The next moment, Articuno flapped its wings heavily, unleashing a fierce, bone-chilling snowstorm that covered the sky and earth. The range was massive.

"Salamence! Hurricane!"

Zinnia's face was serious. The range of this Blizzard showed just how powerful this Articuno was.

Mega Salamence's crescent-shaped wings flapped with equal force, unleashing a violent wind mixed with wind blades, charging toward the Blizzard.

The Flying-type move, Hurricane!

Boom!

Blizzard and Hurricane, two powerful moves, clashed mid-air and finally exploded with a loud bang. The terrifying gusts spread in all directions, even changing the color of the sky.

"So strong!"

The overflowing wind made it hard for the spectators like Lillie to even open their eyes. They weren't too close or too far, but these shockwaves were blowing down from the sky!

"Even its special attacks are this powerful," Dylan narrowed his eyes slightly.

As expected of Zinnia's ace, Mega Salamence. When Blizzard and Hurricane collide, Hurricane should be at a disadvantage.

Because Ice counters Flying, this type advantage would show in the clash of moves as well. In other words, under normal circumstances, Blizzard should definitely overpower Hurricane!

Yet, Mega Salamence's Hurricane was evenly matched with Articuno's Blizzard and even pushed back a bit. This shows just how strong this Mega Salamence is.

Even though Mega Salamence can be a mixed attacker, its physical attack is much stronger than its special attack.

This Salamence is at least level 70 or higher! And Mega Evolution greatly boosts Salamence's strength.

"As expected!" Zinnia's face lit up with excitement. As expected, it can be done!

Even Articuno is just like that!

But just as Zinnia was feeling elated, snow suddenly began to fall from the sky.

"What's this?"

Zinnia reached out to catch a snowflake, watching it melt in her palm with a look of confusion. How could it be snowing in Meteor Village?

"Hail, Articuno used Hail."

Dylan answered her. Yes, right after Blizzard and Hurricane exploded, Articuno used Hail to change the weather to a snowy day.

In snowy weather, the defense of Ice-type Pokémon is boosted.

Despicable!

Looking at the dense snowfall, Zinnia gritted her teeth. Dragons hate Ice, and at this moment, Mega Salamence was uncomfortably shaking the snow off its body.

And because the heavy snow was obscuring her vision, Zinnia was finding it hard to keep track of Articuno's movements.

"Should I have Salamence use Sunny Day? But…"

Using Sunny Day takes time, and what if Articuno takes the opportunity to attack Mega Salamence?

Should I switch out Salamence? But the remaining Pokémon are an Altaria and a Noivern, both low on health and both quadruple weak to Ice…

Even if they both went out together, Articuno's Blizzard might just knock them both out in one go. Even at full health, Noivern would struggle to withstand Articuno's Blizzard!

Damn you, Dylan! Using weather effects in secret! Shameless!!

"Articuno, Blizzard!" Dylan commanded with a smile. The confident and composed young man in black was utterly captivating at this moment.

Articuno's melodious cry echoed through the heavy snowfall, and the wind grew piercing. Mega Salamence cautiously surveyed its surroundings.

But all around was a blinding white blizzard; even Articuno's figure was no longer visible. From where would the blizzard strike?

Whoosh~ Whoosh~

A fierce, biting blizzard surged in from the side. Mega Salamence's keen senses detected it immediately.

"Hurricane!" Zinnia commanded instantly.

Mega Salamence flapped its blood-red wings, sending a violent tornado roaring toward the incoming blizzard.

The fierce gusts dispersed the snowflakes all around.

But Zinnia saw clearly that there was no sign of Articuno behind the blizzard! How could this be?!

"Wait! Salamence, watch out on your left!"

Zinnia quickly scanned the area, her pupils suddenly narrowing. She saw that, somehow, Articuno's elegant and graceful form had already flown to Mega Salamence's left side.

How could it be so fast?! That's right, it had used Agility to boost its speed earlier.

Realizing this, Zinnia's face changed dramatically.

"Ice Beam."

A cold blue energy gathered at Articuno's beak, and a twisting Ice Beam shot rapidly toward Mega Salamence.

"Salamence! Use Protect, quickly!"

Mega Salamence tried to turn around and use Protect, and the green barrier started to form, but before it could fully materialize, the Ice Beam hit it square in the chest.

Crack! Crack!

The sound of ice forming was continuous, and in a second, a thick layer of ice had formed on Mega Salamence's chest.

Mega Salamence roared in pain. The Ice Beam's damage was severe due to its Ice weakness! The intense pain and cold were unbearable for Mega Salamence, making it wobble and almost lose its balance.

Zinnia's face turned extremely grim. This was bad!

"Blizzard!"

Dylan smiled lightly and shook his head. It was time to end this.

Snowflakes began to fall again. Articuno flapped its wings powerfully, unleashing a fierce and powerful snowstorm.

Mega Salamence, just hit by Ice Beam, had only just steadied itself. The Ice Beam had dealt a heavy blow, and it needed a moment to recover.

But its opponent wouldn't give it that chance.

"Salamence! Dodge quickly!" Zinnia shouted, unwilling to give up.

In this state, Protect probably wouldn't work, let alone Hurricane. But could it dodge?

Mega Salamence struggled to flap its wings, trying to fly up and avoid the powerful Blizzard, but the storm's range was too vast.

The brutal snowstorm mercilessly engulfed Mega Salamence, which hadn't managed to escape the attack's range.

When the blizzard finally subsided, Mega Salamence collapsed to the ground, covered in frost.

With a flash of light, Mega Salamence reverted from its Mega Evolution form and fainted.

Dylan's Articuno's Ice Beam and Blizzard were just too much for it to handle.

Zinnia was left numb, staring blankly at her defeated ace.

"Salamence is unable to battle. Articuno wins," announced the referee, his breath visible in the cold air.

Snow was still falling from the sky, and the temperature had dropped. Amid the snowstorm, Articuno continued to flap its wings gracefully. Its ice-blue feathers seemed to shimmer with a faint blue light, divine and beautiful.

Finally snapping out of it, Zinnia tightly pressed her lips together and recalled her fainted Salamence. She stared at the elegant and noble Articuno, feeling lost.

With her ace Salamence down, how could she continue the battle?

It seemed impossible. How could Altaria and Noivern fight Articuno?

There was no way to win this, right?

Should she just surrender? But as an heir, that didn't seem right, especially with so many elders watching.

If only Salamence wasn't four times weak to Ice, Zinnia thought wistfully.

If it wasn't four times weak to Ice, it wouldn't have been taken down by Articuno in just two moves.

She suddenly felt like maybe a pure Dragon-type team wasn't that strong after all... Even though her grandmother had said she was already at Champion level, she'd been crushed by Dylan, a mere Gym Leader.

Dylan was truly impressive. Are all Gym Leaders this strong nowadays? It's terrifying.

Or maybe she was actually weak? Had her grandmother been lying to her? Was she not really at Champion level? The heir to a legacy wondered, feeling a bit dejected.

Maybe she should start training some Pokémon of different types in the future? That Incineroar seemed pretty good, and there were no clan rules saying she couldn't use other types of Pokémon.

(End of Chapter)
