# 119: Chapter 119: How Beautiful It Is!

Dylan and his two companions arrived in Saffron City at dusk. According to the information from Dylan's informants, N had also arrived in Saffron City that afternoon.

"One suite, please."

The three of them checked into a hotel and booked a suite. It was already late, so they knew they'd need to rest for the night.

After resting in the room for a while, Dylan headed to the training ground in the hotel's backyard.

Though the world clearly separates daily life from trainers, high-end hotels like this one typically have training grounds and battle areas.

"Buui~"

"Pikachu"

"Meow~"

Dylan released his Pokémon. Meanwhile, a certain plump 'Clefairy' had already eaten its fill and was now sound asleep in his bag.

That's right—Dylan had brought his 'Clefairy' along on this trip as well.

"Eat as much as you can. Growing stronger requires good health," Dylan smiled, gently rubbing Pikachu's head while the nearby Eevees curiously watched each other.

"Oh right, Eevee," Dylan said as he picked up the Eevee wearing an Everstone.

"Bui~" The Eevee affectionately nuzzled Dylan's cheek, its voice sweet and soft, full of endearment.

"You clingy little fox," Dylan gently tapped Eevee's nose with a warm smile.

"Since you still want to evolve, take a look at these pictures. Which form do you want to evolve into?"

Dylan let his Rotom Pokédex hover in front of Eevee, displaying the different evolutions, excluding the Water, Fire, and Electric types.

"Bui…"

The other Eevee, watching from the side, seemed both envious and a little downcast. It was different from the others, unable to evolve no matter what.

"Don't worry. Even without evolving, I believe you're still the best Eevee," Dylan noticed and tenderly squeezed its cheeks.

The Eevee was momentarily stunned but then smiled adorably, its large eyes narrowing into crescent moons.

Meanwhile, the Eevee in Dylan's arms bit its paw, looking at the various evolution forms on the screen with a confused expression. Its little head was spinning.

"Bui!!" But when it saw one particular image of an Eevee evolution, it suddenly became excited, pointing at the picture and calling out to Dylan.

"Do you like this one?"

"Bui~!"

"Alright, then we'll evolve into this one," Dylan nodded with a smile.

"Buibui~"

Eevee happily snuggled into Dylan's chest, its long ears swaying back and forth.

"But if you want to evolve into this form, you'll need to learn a special move from our Eevee here," Dylan said, referring to the other Eevee.

"Bui~!"

"Good, very determined! Keep it up," Dylan smiled warmly.

And then…

"You've been watching for a while now. Are you not going to show yourself, mysterious sir?" Dylan turned, still holding Eevee, and looked in the direction behind him.

Footsteps echoed as a tall, slender, handsome young man with green hair slowly emerged from behind a tree. His dull eyes gazed at Dylan and his Pokémon.

Dylan raised an eyebrow, surprised at the coincidence. Or rather, it wasn't entirely coincidental—he had chosen this hotel knowing N would stay here as well.

Behind N stood five Pokémon: Zoroark, Archeops, Vanilluxe, Klinklang, and Carracosta.

"I didn't expect to see someone else letting their Pokémon out to stretch this late at night," Dylan said to N with a smile.

N remained silent, his gaze shifting toward the Pokémon. His previously dull eyes flickered with emotion.

"Your Pokémon… they all really like you," N said calmly.

"I treat my Pokémon as important friends. If they didn't like me, I wouldn't force them to," Dylan replied. "But sir, your words are interesting. You're quite special, aren't you?"

Dylan looked at N with curiosity.

"You're special as well," N replied without denial, his gaze fixed on Dylan. N had been special since childhood, but the man before him was equally unusual.

N could sense a comforting and familiar aura from Dylan, one that made him want to approach. But N quickly suppressed that feeling.

There was no doubt—this man was no ordinary person.

"That's true. I've been blessed by nature since birth, gifted with the ability to heal Pokémon," Dylan said as green light glowed from his fingertips, which he gently placed on Eevee's head. Eevee closed its eyes in contentment.

Seeing this, N's eyes rippled with emotion. This man… he was special like himself! It gave N a sense of kinship.

Because of his own special abilities, N had been treated as a monster and shunned by others, until Ghetsis took him in.

"But no matter how special someone is, this world is shared by both humans and Pokémon. Pokémon are humanity's greatest treasure, and humans can be the best friends of Pokémon. Both are integral parts of this world," Dylan said with a smile, playfully petting the Pikachu on his shoulder.

N fell silent at Dylan's words.

"What do you think? You're special like me," Dylan smiled, asking N.

N's dull eyes flickered again as he picked up a nearby Oddish that had quietly wandered by. Oddish didn't resist N; in fact, it leaned into him affectionately.

"I don't agree with what you said," N finally spoke.

"Not all Pokémon and humans are happy. In many places we can't see, people are hurting Pokémon."

"They're all suffering."

N gently stroked Oddish's leaves, his voice filled with sadness. That's just the kind of person he was.

Because he could feel Pokémon's emotions, he was acutely aware of the pain endured by those hurt, abused, or abandoned by humans.

"There are far too many people who treat Pokémon as mere tools, exploiting them, using their power to fulfill their desires."

N's tone remained calm, but his emotions were already starting to stir beneath the surface.

"You're right, but that's not the majority," Dylan shook his head.

While there are dark corners in the world, most people strive for goodness and light, a result of the Pokémon League's efforts.

"Yet these things are still happening, even now. Somewhere, there's probably someone discarding their Pokémon like an unwanted tool," N said, his eyes dim.

"And why? Simply in the pursuit of strength."

"So what we should do is change that minority as well, right?"

Dylan nodded. He didn't deny N's words. Even in the anime, Ash encountered an abandoned Charmander shortly after starting his journey, a clear example of this reality.

But to Dylan, it wasn't surprising. Humans are complex beings with a mix of emotions, and even in a Pokémon world mostly filled with kindness, the debate over whether people are inherently good or evil remains unresolved.

The Pokémon League has been working hard to guide people toward treating Pokémon with kindness, but unfortunately, some cruelty still lingers.

"There's no need to change them, nor can we change them," N shook his head.

He had his own ideals. Change? Changing those who are already consumed by greed and darkness? No, N had accepted that wasn't possible.

"As long as humans and Pokémon are separated, the source of all problems will be completely resolved."

N spoke calmly. This was his ideal—he wanted to create a world where Pokémon could truly be free and happy.

"You're wrong," Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"Humans and Pokémon cannot be separated. It's precisely because they have each other that such beautiful bonds can form."

"But these bonds aren't necessarily good for Pokémon," N countered without hesitation. While the word "bond" sounds beautiful, N didn't believe it was truly beneficial for Pokémon.

"Our views differ," Dylan gave up on discussing this topic with N.

Originally, he wanted to understand N's thoughts better and perhaps sway his current obsessive ideals.

After all, N was an outstanding person who genuinely loved Pokémon. However, it was clear that mere words wouldn't make N abandon his current beliefs.

N's ideas had taken root since his childhood, and the phenomena he spoke of did indeed exist.

"I will prove that I'm right."

N's eyes shone as he looked at Dylan. He acknowledged that there were indeed many humans who treated Pokémon as family, living happily together.

But from N's perspective, that wasn't the norm. Pokémon's happiness was their own, not something shared with humans.

"With all due respect, your ideas are completely unrealistic," Dylan responded bluntly.

To separate Pokémon and humans as distinctly as black and white—how could that ever be achieved? Based on what?

Just because of Zekrom (Reshiram)? What a joke. The Black and White Dragons were indeed powerful, but they weren't invincible.

Not to mention, in certain scenarios, even a skilled trainer like Lance from the manga wouldn't fear taking on the likes of these legendary dragons. Though the odds may not be in his favor, he'd still have a fighting chance.

If it were the Ice Dragon Kyurem, then perhaps there might be a chance to overturn the Unova League, but changing the world would still be impossible.

Who knew the full extent of the Pokémon League's hidden strength? The number of Champions across various regions was no small figure.

"Yes, this ideal is difficult, which is why I'm striving for it."

N's eyes gradually dimmed. The path to his ideals was destined to be filled with obstacles, but N was prepared. No matter what difficulties lay ahead, he would overcome them with his friends!

"If you continue to hold these beliefs, we may become enemies in the future," Dylan said directly, looking at N's vacant eyes.

"Enemies…?"

N's gaze grew slightly complex as he looked at the man before him, who was as special as he was. Once again, he fell into silence.

This was, after all, the first time he'd spoken his innermost thoughts to anyone other than his father.

"I am N, N Harmonia," N said after a long pause.

"Dylan, I'm the Gym Leader of Rota," Dylan extended his hand to N.

N hesitated for a moment but slowly reached out to shake Dylan's hand. He wasn't fond of physical contact with others.

"Your Pokémon are all strong, I can sense that they really like you," Dylan glanced at N's Zoroark and other Pokémon.

"Thank you, they are my dearest friends," N's lips subconsciously curled into a faint smile.

"I'd love to spar with you sometime, though I hope our next meeting won't be when we're forced to battle," Dylan said with a smile.

N: "…"

'Not when we're forced to battle?'

N's indifferent expression turned somewhat complicated. Watching Dylan's back as he walked back to the hotel, N couldn't help but purse his lips.

Yes, there was no guarantee of that, because he was the king of Team Plasma, and he had to fight for them, even if it wasn't his wish.

"Dylan…" N murmured.

"N, that person isn't ordinary. He carries a different kind of weight," a deep voice resonated telepathically in N's ears.

"Is that so? He's special like me," N responded calmly.

"But no one can stop our ideals, right? You'll help me, won't you, Zekrom?"

"Yes, N, I will do everything in my power for your ideals," the deep voice replied again.

"The White Dragon is stirring. I can feel it."

"The true White Dragon?" N fell silent. At Dragonspiral Tower, he had awakened Zekrom with his strong ideals.

But ideals and truth are opposites—this was a battle of destiny and beliefs.

Now that the Black Dragon, Zekrom, had awakened, it was only a matter of time before Reshiram also awakened, and the hero of truth would appear.

"Will it be you?" N suddenly thought of Dylan, the 'friend' with different beliefs.

Meanwhile, as Dylan entered the hotel, a 'Clefairy' poked its head out of his bag.

"Dylan, that person seemed strange," a clear and melodious telepathic voice sounded in Dylan's ear.

"What do you mean?"

"The dragon aura around him is way too strong, not something a human should have, and it feels a lot like that big Black Dragon."

Clefairy chewed on its paw, having been roused from its sleep by the intense dragon aura.

"Zekrom?"

"Yes, that big Black Dragon."

"It's not surprising," Dylan responded calmly, reflecting as he entered the elevator.

Indeed, N had awakened Zekrom. In that case, Reshiram should still be without a master.

He thought about trying to awaken the White Dragon himself. If it came to ideals, he didn't think he stood a chance, but as for truth… Dylan felt he might have a shot.

No problem—he would take a leave of absence soon to visit Dragonspiral Tower in Unova.

The Black Dragon was cool, but the White Dragon was beautiful!

(End of Chapter)

---

# 120: Chapter 120: Come And Bust Them

No problem, let's first see if we can awaken the White Dragon. If we succeed, then we can look into Kyurem.

If we follow the location in the game, Kyurem should be residing in the Giant Chasm. I wonder if this world's Kyurem can transform on its own?

"Mew, huh~"

"Mew?"

"Can you defeat Kyurem?"

Dylan pondered, because according to the Sacred Swordsman movie and the Hoopa movie, the Ice Dragon doesn't need to fuse with the Black Dragon or White Dragon to freely switch between White Flame form and Dark form.

The Ice Dragon that can switch between two forms at will is insanely powerful! It's beyond formidable!

"??" Mew widened its eyes.

"I don't like the Ice Dragon. It's too fierce, meow." Mew pouted, clearly having been scolded by Kyurem before.

"Emmm..."

Dylan fell silent. Judging by the movies, Kyurem's temperament was indeed quite aggressive.

Our adorable, non-flying Primal Groudon just accidentally breathed fire at it, and then the Ice Dragon 'accidentally' froze Primal Groudon into an ice sculpture. It was an instant payback. (From the Hoopa movie)

The same was true for the Sacred Swordsman movie; Kyurem's personality was indeed rather violent.

Forget it; thinking about this is pointless. We'll talk more about it when we go to the Unova region later.

A calm night passed, and at 8 a.m. the next day, Dylan woke up on time.

"About a month should be enough." Dylan thought.

The storyline of his growth simulation in the Alola region was already approaching Akala Island. A month should be sufficient to complete the first cycle, and by then, he could bring out Necrozma.

Once he obtained Necrozma, he would have a lot more confidence!

After waking Leah and Lillie, the three of them had breakfast and headed to the Silph Company.

Silph Company was quite prominent in Celadon City, as the tallest building in the city was the Silph headquarters.

"Young Master Dylan, Miss Leah, Miss Lillie, good morning."

Arriving at Silph Company, a female secretary in a professional outfit was waiting at the entrance. She greeted them respectfully as they exited the car.

"Hello."

"President Vernon is waiting for you in the boardroom."

"Let's go, then."

Dylan nodded, and the secretary gestured for the three to follow her.

Entering Silph Company, the secretary pressed the elevator button. Dylan looked around; the first-floor lobby was spacious and luxurious, though not surprisingly so.

Upon reaching the top floor of the Silph Building, the secretary led them to the boardroom, where an elderly man with a kind face, the current president of Silph, Vernon, was already waiting.

To Dylan's surprise, there was also a middle-aged man sitting there, dressed in a refined, expensive black suit with a neat, short haircut, a slender face, and an air of maturity and composure. It was Giovanni!

"Dylan, you're here."

Seeing Dylan's arrival, President Vernon's eyes lit up, and he stood up with a friendly smile to greet Dylan.

"Hello, President Vernon."

Dylan politely bowed to President Vernon.

"Hahaha~ No need to be so formal, just call me Grandpa." President Vernon was very enthusiastic.

"In the blink of an eye, over ten years have passed. I wonder if you still remember that I held you when you were a baby. How's your father, Itto, been lately?"

"My dad is doing great, enjoying life with my mom."

"Itto, huh~ He's really taking it easy by leaving all the work to others." President Vernon shook his head helplessly.

"I've also heard that you've surpassed your father in every way, Dylan. You're doing much better than he did at your age."

President Vernon patted Dylan's shoulder, full of praise. After all, Dylan was still young, yet he was already capable of capturing high-ranking members of Team Rocket. His future was promising.

"Come, sit down, Dylan. Let me introduce you to this gentleman here."

President Vernon led Dylan to a seat and then warmly introduced the man in the black suit.

"Actually, there's no need for President Vernon to trouble himself. Dylan and I have long admired each other."

The middle-aged man wore a faint smile as he extended his hand toward Dylan.

"Viridian Gym Leader Giovanni, Dylan, this is our first meeting, isn't it?"

No, it actually wasn't.

"Pleasure to meet you. I've always admired you, Senior Giovanni." Dylan shook Giovanni's hand and smiled in response.

"That's right. After all, Giovanni, like Dylan, is also a Gym Leader. Hahaha~ What a coincidence." President Vernon chuckled.

"Mr. Giovanni is not only an excellent Gym Leader but also a very successful entrepreneur. I've been in contact with him for a long time and have immense respect for his talents and vision."

President Vernon had a high opinion of Giovanni, clearly appreciating his abilities.

"You're too kind, President Vernon," Giovanni replied modestly.

Dylan raised an eyebrow but wasn't too surprised. Giovanni's Team Rocket had indeed expanded rapidly under his leadership, thanks in part to his many business ventures.

For example, the lucrative Game Corner in the original Celadon City—Giovanni's business acumen was certainly sharp.

"I think that if Mr. Giovanni were to join our Silph Company, we could reach even greater heights," Vernon remarked.

Dylan, however, remained silent, thinking, Are you seriously trying to recruit Giovanni? That's bold! This isn't just inviting a wolf into the house; it's inviting a man-eating tiger!

You really aren't worried about Silph collapsing fast enough, are you?

But Dylan wasn't too surprised, given that Giovanni maintained a very respectable persona—a Gym Leader of great repute, and a successful businessman. It wasn't unusual for a major company like Silph to be interested in his abilities.

"Please allow me some time to consider, President Vernon," Giovanni replied thoughtfully, still wearing a mature and steady expression.

"Of course. Please give it some serious thought, Mr. Giovanni. With your talents, you deserve a bigger stage! Even if your duties as a Gym Leader prevent you from fully dedicating yourself, we would understand." President Vernon was earnest.

"Thank you for your consideration, President," Giovanni replied calmly.

"By the way, Dylan, your father, Itto, has already told me about your request. Take a look at this."

President Vernon took a valuable-looking box from the side and placed it in front of Dylan, slowly opening it.

Inside was an exquisitely crafted purple Poké Ball, with a pair of magenta markings on each side of the upper half, and a large 'M' emblazoned on the button.

"This is Silph Company's crowning achievement—the Master Ball!"

Pride was evident in President Vernon's voice. After all, this was the pinnacle of Silph's technology and materials.

Leah and Lillie looked at the brand-new Master Ball with curiosity.

Even Giovanni's eyes glimmered slightly as he observed the Master Ball.

"This is the only finished product at present, Dylan. To be honest, due to the scarcity of the necessary materials for making Master Balls, we estimate that only eight of them can be produced in total."

"This is undoubtedly the most precious Poké Ball in the world, far more valuable than even a Luxury Ball," President Vernon said to Dylan earnestly. The Luxury Ball was already considered one of the most precious Poké Balls, and even Dylan only had about a dozen of them, having already used three.

That's right, they were used for capturing Articuno, Darkrai, and Regirock.

"And purely in terms of performance, the Master Ball far surpasses any current Poké Ball—there's no question about it!"

"I really like it."

Dylan chuckled softly, playing with the shrunken Master Ball in his hand. Ah, the Master Ball~

"These Master Balls are not intended for sale. As the name suggests, only true masters deserve to own one. And Dylan, your Master Ball is currently the only one in the world," President Vernon said with a smile.

Impressive~!

Lillie blinked her big eyes in awe. Silph Co. was a leader in global manufacturing, having pioneered the foundational technology for modern Poké Balls. In terms of Poké Ball production, they were the undisputed authority.

And according to President Vernon, the Master Ball, said to surpass all other Poké Balls on the market, currently existed only in her brother's hands!

Even if the remaining seven were produced in the future, Silph Co. had no plans to sell them to the public.

"I believe I'll be worthy of this Master Ball," Dylan's eyes flashed slightly.

What kind of Pokémon should he use this Master Ball for? Such a troubling question.

"Hahaha~ I think so too. Dylan, did you know? Our Silph Company is one of the world's strongest companies, but we've never had a famous Trainer emerge from within."

"Though Itto likes to delegate, he sure had a good son~!" President Vernon remarked.

"Haha~ Indeed, Gym Leader Dylan has a promising future," Giovanni agreed in his magnetic voice.

"Senior Giovanni is too kind. I still have a long way to go compared to you," Dylan said humbly, shaking his head.

"You're being modest, Gym Leader Dylan. As an older generation of Trainers, I can no longer compete with you rising stars," Giovanni responded, still wearing his gentle smile, his manner and poise flawless.

No, I still vividly remember your Beedrill, thought Dylan as his brow twitched slightly. Giovanni truly could lie without batting an eye—a seasoned actor.

After a while of pleasant conversation with President Vernon, Dylan finally left, escorted warmly by Vernon himself.

"Has the board meeting Itto was asked to convene passed the proposal to place restrictions on the company's financial accounts?"

Dylan glanced back at the Silph Co. building. Giovanni was about to take over Silph Co., but with proper preparations in place, Silph wouldn't suffer much.

After all, the largest shareholder of Silph Co., Itto, was located in another region. As long as the company's accounts were manipulated beforehand, it wouldn't matter if Team Rocket occupied the Silph Building.

Without Itto's approval, no large sums of money could be transferred out of the company accounts.

This strategy was admittedly a bit extreme, but with Itto holding overwhelming shares, the other shareholders had no choice but to agree. And the restriction would only last for a year.

Moreover, with so many variables in this world, there was no telling if Team Rocket would go as far as they did in the original story and attempt a hostile takeover of Silph Co.

"Dylan, where are we heading next?" Leah yawned as they left Silph Co.

"I'm taking you to see something interesting."

Dylan glanced at his phone. The people he had hired to track N had sent him another message—N and another person had already left the hotel.

About ten minutes later, Dylan arrived near an industrial park.

"Young Master, it's that factory. I saw them go inside with my own eyes."

"Good, you've done well. Take this, let the boys have a good lunch." Dylan handed a bag of cash to the man in front of him with a smile.

"You're too generous, Young Master! If you need us again, just call."

The man gave Dylan a thumbs-up, beaming as he took the cash.

Even in the world of Pokémon, while money couldn't get you everything, it certainly made things a lot easier. Otherwise, why would so many Pokémon hunters take the risk of defying the League's bans? It was all about money.

"I'll go gather some intel."

Leah's skin on her neck shifted, covering her cheeks, and in the blink of an eye, she had transformed into an ordinary-looking, slightly chubby woman. She spoke to Dylan.

This was her specialty.

"Be careful."

"No worries, leave it to me." Leah winked at Dylan before casually strolling toward the factory. After surveying the area, she disappeared into a corner.

"Brother, what's this all about?" Lillie asked, confused.

"We'll wait for Leah's intel. This afternoon might get pretty exciting. Have you ever seen a street fight, Lillie?" Dylan smiled at her as he scrolled through his phone.

"Emmm…" Lillie tilted her head, not fully understanding, but looking adorable in her confusion.

A few minutes later, a message from Leah popped up on Dylan's phone.

'Archer, Proton, and an unknown high-ranking executive. Team Rocket's boss will also be here.'

Wow~ As expected of Leah, her efficiency was top-notch!

Dylan chuckled softly, then immediately called his dear Senior Lance.

"Dylan? What's up?"

Proton's voice came through, still as cold and aloof as ever.

"Senior Lance~ There's an illegal gathering of an evil organization here! You'd better come and bust them!"

(End of chapter)

# 121: Chapter 121: Damn! Not this guy again!

"Evil organization gathering illegally?"

Lance, who was deep in the wilderness, frowned upon hearing this. What does this mean?

"Sir, I've received reliable intel. Team Rocket and Unova's Team Plasma, these two snake-in-the-grass organizations, are conducting a dirty deal!"

"As far as we know, three Rocket executives and the Rocket leader himself will show up, and from Team Plasma, their 'king' and a scientist will also be present."

"Sir, they need your righteous judgment now!"

Dylan spoke rapidly, leaving the girl behind him, Lillie, utterly confused.

Lance's usually calm face also changed swiftly. The cold, shadowy look in his eyes was terrifying.

Team Rocket's leader!!!

'Your dragon is nothing special.'

That person's words seemed to echo once again in Lance's mind. His face twisted uncontrollably, his mouth twitching up and down, as if trying to smile but failing, creating an eerie, unsettling look.

Are you finally going to show yourself again, you filthy rat!?

Being mocked by the despised leader of Team Rocket like that was something Lance could not endure! After all, it was only Team Rocket!

Team Rocket, who could only hide in the dark sewers, feeding on dirty water like filthy rats! A bunch of reptiles afraid of sunlight!

"Location."

Lance's voice had grown rougher.

"Saffron City."

"Half an hour, wait for me."

With that, the call abruptly ended. Dylan's lips curled into a slow grin. Well, well~ things are getting interesting now, aren't they?

Leah had already reemerged and sat in Dylan's car.

Dylan immediately reversed the car and drove to a higher vantage point overlooking the area ahead. It was an industrial park near the outskirts of the city, with plenty of factories around.

"Hello? Officer Jenny? I'm a Gym Trainer from the Rota Gym, and I'm here with Champion Lance for a secret operation. We need your assistance, but this is a classified mission, and we cannot disclose the details to others."

Dylan paused for a moment, but eventually called Saffron City's police department. The industrial park was likely going to turn into a battlefield soon, and they needed to evacuate the workers beforehand.

Though this might alert the enemy, it wasn't a big issue. They had enough time.

Meanwhile, in the underground factory, there was a small Team Rocket base.

"Please wait a moment; our boss will arrive shortly."

Archer smiled with his hands behind his back. Team Plasma sending their 'king' over was undoubtedly a sign of respect for Team Rocket.

Since it was a collaboration, Team Rocket had to show sincerity in return. N, the leader of Team Plasma, personally came to Kanto, so naturally, Team Rocket's boss would also make an appearance.

This wasn't someone Archer, one of Team Rocket's four commanders, could receive alone.

"No problem, we're not in a hurry."

N remained silent, while Colress, who was reading a book on genetics, casually replied.

The reason Colress accompanied N to Kanto was clear.

N was highly intelligent, but he wasn't particularly enthusiastic about working with Team Rocket. However, after much persuasion from Ghetsis, N reluctantly agreed.

Under these circumstances, expecting N to negotiate well with Team Rocket was impossible. Hence, Colress was sent by Ghetsis.

Moreover, since both sides were exchanging technological expertise, Colress was the ideal candidate for the discussion. Ghetsis specifically instructed N to follow Colress' lead during this mission.

Trusting Ghetsis completely, N agreed.

Archer smiled as he left the room, where Proton stood with his arms crossed, leaning against the wall.

"How's it going?"

"Not great. N doesn't seem too enthusiastic, and it feels like Colress is the one in charge here."

Archer shook his head. He had a keen sense of these things.

"I don't know why, but I've got a bad feeling about all this."

Archer furrowed his brows, feeling uneasy, as if something bad was about to happen, but he couldn't pinpoint why.

"Relax~ you're just paranoid lately. Is it because of that beatdown you got from Dylan and Red last time?"

Proton grinned, teasing his colleague.

"Don't worry, Archer, Dylan's not here this time. Just avoid him in the future."

Proton, although shaken by the incident at Celadon City's base, considered himself an optimist. If you lose, you lose; it's no big deal.

"Shut your mouth." Archer's face twitched as he glared at Proton. The events at the Celadon City base had become a mental scar for him!

Proton raised his hands innocently.

Not long after, a slightly overweight middle-aged man wearing a black bowler hat and a black trench coat walked over slowly, followed by a young woman with black hair wearing a white Team Rocket uniform.

"Boss."

Archer and Proton greeted the disguised Giovanni with respect, and Archer opened the door for him. Giovanni and the woman entered the room to discuss the collaboration.

Outside, Proton leaned back against the wall again while the black-haired woman closed her eyes, resting.

"So boring. By the way, Sabrina, the Three Legendary Birds project is about to start. Which one were you assigned?"

Sabrina didn't seem to notice his question, as if she hadn't heard him at all.

"Come on, don't be so cold, Sabrina. We're colleagues after all."

"Oh yeah, do you know Dylan? I heard all the Gym Leaders are in a group chat, right?"

"That guy is insane! Last time, he really wiped the floor with me. It felt like he was treating me like some Bug Catcher on the side of the road! No idea how he trains his Pokémon."

Sabrina, irritated by Proton's constant chatter, finally opened her eyes and gave him a sideways glance.

(You're just too weak.)

"What's with that look, Sabrina? It's not my fault! That guy's way too strong!" Proton, sharp as ever, picked up on the meaning behind Sabrina's look and helplessly defended himself.

(Yeah, right.)

Sabrina nodded and closed her eyes again, hoping Giovanni would finish the meeting soon.

"Hey, Sabrina, do you think I should ask Lambda to help me disguise myself and challenge Dylan's gym? I haven't had many missions lately."

Proton stroked his chin, seeming to seriously consider the idea.

(What are you talking about!?)

Sabrina instantly opened her eyes and stared at Proton with a baffled expression.

"Don't look at me like that! It's just that I don't get it. Archer's been acting weird since Dylan and Red wrecked him. I heard Rota Gym accepts double battles."

"So, I'm thinking, why not team up with Archer for a gym challenge? The Gym Leader's gotta go easy on challengers, right? If we beat Dylan just once, I think both Archer and I would feel a lot better. It could work!"

The more Proton spoke, the brighter his eyes became. He really thought he was onto something.

"Are you insane? I think you need to get your head checked." Sabrina finally snapped, coldly mocking him.

Seriously? A gym challenge? You're a high-ranking Rocket executive, not a legitimate Trainer. Have you lost your mind?

"Huh? I'm not crazy." Proton's handsome face was full of innocence.

"If you're not crazy, then go patrol outside." Sabrina said flatly.

"Come on, Sabrina, just because I can't beat you doesn't mean you can order me around! We're equals, you know." Proton complained.

"Besides, only a handful of people know about this base. We got here through a secret passage, so there's no way we're—"

Boom!

Proton hadn't finished speaking when the entire base suddenly shook violently. He almost lost his balance and fell, but a surge of blue light steadied him—it was Sabrina's psychic power.

Sabrina's beautiful eyebrows furrowed slightly as she looked upward.

"What's going on?" Proton's face was grim. He knew full well this wasn't an earthquake.

Moreover, the tremors were growing stronger, and bits of debris were already falling from the ceiling of the underground base.

"Someone is attacking from outside," Sabrina's eyes flickered with blue light.

...

Outside the factory.

Brilliant dragon meteors streaked across the sky, falling towards the factory.

It was Draco Meteor, a Dragon-type move!

And the ones using Draco Meteor were two Dragonair, with Lance standing tall on the back of an Aerodactyl behind them.

Lance's blood-red hair was whipped up by the fierce winds, his stern face cold and merciless, his sharp eyes gazing down at the rising flames below.

Boom! Boom, boom!

The continuous bombardment of Draco Meteor shook the earth, and soon the factory, once intact, was reduced to ruins.

"Is this okay?"

Not far away, Dylan, Lillie, and an Officer Jenny stood watching the scene unfold.

Lillie bit her lip, her eyes reflecting the flames shooting into the sky, her voice soft and uncertain.

After a quick briefing, the Kanto Champion had immediately launched an attack.

With the intensity of this Draco Meteor assault, the underground base would likely collapse. Could Team Rocket's people really survive?

"Champion Lance has always been like this," Officer Jenny of Saffron City said with resignation.

Although this was her first time witnessing Lance personally annihilate Team Rocket, his "violent methods" were something she'd heard of for a long time.

"Officer Jenny, has the area been fully surrounded?"

"Rest assured, we've secured the entire industrial park," Officer Jenny replied seriously.

"Good," Dylan nodded, his face lighting up with a smile as he looked back toward the ruins.

"Well, well, look what we have here, another old friend."

A Beedrill burst out from the rubble, and seeing the imposing Beedrill, Lance's lips twisted into a savage grin, showing his sharp white teeth—a chilling sight.

That's right! That filthy rat was here!

A pile of rubble was blown apart by a fierce jet of flames, and a Houndoom leaped out, followed by Giovanni and the others, but there was no sign of Sabrina.

Sabrina couldn't reveal herself just yet, so she remained hidden, waiting for the right moment to escape from another direction.

She could have used Teleport to leave, but with the interference devices Officer Jenny and her team had set up, it wasn't safe to use Teleport haphazardly.

"Boss, it's Lance, the Dragon Master," Archer's expression changed as he looked up at Lance, speaking to Giovanni in a low voice.

Giovanni narrowed his eyes slightly. How had they been exposed? No, now wasn't the time to think about that.

"It seems we have some unexpected guests. Mr. N, Mr. Colress, my apologies."

"This certainly isn't a good time, sir. We'll take our leave for now," Colress replied, pushing up his glasses and frowning. He had no intention of getting involved with the Kanto League.

"Of course. Archer and Proton will escort you," Giovanni said calmly.

"No need, we can leave on our own, sir. We'll contact you again once we've safely withdrawn," Colress declined. He wasn't foolish—going with Archer and Proton would only bring trouble when it was clear the Kanto League was here to wipe out Team Rocket.

"I'll hold off Lance for you," Giovanni nodded. He wasn't pleased with the situation either. They had been so close to securing a partnership with Team Plasma.

Yet here they were, thanks to Lance, a maddeningly persistent "hound," Giovanni thought darkly as he raised his head slightly to gaze at Lance in the sky.

"Much appreciated, sir," Colress replied bluntly before retreating with N.

"You two should leave as well," Giovanni ordered.

"Yes, boss," Archer and Proton turned and left without hesitation.

"Have you said all your last words?" Lance stood with his arms crossed, looking down at Giovanni, his blood-red hair billowing, his black cape flapping violently in the wind.

"You won't escape this time, filthy rat."

Lance's eyes were cold and filled with murderous intent.

"Heh heh~"

Giovanni chuckled softly, elegantly adjusting his collar with a calm and composed demeanor.

Meanwhile, Archer's face was as dark as ever. This meeting had been his arrangement—how had it been discovered?

"Something's wrong."

Proton suddenly stopped in his tracks, looking at the narrow alley ahead.

"Why isn't there a single League officer around here?"

Hearing Proton's words, Archer's expression changed as well. He was right—this wasn't normal. If Lance was here, there should be League officers as well.

Click-clack~ Click-clack~

Footsteps echoed from the alley ahead, and three figures slowly emerged, their forms entering Archer and Proton's sight. Their faces paled instantly.

"Ah~ Fate is such a marvelous thing, don't you agree?"

Dylan, with an impossibly warm smile, looked at Archer and Proton.

"My dear Mr. Archer and Mr. Proton~ Every encounter in this world is a reunion after a long parting."

"Don't you think? After all this time, I've missed you both so much~"

Ah, how touching! Dylan had been wondering how he'd get a Dragapult, and now here they were, delivered right to his doorstep.

Although it was a second chance, it wasn't his fault that Archer and Proton had escaped the last time. Capturing them again and asking the Kanto League for a Dragapult wasn't too much, right? Not too much at all!

Dylan's eyes grew increasingly gentle as he looked at Archer and Proton. He was truly moved. These two were like walking treasure chests!

But unlike Dylan, Archer and Proton's faces were growing increasingly grim.

Damn! Not this guy again!

(End of Chapter)

# 122: Chapter 122: Dragon of...

Archer and Proton were both about to crack. Why? Why did they encounter this person again?!

"Dylan, I think we can talk this out," Archer said, trying to remain calm. If it weren't for the sweat dripping down his forehead, he might've pulled it off.

"Oh my, oh my~ Archer, you're not joking, right?" Before Dylan could respond, Leah's sarcastic voice rang out.

Archer's eyes immediately darkened as he looked at the girl standing behind Dylan. It was that clown of a girl again!

"Ah~ Archer's gaze is so terrifying, like he's about to eat someone," Leah pretended to shrink back in fear, though her face bloomed with a radiant smile like a spring flower.

Archer's face twitched, while Proton watched with amusement, clearly enjoying the scene.

"Heh~ Dylan, even if you capture us, it's useless. Our boss can get us out anytime he wants," Archer said coldly, ignoring the noisy girl and directing his words at Dylan again.

"No way! Archer, you still expect your boss to save you? And you're proud of that? How useless are you?" Leah said, looking at Archer in disbelief.

"Pfft..." Archer's face turned even darker as he heard the stifled laughter—coming from Proton.

How could he laugh at a time like this?! Sure, it's embarrassing that he needed the boss to rescue him, but wasn't Proton in the same boat?

Lillie gave a strange look at the two senior Rocket executives. So, this is what evil organization's officers are like?

"Sorry, I just remembered something funny," Proton said awkwardly, turning his head away to avoid Archer's death glare.

"So, Dylan, if you let us go, I promise Team Rocket won't be your enemy," Archer said, swallowing his pride and trying to reason with Dylan.

They had no choice. Fighting was definitely not an option, so negotiation was all they had.

"Sorry, Archer. I know you're sincere," Dylan said, his tone still polite.

Two flashes of white light appeared, and Dragonite and Goodra materialized in front of Dylan. The sheer pressure from the two Dragon-type pseudo-legendaries made both Archer and Proton's scalps tingle.

This was even more ridiculous than the last time with Salazzle and Nidoking!

"But I am, after all, a Gym Leader of the League," Dylan added with a gentle smile.

"Dylan! Leave a way out for future encounters!" Archer's face turned pale as he looked at the imposing Dragonite and Goodra.

"Do you really need to be this harsh?"

"No worries. I'll visit you two in prison if I have time. But for now..." Dylan smiled, his eyes narrowing at the two.

"Let's go over your crimes, shall we?"

"Damn it! Dylan! Do you really think you have us cornered?" Archer gritted his teeth. Surrender was not an option; at the very least, they had to put up a fight!

"Let me show you the strength of the Rocket Executive!" Proton said, his expression serious. After all, as Rocket officers, they couldn't just roll over and give up. It was a matter of pride!

"Come on, Dylan!"

And then...

"Well done, Dylan. As expected of you," Officer Jenny said, arriving just in time to cuff Archer and Proton. She couldn't help but be impressed.

"I'll leave them to you, Officer Jenny. I have to check on something else," Dylan responded with a smile.

"Rest assured! We'll keep a close eye on these two top criminals!" Officer Jenny pounded her chest confidently.

Dylan nodded and, with Leah and Lillie, headed off in another direction.

"Um, Officer Jenny, these cuffs are a bit too tight. Could you loosen them a little?" Proton raised his hand to show the cuffs, which were digging into his wrists.

"Tight? Handcuffs are supposed to be tight! Now behave!" Officer Jenny said, hands on her hips, glaring at him.

"But the ones in Celadon City were perfectly fitted…" Proton grumbled.

"Why are they so tight in Saffron City? We criminals have rights, too!" he argued, trying to defend his case.

Archer, his face drained of all color, looked up at the sky in despair, completely numb to the conversation.

…

Meanwhile, in an alley, Colress and N were making their escape.

"Seriously, how could the security have been this lax? Even the Champion showed up!" Colress adjusted his glasses, glancing back at the distant explosions that indicated the fierce battle raging behind them.

"Rocket's leader is truly remarkable, though, to be able to fight Lance to a standstill."

Colress was amazed. Even from Unova, he had heard of Lance, the current Kanto Champion, considered the strongest Champion in Kanto's history!

N remained silent.

"But this is troublesome. I need to contact Ghetsis and figure out our next move," Colress muttered to himself. The collaboration with Team Rocket hadn't even officially started, and already they were in this mess. Unbelievable!

Still, Colress couldn't deny his fascination with Rocket's genetic research. Even with just a brief overview, he could see its potential value to his own studies.

Just then, the wind suddenly grew icy, and the temperature plummeted.

Colress looked up sharply, and what met his eyes was a blizzard, filling his entire view.

An Ice-type Blizzard!

"Zoroark," N's hollow eyes flickered with focus.

In a flash, the dark shadow of Zoroark leaped in front of N, its claws gathering black energy.

With a piercing screech, a Dark Pulse shot out.

The Dark-type attack collided head-on with the Blizzard, black and white energies clashing furiously, before finally exploding and sending shockwaves rippling in all directions.

"Such a powerful Blizzard!"

Colress raised his arm to shield himself, squinting through his glasses. Zoroark was one of N's strongest Pokémon, a Champion-level fighter!

But Zoroark's Dark Pulse couldn't overpower that Blizzard!

The only known Ice-type trainers in Kanto... could it be? No, she's supposed to be an Elite Four member, not a Champion!

A path of ice extended toward them, and a massive Lapras swam forward slowly. Riding on its back was a striking woman with long red hair, dressed in black.

It really was her, the first of Kanto's Elite Four, Lorelei of the Ice-type!

"This is troublesome," Colress muttered, his brows furrowing. It wasn't just Lance they had to worry about.

"Looks like I've caught you little mice," Lorelei said, stepping off Lapras and casting a cold smile as her gaze swept over N and Colress.

She'd been in Saffron City, and when Lance contacted her, she happened to catch two big fish.

"Elite Four Lorelei, we're not with Team Rocket," Colress said, adjusting his glasses with a narrowed gaze.

"Attacking us without warning like this is uncalled for, don't you think?"

"Heh…"

Lorelei let out two cold laughs, her icy gaze unchanged.

"Team Rocket is nothing but filthy rats, and you of Team Plasma are the same — maggots in the sewer. There's no difference."

Lorelei had specifically researched Unova's Team Plasma, and upon learning that their ideology was to "liberate Pokémon from humans," she immediately saw through their sinister intentions.

Their slogans were loud and clear, but in the end, they simply wanted others to give up their Pokémon so they could use them to fulfill their own ambitions and desires.

Just another trash organization that needed to be eradicated.

"Do you think perhaps Elite Four Lorelei has some sort of misunderstanding about us?"

A bit exasperated, Colress felt he was being unjustly dragged into this. He had joined Team Plasma mainly because Ghetsis supported his research, but now he was suddenly being labeled a "maggot in the sewer"?

Colress had always considered himself a genius, especially in scientific research — it was his pride.

But this time, Lorelei didn't respond to him. Instead, she released another Pokémon, Dewgong.

"Dewgong, Lapras, Blizzard," Lorelei said calmly. Her gaze toward N and Colress was cold and indifferent, as if she were looking at two corpses.

Dewgong and Lapras opened their mouths simultaneously, ice-blue energy condensing before a fierce blizzard erupted from them.

An Ice-type Blizzard!

In Colress' perspective, the immense Blizzard seemed to cover the sky. If they were hit directly by this, how could they possibly survive?

This crazy woman! How could she attack humans like this? Even if they were members of an evil organization, this was too much!

Colress was stunned!

As a well-known "dark scientist" in the industry, he had long been on the blacklist of the Pokémon League, but the Unova Elite Four weren't this insane!

"Protect."

N's lifeless eyes flickered slightly, his voice calm.

Zoroark stepped forward, positioning itself in front of the two. A green Protect barrier appeared, capable of blocking all standard attacks.

Of course, if the difference in power was too great, Protect might not be able to fully defend, as demonstrated in the anime.

The howling Blizzard devoured everything in the alley, and after a while, the storm gradually subsided.

The scene had completely transformed, with the road and walls covered in thick snow, and even the air filled with scattered snowflakes.

Crack~

It sounded like glass shattering as the green Protect barrier cracked and dissipated into light particles, revealing Zoroark, Colress, and N.

"It seems you really are quite skilled, little rats."

Lorelei's icy gaze didn't change much. Based on the intelligence she had gathered, the green-haired young man in front of her was the leader of Team Plasma.

"N, we can't afford to delay here. Her strength is abnormal."

Colress glanced at their surroundings. This Lorelei was definitely a Champion-level trainer!

And yet Lorelei was only the first of Kanto's Elite Four. No wonder the rumors said Kanto had the strongest Elite Four in the world.

If Lorelei was at Champion-level, then the others above her — Bruno and Agatha — must be at Champion-level too.

"I understand," N nodded silently, comprehending Colress' point.

"We'll leave now, and no one will get hurt."

N took a step forward, signaling Zoroark to stand down, and spoke calmly to Lorelei. He was not someone who preferred violence.

The reason he had awakened Zekrom was to draw upon Unova's founding legend, but the road to ideals was never easy.

"Oh?" Upon hearing this, a cold smile once again appeared on Lorelei's icy face.

"You're nothing but the leader of an evil organization. Scum like you should all be turned into ice sculptures."

Lorelei's voice grew colder. She despised evil organizations, loathed humans with darkness in their hearts, and hated all those who harmed Pokémon and nature!

"We are not an evil organization."

N pursed his lips, slowly raising his right hand.

As N made this motion, a massive black cloud suddenly drifted over the once-clear sky, blue arcs of lightning crackling within it.

A heavy pressure spread across the sky and earth, difficult to describe.

Lorelei sensed something was wrong and looked up at the sky. This massive black cloud had appeared without any warning. Something was off!

"My ideal is for Pokémon to achieve everlasting freedom and happiness."

N continued to gaze at Lorelei, unable to agree with her perception of him. His ideals had always been for the sake of Pokémon.

Roar~!

A deafening dragon roar echoed through the sky, and a blue bolt of lightning descended. A massive black dragon god soared down from the heavens, landing heavily behind the green-haired youth, exuding an awe-inspiring Dragon-type aura!

Lorelei's eyes widened in shock as she stared at the pitch-black dragon. This overwhelming presence — it was a Legendary Pokémon!

"I am N! N. Harmonia!"

N slowly lowered his arm, his usually lifeless eyes now shining with dazzling brilliance.

"This is my best friend and partner, the one who shares my ideals and supports me in achieving them..."

"Zekrom!"

The Dragon of Ideals from Unova's founding legend!

Lorelei's expression became incredibly dark at this moment. A guy willing to cooperate with trash like Team Rocket had been recognized by the Dragon of Ideals? How could that be?!!

# 123: Chapter 123: I you!

At this moment, Lorelei's expression was truly grim, and understandably so. Facing the legendary Dragon of Ideals, Zekrom, would unsettle anyone.

Well, anyone except for a certain old monster.

Zekrom was not just an ordinary Legendary Pokémon like Regirock. It was a Legendary Pokémon comparable to the likes of Ho-Oh and Lugia from Kanto.

"Oh my goodness! Is that the Pokémon of the king of Team Plasma?"

In the distance, Dylan and the two others were watching from afar, with both Leah and Lillie visibly shocked.

"That's right, this is Zekrom, one of the 'Legendary Dragon Pokémon' of the Unova region," Dylan said, his eyes glinting slightly.

The Tao Trio is the common term for Unova's Zekrom, Reshiram, and Kyurem. They all belong to the group known as the "Legendary Dragon Pokémon."

"Zekrom is one of the Dragon gods from Unova's founding legend. It's said to have emerged from the split of the original Unova Dragon and chooses individuals who harbor ideals, assisting them in realizing their dreams."

"In terms of power and status, it's on par with Ho-Oh and Lugia from Kanto, as well as Lunala and Solgaleo from Alola."

Dylan provided a brief introduction.

"Amazing..." Lillie bit her lip, clutching her bag instinctively. So, does that mean Nebby would also evolve into a being as grand and powerful as this?

She could hardly imagine it. Nebby, so innocent, playful, and a bit mischievous, would one day evolve into such a majestic and powerful Legendary Pokémon.

It wasn't an exaggeration on Lillie's part; just looking at Zekrom made it difficult for her to breathe. It was due to an inexplicable sense of anxiety.

"Why did Zekrom choose the leader of Team Plasma as its hero?" Leah frowned. She couldn't understand!

A Legendary Pokémon like this actually chose the leader of an evil organization; wasn't it just asking for trouble?

'Zekrom, Dragon/Electric type, the Deep Black Pokémon

Ability: Teravolt Signature Moves: Bolt Strike, Outrage, Thunder, Fusion Bolt, Hyper Voice, Dragon Claw, Roar, Zen Headbutt, Slash…

A legendary Pokémon said to scorch the world with its lightning. It assists those who seek to create an ideal world.

According to myth, if humans lose their sense of justice, it will destroy their kingdom with its fierce lightning.'

This was information from Dylan's Rotom Pokédex. He had included extensive data on Legendary Pokémon, otherwise, he wouldn't have had access to Zekrom's details.

What an exaggerated description!

Lillie and Leah were stunned as they listened to the Rotom Pokédex's introduction of Zekrom—it was just so incredible and dramatic!

"Can Lorelei possibly defeat Zekrom?" Lillie asked worriedly.

"No way. Lorelei's strength hasn't reached that level yet."

Dylan shook his head, directly denying it. Although Lorelei was also Champion-level, her strength was still far behind Trainers like Lance, Giovanni, and Leon.

Even though her specialty was the Ice type, which was effective against Zekrom, the difference in power was too great. Even if it were a quadruple weakness, Lorelei would still stand no chance.

Reality isn't a turn-based game where a move hits automatically if the accuracy is sufficient.

In real life, Pokémon aren't stationary targets, and it's not a turn-based system. Even if Lorelei's Pokémon launched Ice-type moves, Zekrom could counter with its own attacks due to the power gap—it simply wouldn't be a fair fight.

"Ice Beam!"

Lorelei steadied her emotions and ordered Lapras and Dewgong without hesitation.

The pressure exerted by Zekrom was immense, underscoring its strength, but Lorelei wouldn't back down, even when faced with the Dragon God!

An evil organization was something she could never tolerate.

Lapras and Dewgong opened their mouths simultaneously, with icy light gathering within.

In the next moment, two winding beams of ice shot rapidly toward Zekrom.

"Thunder," N's eyes had already calmed.

The turbine on Zekrom's tail began to glow, and the next moment, a massive golden lightning bolt shot forth.

An Electric-type Thunder attack!

The Thunder collided with the two Ice Beams in mid-air, but the stalemate lasted only a second before the golden lightning overpowered the Ice Beams and continued toward Lorelei.

Lorelei's pupils contracted, and she quickly tried to release Cloyster from her Poké Ball to use Protect, but the Thunder was too fast!

Boom!

The Thunder struck the ground in front of Lapras and Dewgong, sending out a deafening roar, with golden sparks scattering everywhere.

The burst of energy from Thunder still managed to impact Lapras and Dewgong, making the two Pokémon cry out in pain, as Electric beats Water!

However, Lapras protected Lorelei by positioning itself between her and the stray electricity.

As the storm subsided, Lapras and Dewgong were visibly injured, with multiple wounds. Lorelei's expression turned grim.

Although she had expected the legendary Zekrom to be incredibly powerful, she hadn't imagined it would be this overwhelming.

With just one move, the disparity in strength had already become apparent.

"I have no intention of harming you, nor do I wish to hurt your Pokémon," N spoke calmly, his voice tranquil.

"Zekrom held back just now; I hope you understand that."

Had Zekrom's Thunder been aimed directly at Lorelei, the situation would not be what it was now.

Lorelei's fingers trembled slightly, but not from fear—it was from anger!

The fact that the leader of an evil organization showed mercy towards her in battle—wasn't that an insult?

"Don't joke around!" Lorelei's eyes were cold as ice.

"Zekrom, Thunder." N made no further attempts to persuade.

Zekrom's body flashed with lightning once again, and a massive thunderbolt descended from the sky, aimed directly at Lapras and Dewgong.

The lightning illuminated the darkened sky, clouded by black clouds.

"Protect!" Lorelei immediately retreated, commanding Dewgong and Lapras to use Protect.

However, at that moment, a magnificent Articuno flew swiftly over and stopped above Lapras and Dewgong, with a green Protect barrier appearing.

Boom!

The Thunder struck the Protect barrier hard, golden sparks crackling and spreading. Ripples spread across the surface of the Protect barrier.

With a loud crash, the Protect barrier shattered, and Articuno was pushed back by the strong impact. But it managed to just barely withstand the Thunder.

"Hmm?"

Lorelei frowned. Was this Articuno? She looked behind her, and a man and woman entered her view.

"It's you."

N also saw the approaching pair, and a flicker of emotion appeared in his eyes.

"Ah~ N, I really didn't expect to see you again so soon," Dylan looked at N with a hint of regret.

"Indeed, I didn't want to meet you again in this way," N replied, his expression somewhat complicated.

Colress glanced at Dylan, then at N, showing great interest in their relationship.

"You're Dylan?" Lorelei looked at Dylan, who had walked over, adjusting her glasses.

"Yes, it seems that Senior Lance has mentioned me to you, Senior Lorelei," Dylan smiled at Lorelei.

I have to say, up close, Lorelei looked very beautiful, and her figure was excellent; however, the aloof aura she exuded was quite strong.

Lillie, standing beside Dylan, was also sneaking glances at Lorelei and then lowered her head to look at her own feet, feeling a bit inadequate.

"Not just Lance; the chairman has mentioned you several times as well," Lorelei said indifferently.

"Is that Articuno yours?" Lorelei glanced at the Articuno in the sky.

"Yes."

"No wonder both the chairman and Lance have such high expectations of you," Lorelei praised, sensing the Articuno's strong power.

"But this guy's Zekrom is too strong; even if we work together, I'm afraid we wouldn't stand a chance."

Lorelei's gaze shifted to N and Colress, and her expression instantly turned cold, as if the temperature had dropped.

"What about Lance?"

"Senior Lance is battling against the Team Rocket leader and can't come over right now."

"I have a secret technique, but I'm not sure if it would be effective against them, and Zekrom…"

Lorelei's aura grew colder, her unwillingness evident.

Lillie shrank back behind Dylan, wrapping her arms around herself, feeling goosebumps all over.

"Lorelei is right, Dylan; even if you have Articuno, it won't help," N spoke calmly.

"I have no intention of fighting you. As long as you step aside, I won't harm you."

N repeated his stance; he didn't like fighting. Although he wanted to fulfill his ideals, his original intention was to achieve them peacefully as much as possible.

Upon hearing this, Lorelei pressed her lips tightly together, her brows furrowing as she coldly stared at N.

"Truly arrogant, N. Do you really think Zekrom is invincible?" Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"No," N shook his head.

"But you can't stop Zekrom. Or do you mean, Dylan…"

N wasn't arrogant; the world was vast, and Zekrom wasn't the strongest Pokémon. However, if it were just Articuno, it was definitely no match for Zekrom.

Or…

N's gaze flickered as he looked at Dylan. Did Dylan have other trump cards? Zekrom had mentioned that Dylan bore a 'weight' that even it cared about.

"But as an elite trainer, even if I can't win, I won't let you leave easily, N."

"I see."

N fell silent; there was nothing more to say.

"Zekrom, use Thunder."

Zekrom let out a deep roar, and golden lightning shot through the air.

Lorelei's expression became serious, just as she was about to instruct her Dewgong and Lapras to use their ultimate moves against Zekrom when she noticed Dylan rummaging through his backpack.

Lorelei was stunned because Dylan actually pulled out a chubby Clefairy from his backpack?

"Ok!"

Dylan held his breath and then forcefully threw the fat Clefairy toward the lightning shot by Zekrom.

The black dragon was completely unfazed!

"It's decided, you're the one! Clefairy!"

Lorelei: "???"

Lillie: (—)

Zekrom: ???

Boom!

"Me!!!"

The lightning directly hit Clefairy, causing an explosion and a terrified screech.

Thud…

A charred, black object fell from the sky, landing on the ground and kicking up a bit of dust.

The scene fell silent.

N and Colress looked at Dylan with strange expressions, and Lorelei felt a mix of admiration and confusion.

Zekrom: "…"

From the pile of blackened debris, a plump, pink paw shakily emerged, pushing against the ground, as Clefairy struggled to stand and looked toward Zekrom.

Its chubby body heaved up and down as it gasped for breath, and its originally clear eyes gradually filled with a deep and terrifying psychic power.

"Lorelei, we should retreat quickly; the little guy seems to be angry."

Dylan pulled Lillie behind him and shouted to Lorelei, quickly backing away.

They needed to run further back to avoid being caught in the fallout.

"???" Lorelei looked puzzled.

"Me!!! (How dare you zap me, black dragon! I you!)"

With an angry screech, terrifying pink psychic energy surged into the sky.

Yeah, it's about to go wild.

(End of this chapter)

# 124: Chapter 124: Your waistline is about to match Nidoking’s!

A vast and surging psychic force materialized and shot into the sky, piercing through the dark clouds, leaving a giant gaping hole, with fierce winds bursting forth.

The shocking pink 'light pillar' dyed Lillie's pupils pink, horrifying and terrifying!

Without a doubt, Dylan and the others ran even faster. Articuno flew in the sky, while Lapras and Dewgong swam behind the three of them.

"Dylan, what is that thing?"

Following behind Dylan and Lillie, Lorelei was stunned.

Could a Clefairy actually do something like this? Lorelei wouldn't believe it even if her life depended on it! And besides, that sound didn't even come from a Clefairy!

"A little freeloader who eats for free every day. It's finally time for it to pay some rent today."

Having reached a safe distance, Dylan let out a sigh of relief and replied to Lorelei.

"???" Lorelei was even more confused.

"So, brother, what exactly is Clefairy?" Lillie asked curiously.

From the time of the Regirock incident, Lillie was certain that this was no ordinary Clefairy. Whose Clefairy was this powerful?

But Lillie had no clue what Clefairy really was. What made it even stranger was that even the Pokédex identified it as a normal Clefairy.

"The little guy doesn't want to reveal its true form. Maybe you could try offering it some tuna cans, and maybe it'll respond to you," Dylan said with a smile. Since Clefairy didn't want to show its true identity, he didn't elaborate further.

"..." Lorelei thoughtfully watched as the once bright pink psychic energy gradually faded. A powerful psychic Pokémon in Kanto that could disguise itself as a Clefairy... there was probably only one Pokémon that fit that description.

Meanwhile, on N's side...

"What is this thing?"

Colress was shocked as he stared at the Clefairy floating in mid-air. It was releasing such an overwhelming and terrifying psychic energy, something no ordinary Pokémon could ever do.

"Zekrom?" N furrowed his brows.

"It's Mew."

Zekrom's deep telepathic voice resonated in N's mind.

Mew? The mythical Pokémon from Kanto? N immediately recalled the legend.

"It's angry."

Zekrom's dragon eyes showed a hint of helplessness. In fact, when Dylan first threw out Clefairy, it had sensed something was off, but by the time it confirmed it, lightning had already struck Clefairy.

And now, without a doubt, the normally sweetly-sleeping Clefairy had been given a good electric shock, waking up with some serious 'wake-up anger.'

"Mii!!".

Clefairy's normally pleasant, crisp cry sounded sharp and heavy under the intense psychic pressure.

The materialized psychic energy burst out, transforming into a shockwave that rushed toward Zekrom.

It was a Psychic-type Psychic!

"Zekrom, Thunderbolt," N commanded, eyes sharp.

Zekrom's massive, heavy black dragon body took to the sky, with the turbines on its back spinning wildly, generating blue electrical currents.

The Electric-type Thunderbolt!

A massive bolt of lightning surged forth, clashing fiercely against the incoming psychic shockwave.

Thunderbolt and Psychic collided violently, but soon the psychic energy began to suppress the thunderbolt.

"Zekrom!" N noticed this immediately and called out to his dragon.

Roar!!

Zekrom, the dragon of ideals, let out a deafening roar as the electric power surged dramatically.

Boom!

Thunderbolt and Psychic exploded, sending shockwaves of intense energy rippling out, pushing both Zekrom and Clefairy back slightly.

The black clouds in the sky had been completely dispersed by the aftermath of their attacks, and the ground below also began to crack from the wind pressure, though the battle itself remained in the sky.

A green Protect barrier faded away, revealing Zoroark and the figures of N and Colress behind it.

"So strong."

N frowned deeply. Was Mew really this powerful?

"N, we can't stay here any longer. It's too dangerous,"

Colress said, releasing Metagross from its Poké Ball.

No wonder those guys ran off at the beginning; they were probably worried about getting caught in the aftermath of this legendary battle.

"Zekrom, tell Mew that we didn't mean to attack it. This fight can be avoided."

N climbed onto Colress's Metagross and sent a telepathic message to Zekrom.

"As expected, Mew isn't afraid of a legend like Zekrom."

From a distance, Dylan was watching the battle between the two legendary Pokémon with great interest. Though they had only exchanged one move, the visual effects were truly spectacular!

It also proved how strong this Mew really was. At least, it was definitely not weaker than a legend like Zekrom. That made sense, considering this Mew could even transform into Ho-oh in the movies.

"So strong~!" Lillie gasped in awe. Even though it was just Psychic versus Thunderbolt, when these two Pokémon used those moves, it was a completely different level!

Meanwhile, in the sky...

Clefairy was still enveloped in pink psychic energy, with deep blue light glowing in its eyes, its aura intensifying, still clearly in its 'wake-up anger' mode.

How many years had it been? It couldn't even remember the last time it had been struck by lightning!

"Mew, I didn't mean to," Zekrom tried to reconcile, its deep telepathy reaching out to Clefairy.

"Then that means you did it on purpose, mii!" But obviously, Clefairy wasn't going to be easily appeased.

"???" Zekrom's dragon eyes widened slightly. What? I said I didn't do it on purpose, and you're saying I did? How is that logical?

When did you become like this?

Zekrom sensed something was off. It remembered that Mew wasn't this unreasonable before.

"No response? Alright! So it was on purpose, mii!"

Clefairy got even angrier, convinced now that Zekrom had done it intentionally.

"No, wait! Mew!" Zekrom noticed the intense white light enveloping Clefairy's body and realized something was very wrong.

A vast dragon aura mixed with an icy chill began to spread, and the light grew brighter and brighter. As the transformation light faded, an imposing gray-blue dragon-like Pokémon appeared.

Zekrom's dragon eyes narrowed. It knew this dragon all too well!

Its head was adorned with icy crystals, its terrifying golden eyes gleamed, its broken ice wings extended, and its massive gray dragon body loomed ominously.

This was Kyurem, the ice dragon left behind after the Unova dragon split into two, and undoubtedly the strongest of the three legendary dragons of Unova!

"Eh? What Pokémon is that?" Lillie asked, puzzled.

"It's Kyurem, the legendary ice dragon from Unova. I

wonder if it can transform into its other forms."

Dylan raised an eyebrow, amused. Transforming into Kyurem to fight Zekrom, Mew was really showing its playful side today.

The terrifyingly heavy pressure from Kyurem filled the air, making the atmosphere feel thick and oppressive, much more so than Zekrom's presence.

Overwhelming pressure!

"Mii~!"

Kyurem raised its head and roared, a surge of cold air rippling out in a shockwave, accompanied by a tangible soundwave.

But that roar...

Zekrom, who had been intensely focused, almost lost its composure. Did Kyurem just say "mii?

For some reason, despite Kyurem's fierce and terrifying appearance, hearing it say "mii" gave Zekrom a weird sense of... cuteness?

No, no, that's all wrong! Zekrom had no affection for Kyurem at all. In fact, its feelings toward Kyurem were rather complicated.

As the empty shell left behind after the Unova dragon's split, Kyurem not only inherited the dragon's most powerful abilities but also had the potential to absorb both Zekrom and Reshiram to forcibly fuse with them. Because of this, both black and white dragons usually kept their distance from Kyurem.

Fortunately, Kyurem was rather reclusive and rarely ventured out, so it hadn't actively pursued Zekrom or Reshiram.

No, no, now wasn't the time to be thinking about this.

"Mii~!"

A dreamy ice-blue energy condensed on Kyurem's body, and the freezing breath spread wildly.

"N! We have to retreat now!" Zekrom's dragon eyes were locked on Kyurem opposite, communicating with N through telepathy.

It didn't want to fight to the bitter end with Clefairy,

especially since Clefairy had transformed into Kyurem and was now even using Kyurem's moves.

Without hesitation, Zekrom turned and flew away from Kyurem at high speed, lowering its altitude mid-flight to pick up Metagross, where N and Colress were standing.

"Miiiii!"

With another sharp screech from Kyurem, its tangible freezing breath turned into a shockwave, rapidly spreading towards Zekrom in an arc.

Where the Glaciate technique passed, a trail of ice quickly froze into place—the moisture in the air solidifying!

Kyurem's signature move: the Ice-type Glaciate!

The freezing wave shot towards Zekrom like lightning. Realizing the attack, Zekrom immediately accelerated, but the brief delay from picking up N and Colress made it too late to dodge!

Crack! Crack!

The Glaciate hit Zekrom's back. It groaned, pain flickering in its dragon eyes as ice crystals and frost rapidly formed over its back, turning into a thick block of ice in an instant.

"Zekrom!" N shouted, noticing something was wrong and calling out in concern.

"I'm fine, N. Don't worry."

Zekrom gritted its teeth, enduring the pain in its back. It could still withstand a single Glaciate.

Consider it payback for that Thunderbolt it hit Clefairy with earlier. Otherwise, it feared Clefairy would never give up and chase after them again.

Zekrom accelerated once more and soon vanished into the sky. Watching Zekrom flee, Kyurem's body shone with light again, shrinking back into Clefairy, who pouted before turning into a pink beam of light, shooting down towards the ground.

Then...

"Clefaaa! Clef-clef-clefaaa!!"

Clefairy launched itself into Dylan's arms with a headbutt, endlessly squeaking "Clef clef clef," as if complaining about Dylan's cruelty.

Of course, Clefairy knew why Zekrom's Thunderbolt had suddenly hit it. Who wouldn't get it? It had been peacefully sleeping and then got struck by lightning!

How could they do this? If it weren't for its thick body, the Thunderbolt would've really hurt!

"So cute!" Lillie's eyes sparkled.

Lorelei, meanwhile, gave Clefairy, who was now acting all cutesy, a strange look. Her thoughts drifted back to the terrifyingly imposing Kyurem from moments ago, leaving her deep in thought.

If she hadn't witnessed it herself, she would never have believed that these two were the same creature.

"Alright, alright, I'm really sorry, sorry, sorry," Dylan apologized sincerely, looking at the little cutie with puffed-up cheeks.

"But our Clefairy really is amazing, managing to scare even the legendary Zekrom away."

Dylan smiled and praised it.

"Clef!"

Clefairy proudly raised its head, placing its tiny paws on its hips. That's right, I'm awesome! That black dragon?

Nothing special.

"Clef~"

Clefairy then looked up at its trainer with big, watery eyes, its voice soft and cute.

"No way. Have you seen your waistline? You're almost as big as a Nidoking! Didn't I just give you a ton of canned food? You finished it already?" Dylan was stunned for a moment before quickly refusing.

This greedy cat was trying to ask for more cans again!

"Clef! Clef clef!!"

Clefairy used its move, Argument, claiming those cans were rightfully its. So what if it ate them quickly?

"Are you a pig? It's only been a few days! That was thirty whole crates!" Dylan was astonished.

"Clef!!!"

Clefairy used Stubborn Persistence, reminding Dylan it had shielded him from the lightning!

Dylan fell silent.

(End of chapter)
