# 137: Chapter 138: I’m truly impressed

Of course, all these thoughts only flashed in his mind for a moment, and Brandon's emotions gradually calmed down. He was not someone who couldn't handle a loss.

Or rather, as someone who genuinely cared for his juniors, Brandon actually hoped to see those excellent and powerful junior trainers thrive.

This was also the reason he accepted Carsey's invitation to become one of the leaders of the Battle Frontier.

But... it wasn't supposed to be like this.

"Dylan, is this really your newly formed team?"

Brandon frowned and asked Dylan. He didn't mean to doubt him; it was just too fantastical, wasn't it? Brandon considered himself one of the top trainers in the world, but Dylan using a newly formed team to pressure him was truly unbelievable.

"Yes, Senior Brandon, what do you think?" Dylan smiled and nodded.

Brandon fell silent again. He could tell that Dylan didn't seem to be lying; he was confident in his own judgment.

"A very powerful dragon."

Brandon sighed and spoke. He suddenly began to doubt whether he was really getting old. But he was still under forty, in his prime!

"I think so too; dragon-type Pokémon are so powerful and beautiful." Dylan's smile was warm.

"The charm of dragon Pokémon is so simple; their strength can crush any enemy, and I'm deeply fascinated by it."

Dylan actually loved dragons because there were too many dragon-type Pokémon he liked.

Well, including the Dragon Plate's alpaca.

"Oh? Dylan, although I admit your dragon is strong, you still haven't defeated me."

Brandon's expression was serious; his emotions had settled, and he had regained his composure.

"Your dragon is indeed strong, but how does it fare against legendary Pokémon?"

Brandon took out a Poké Ball, his gaze focused on Dylan as he spoke in a low voice.

The new generation pushing forward the old, Brandon did not deny this. He also believed that Dylan would definitely surpass him, an old-school trainer, in the future.

But right now? Dylan had yet to defeat him! He still had his legendary Pokémon!

"Please, Senior."

Dylan smiled and gestured for Brandon to continue.

"Dylan, let me show you the legendary Pokémon that I spent a lot of effort and energy to capture! Let your dragon experience their power!"

Brandon's aura had already changed; this was his personal pride! He had captured a legendary Pokémon!

"Registeel!"

Brandon threw the Poké Ball forcefully, and with a flash of white light, a gray metal Pokémon landed heavily in front of him.

Its spherical upper body was gray, with a hexagonal pattern formed by seven red dots on its face, exuding a heavy aura.

"Regi~"

A deep, metallic hum echoed. This was one of the 'legendary giants,' Registeel!

"The legendary Registeel~"

Lillie's bright eyes sparkled slightly. She had seen Regirock before, after all; she witnessed her brother capturing Regirock in person.

Moreover, according to what her brother said at the time, the so-called 'Tree of Beginning' should also have Registeel and Regice nearby.

However, Registeel and Regice did not appear back then, which was quite regrettable. Otherwise, her brother could have captured them all.

But now she saw them from Senior Brandon.

Ding~ Registeel, Steel-type, Iron Pokémon

Ability: Clear Body

Signature Moves: Flash Cannon, Lock-On, Superpower, Heavy Slam, Hammer Arm, Iron Defense, Iron Head, Tackle, Heavy Slam...

Description: It possesses a body harder than any metal, and its interior seems hollow. It's unclear what this Pokémon feeds on.

A very powerful Registeel! Just by feeling, it was much stronger than the wild Regirock her brother had captured.

Lillie looked at the information in the Pokédex, blinking her large eyes.

However, after her brother's training, Regirock shouldn't be any worse than this Registeel now.

"Oh~ so this is Registeel~"

Carsey pulled down his sunglasses, carefully observing Registeel in amazement; legendary Pokémon were indeed different!

This was the first time he had seen Brandon's Registeel. Although he knew that Brandon had found and captured Registeel not long ago, Brandon had always been busy traveling, and Carsey hadn't seen him for a long time until today.

"The dragon type has a disadvantage against steel types; it's not surprising that Mr. Brandon would choose Registeel for the battle."

Anabel nodded thoughtfully. A pure Steel-type like Registeel was resistant to dragon moves, although it didn't have an advantage over them.

"However, aside from their renowned strength and physique, the rich move sets of dragon-type Pokémon are also one of the reasons for their power."

Anabel pondered and added. Although dragon-type moves were not very effective against Registeel, Dragapult was not solely reliant on dragon-type moves.

Generally, dragon-type Pokémon could learn a wide variety of moves, making them renowned for their diverse move sets. This also led to dragons having excellent coverage, as each dragon Pokémon had an exceptional move pool.

For example, the most famous Dragonite! You could never guess what moves the opposing Dragonite would use because it could learn too many.

"But Registeel's defenses are absolutely exceptional; as long as it can seize an opportunity…"

Although the possibility was indeed very low.

Anabel didn't continue; after thinking for a moment, she realized that she actually didn't have much faith in Registeel.

While steel has resistance to dragons, it has no resistance to ghost types. Furthermore, they could see the moves that the two Pokémon in the arena had learned.

Though Dragapult didn't know fighting or ground-type moves, it had learned some fire-type moves.

Just then, Dylan recalled Dragapult.

"?" Brandon was a bit confused; Dragapult was in perfect condition.

"I'm switching opponents for you, Senior. This child has been with me for a while, but I haven't used it in a proper battle yet."

Dylan smiled helplessly. Normally, challengers had average strength, so he couldn't use his main team to face them.

"I'm really satisfied to battle with you today, Senior Brandon."

Dylan said with a smile, expressing that he was eager to battle strong trainers because he always believed that since he had captured the Pokémon, he should let them perform on the battlefield.

Otherwise, they wouldn't just be left to retire in the backyard, would they?

"..." Brandon fell silent again.

So you specifically asked Carsey to help arrange a battle with me just to let your main team stretch their legs, huh?

Alright, alright! At least I'm a champion-level trainer who has captured legendary Pokémon!!

"Dragonite! Prepare for battle!"

Dylan was oblivious to Brandon's complicated thoughts as he threw out his third Poké Ball.

Accompanied by a deep dragon roar, an orange dragon Pokémon fluttered down with its small wings, finally landing heavily in front of Dylan.

The entire body was orange, standing on two feet. Its overall appearance resembled a Western dragon, with two long and slender antennae and a small horn on the top of its head. Its body was relatively stout, but its wings were quite small compared to its body, featuring a protruding belly and a chubby face, giving it a slightly silly appearance.

This Pokémon was none other than the Johto pseudo-legendary Dragonite! The final evolution of Dratini.

Brandon's eyelids twitched. If he hadn't captured Regirock and Registeel himself, he would have suspected that Dylan was intentionally showing off his Pokémon!

Damn it! This was the third pseudo-legendary dragon Pokémon!

"Sure enough, there's a Dragonite…" Anabel pursed her lips. Was this really just a Gym Leader?

Meanwhile, Brandon was feeling a bit dazed as he looked at Erika and Ilene. He just wanted to know how this Dragonite ended up with Dylan.

"This Dragonite was given to Dylan as a reward by Champion Lance." Ilene smiled as she spoke.

Great! The Dragonite given by the old lady, the Dragapult given by Chairman Goodshow, and the Dratini given by Champion Lance. Why don't I have such noble benefactors?

Brandon felt a pang of envy, silently bearing the different joys and sorrows of people with his belly that closely resembled that of the Dragonite.

Ding~ Dragonite, Dragon and Flying type, Dragon Pokémon

Ability: Inner Focus

Signature Moves: Dragon Dance, Twister, Dragon Rush, Divine Pulse, Quick Attack, Aqua Tail, Hurricane, Earthquake, Thunder, Fire Blast, Blizzard...

Description: It can fly calmly over stormy seas, and the captains who have witnessed its graceful form call it the embodiment of the ocean.

The number of signature moves!!

Anabel gazed suspiciously at the data displayed in the Pokédex, roughly counting that it had over fifty moves!

Wait, how long has Dylan been training this Dragonite? Has he been using a move tutor? That's just too extravagant!

"Switching to Dragonite is also good."

Brandon looked at the Dragonite, which had a noticeably gentler aura compared to the aggressive Dragapult, pondering.

Dragapult was just too fast; in fact, it wasn't very good against Registeel, since Registeel couldn't fly.

Of course, Dragonite's speed was definitely faster than Registeel, but at least… at least Dragonite was four times weak to Ice.

As long as he could find an opportunity to attack Dragonite, there would be a chance to strike it down! And coincidentally, Registeel could use Ice-type moves.

"Dragonite, use Dragon Dance." Dylan still wore that warm, sunny smile.

Dragonite let out a low roar, flapping its small wings and dancing with its plump body in an ancient, mysterious pattern.

Dragon Dance enhances its speed and power!

"Registeel, Lock-On!" Brandon commanded without hesitation.

Registeel's seven red dots lit up, and a targeting marker landed on Dragonite's forehead.

Regular-type Lock-On marks the target, ensuring the next attack will hit.

In reality, there aren't really any moves that are guaranteed to hit; even moves like High-Speed Star can be dodged. But moves like Lock-On or Mind Eye that target an opponent ahead of time ensure that the next attack will definitely hit!

Of course, it will hit for sure, but that doesn't mean the opponent will just stand there and let you hit them. The opponent can counterattack with powerful moves or use defensive moves to block.

The Lock-On was completed, and Dragonite successfully performed Dragon Dance, its faint blue glow fading as its attack and speed increased by one level.

"Even after locking on, you don't initiate an attack?" Dylan's eyes narrowed slightly. So Brandon was just waiting for him to have Dragonite attack to find an opportunity.

After all, Brandon didn't immediately follow up with a Lock-On into a Hyper Beam; his intentions were clear.

"Senior Brandon, I think you might be overthinking it a bit."

Dylan chuckled lightly.

He understood Brandon's strategy: waiting for Dragonite to get close, then using the Lock-On mechanism to have Registeel attack Dragonite with Ice-type moves.

But he wasn't foolish; since you wanted to be so obvious, then naturally things wouldn't go as you wished.

"Dragonite, use Fire Blast!"

Dragonite soared above Registeel, gathering crimson flames in its mouth, and in the next moment, a flame shaped like the character '大' surged forth.

A Fire-type Fire Blast! Steel-type Registeel is weak to Fire!

"Registeel, dodge!" Brandon frowned.

Registeel immediately leaped back to avoid the approaching Fire Blast. Although its speed wasn't fast, this wasn't a close-range attack, so Registeel could barely evade it.

"Change it up, Dragonite, use Heat Wave." Dylan raised an eyebrow and snapped his fingers.

Dragonite flapped its wings vigorously, unleashing a torrential red-hot heat wave that surged forth, causing the temperature of the air to rise.

A Fire-type Heat Wave! This is one of the Fire-type moves with a very wide range.

"Registeel…"

Brandon's expression shifted unpredictably. With such a wide range for this Heat Wave, it would be hard for Registeel to dodge!

But using a defense move? That would be pointless; if it blocked the Heat Wave this time, what about the next Heat Wave?

But using Hyper Beam to block the Heat Wave? That would waste the Lock-On! Brandon hesitated at this moment.

Primarily because he hadn't expected Dylan to play like this. Your Dragonite's Special Attack couldn't compare to its Physical Attack, and didn't your Dragonite just use Dragon Dance?

I'm truly impressed; you're not going in for close combat, and you used Dragon Dance for what? Just to show off your dancing in the sky? With that big body of yours, is your dance really that beautiful?

Brandon felt a headache coming on. He suddenly realized that this heavy Registeel wasn't as lovable anymore, facing the distant, long-range attacking Dragonite like a fool just staring in amazement.

Infantry fighting against air units is tough; not being able to fly is truly miserable.

(End of this chapter)

# 138: Chapter 139:

A raging storm of red-hot heat waves surged down, while Brandon hesitated.

"Registeel, use Zap Cannon!" Brandon finally commanded.

"Re~gi!"

Registeel brought its arms together, and a ball of electromagnetic energy quickly formed.

Zap Cannon, an Electric-type move! It's extremely powerful, and any opponent hit by it will be paralyzed!

However, the only drawback of Zap Cannon is its accuracy. Since the projectile's speed isn't particularly fast, opponents can often dodge it easily.

But when paired with Lock-On, Zap Cannon becomes a perfect strike.

However, Brandon didn't actually want to use Zap Cannon. Though he loves powerful moves, as a Champion-level trainer, he naturally adapts to the battle's flow.

Dragonite isn't weak to Electric-type attacks. Even if Zap Cannon hits, it won't deal much damage compared to the cost-effectiveness of Ice-type moves like Ice Spinner.

The Zap Cannon shot toward Dragonite, colliding with the incoming heat wave.

Golden electric light clashed with the red-hot flames, and the two attacks fiercely collided.

Boom!

Eventually, Zap Cannon exploded with a burst of electromagnetic energy, scattering strong winds in all directions. The Zap Cannon successfully blocked Dragonite's heat wave.

Although the heat wave failed, Dylan didn't mind in the slightest. In fact, his smile deepened.

Because the Lock-On symbol on Dragonite's forehead had disappeared.

"Dragonite, Extreme Speed!"

A flash of white light appeared behind Dragonite as it swiftly dove toward Registeel.

Extreme Speed, a Normal-type priority move! With this boost, Dragonite's speed was astonishing!

In almost the blink of an eye, Dragonite zipped past Registeel and appeared behind it.

Registeel couldn't keep up at all!

If Extreme Speed wasn't ineffective against Registeel, Dylan wouldn't have hesitated to let Dragonite strike. Registeel would've already been hit.

"Registeel, Ice Spinner!!" Brandon no longer hesitated.

Registeel crouched down and spun rapidly, ice swirling around it.

Ice Spinner, an Ice-type move!

This is a fairly powerful Ice-type move that can also damage the battlefield. However, there was no terrain in play this time; Brandon simply wanted Registeel to attack.

The icy winds swirled, scattering ice shards as Registeel spun toward Dragonite.

But Dragonite merely flapped its wings and effortlessly evaded the Ice Spinner by moving slightly backward.

Undeterred, Registeel continued spinning toward Dragonite, which tilted its head slightly, matching Registeel's speed in a playful game of "cat and mouse."

Dragonite even deliberately slowed down and lowered its altitude, dodging at the last second or quickly accelerating to "barely avoid" Registeel's attacks.

Registeel tirelessly pursued, but everyone watching understood what was happening.

"This…"

Anabel and Carsey couldn't bear to watch anymore. It was clear Dragonite was toying with Registeel, making it look like a clown.

Brandon remained silent, his face stern.

"Brandon, your Registeel is too slow, and…" After enjoying the "cat-and-mouse" game for a while, Dylan sighed.

"So weak~"

Dylan paused, his lips curling slightly.

"This kind of attack won't defeat my Dragonite~"

His gaze fell on the spinning Ice Spinner. Dylan's eyes narrowed. Such a simple Ice-type move couldn't even hit his Dragonite.

And even if it did, so what?

"Let me show you what real power looks like~!"

"Dragonite, Dragon Rush!"

Roar~!!

Dragonite soared into the sky, spreading its limbs wide, as if an earth-shaking dragon's roar echoed around it.

Pure Dragon-type energy gathered around Dragonite, transforming into a ferocious, clawed dragon in the blink of an eye.

Dragon Rush, a Dragon-type move!

Dragonite adjusted its posture and dove down, unleashing an overwhelming aura of killing intent as it charged toward the spinning Registeel.

"This Dragon Rush… is stronger than Dragapult's and Salamence's!" Anabel's eyes narrowed. The killing intent from this Dragon Rush was more terrifying than those two!

Even as a mere spectator, Anabel felt a deep sense of dread.

"Of course it is. Before my brother got Bagon and Dreepy, he had already fully trained Dragonite," Lillie said with a proud smile on her delicate face.

What a terrifying person! Anabel was in awe.

She didn't know how Dylan managed to raise his Dragonite to be so powerful, but one thing was clear: Brandon was in big trouble.

On the battlefield.

"Registeel!" Brandon called out firmly. Since Dragonite was coming in close for a direct hit, there was no reason to fear.

Ice Spinner versus Dragon Rush! An Ice-type move against a Dragon-type move, with a clear advantage in my favor!

Besides, my Registeel is one of the legendary titans!

Registeel's spinning picked up speed, cold winds swirling around it, as it spun to meet the descending Dragon Rush.

Boom!

Dragon Rush collided with the spinning icy top, as ice and dragon energy clashed violently. The menacing dragon bared its fangs in silent fury.

The surging waves of energy from the clash spread out, sending shockwaves across the battlefield.

But Brandon quickly noticed something was wrong—Dragon Rush was slowly overpowering Ice Spinner, pushing Registeel down! His expression grew serious.

"Dragonite, finish it off!"

Roar~!!

The fierce dragon roared silently, and its draconic energy suddenly surged, pressing down hard.

Crack!

Ice Spinner's icy light shattered under the force of Dragon Rush, leaving Registeel exposed. The remaining energy slammed into Registeel, driving it into the ground.

Boom!

The ground shook like an earthquake, cracks forming all over the battlefield. The sheer weight behind Dragonite's Dragon Rush was staggering.

How is it this strong?!!

Brandon was shocked. Registeel might not be the best attacker, but it was still a legendary Pokémon.

Plus, Ice beats Dragon. Even if Registeel's power was slightly inferior, Ice Spinner should not have lost to Dragon Rush so quickly.

But it didn't matter. Dragon Rush was certainly impressive, but it's no use!

Registeel's defense is incredibly high, and Steel resists Dragon. Dragon Rush's effectiveness would be limited.

Registeel still had a chance!

"Dragonite, Earthquake!"

Dragonite flapped its small wings and rose slightly, looking down at the fallen Registeel, and then slammed back down.

Earthquake, a Ground-type move!

Boom!

The battlefield, which had just settled, trembled violently once again, with a solid shockwave of Earthquake pulsing out.

The Earthquake hit Registeel squarely, and it let out a pained cry.

Ground beats Steel! Earthquake's damage to Registeel wasn't low.

"One more Earthquake!"

Dylan glanced at Brandon's slightly tense face, his smile widening. It should be over soon.

Dragonite raised one foot.

Brandon's expression changed. He quickly retrieved Registeel's Poké Ball, intending to recall it.

He realized now that Registeel simply couldn't handle Dragonite in battle—or rather, an opponent like Dragonite, who was both fast and had a wide range of moves.

Especially now that Registeel had been completely outmatched. Even with its high defense, it wouldn't last much longer!

"Registeel, return…"

But at that moment…

"Fire Spin," Dylan said nonchalantly.

Brandon's expression changed dramatically.

Dragonite halted its Earthquake attack and opened its mouth, releasing scorching flames. The flames rippled in waves, engulfing both Registeel and itself.

The red light from Brandon's Pokéball, meant to recall Registeel, was blocked by the Fire Spin, unable to reach the Steel-type Pokémon.

Fire Spin, a Fire-type move, deals continuous damage and prevents the opponent from recalling their Pokémon.

"Dylan!"

Despite his usual calm demeanor, Brandon's composure shifted at the sudden turn of events.

Is this even a human? Could a person even do something like this? All I wanted to do was recall Registeel!

"After all, your Registeel is too dangerous, so I'd prefer to take it down in one go," Dylan smiled faintly as he addressed Brandon, whose face had darkened.

The flames of Fire Spin roared on, and the trapped Registeel was clearly suffering, burned by the intense heat.

"???"

Brandon stared at Dylan in shock, his eyes filled with disbelief.

'Your Registeel is too dangerous'? He wanted to ask Dylan what kind of mindset he had to say something so disingenuous.

Is my Registeel dangerous? Is it more dangerous than your Dragonite? Your Dragonite literally trapped my 'dangerous' Registeel to pummel it!

"Dragonite, Earthquake."

Dylan issued the command calmly. It would be nice to unlock the achievement of defeating a legendary Pokémon, after all.

Even though Registeel didn't pose much of a threat, it was still a legendary Pokémon.

Still, he had to be cautious of Brandon making a desperate move. Registeel was in a losing position, and it was hard to predict what Brandon might do next.

Dragonite raised its right foot, ready to slam it down.

"Registeel, Explosion!" Brandon shouted with urgency toward his trapped Registeel.

If you won't let me recall Registeel, then let's end this for everyone!

Registeel raised its head, and a blinding white light began to radiate from its body.

Normal-type move: Explosion! It causes a massive explosion, damaging everything around it, with the user losing its ability to battle afterward.

Clearly, Brandon had reached his limit.

"Protect."

Dylan raised an eyebrow, having anticipated this.

Dragonite flapped its wings and swiftly retreated, raising a green barrier of Protect.

Boom!

The deafening blast echoed throughout the Battle Pyramid King, and a powerful shockwave swept across the field. Even those in the audience, like Lillie, could feel the intense wind, forcing them to shield their faces.

The explosion's power was overwhelming, but…

As the green light from Protect began to fade, the barrier in front of Dragonite cracked but ultimately held up, successfully blocking the explosion.

Brandon was silent. It seemed as though every move he made had been predicted by Dylan. How was he supposed to defeat someone like this?

Especially now, as he glanced at the crater where Registeel lay unconscious while Dragonite stood unscathed, Brandon found himself at a loss for words.

"Registeel is unable to battle. Dragonite wins," Anabel declared the result of the battle.

"Yay! My brother beat Registeel!" Lillie exclaimed joyfully, clasping her hands over her chest in excitement.

Next to her, Ilene was also thrilled. After all, Registeel was a legendary Pokémon, and yet Dragonite had forced it to use Explosion in desperation. Even then, Protect blocked the attack.

She wondered what Brandon was thinking now.

"Hehe… as expected of Leader Dylan," Carsey chuckled awkwardly, feeling a bit uneasy. Brandon seemed to be in a tough spot, and Dylan was being relentless.

If you're this strong, couldn't you have given him a little leeway? Blocking Brandon from recalling Registeel like that, completely overpowering him, was a bit much. Now, as the person who arranged the battle, what am I supposed to do?

But just then, Brandon made a decision.

"I forfeit."

Brandon took a deep breath and raised his hand, addressing Anabel, who was acting as the referee.

"Huh?" Anabel was taken aback.

"Sir, there's no need to be so polite. I believe you can still fight," Dylan said sincerely, his tone earnest.

"No need to go easy on me. I can handle it."

Brandon's face twitched slightly. No, I'm really not being polite, and I certainly wasn't going easy on you.

If anything, you should have shown me some mercy, you jerk! You didn't even let me recall my Pokémon. Do you not feel the slightest bit guilty?

"Dylan, I've seen enough of your strength. I'm no match for you," Brandon shook his head, feeling that there was no point in continuing.

If Registeel ended up like this, Regirock probably wouldn't fare any better.

"But sir, I believe…" Dylan began, concerned that Brandon was giving up too soon. This was supposed to be a full battle, and only three rounds had been completed. Was he really going to bail?

I've only used three Pokémon. You should at least let me bring out my whole team!

"You rarely visit my Battle Pyramid King. I've stored some fine tea. Why don't you join me for a cup?" Brandon interrupted him, regaining his usual serious expression.

"Thank you for your hospitality," Dylan replied reluctantly, realizing Brandon had no intention of continuing the battle.

There was no helping it; the other party no longer wanted to fight.

Too bad. His Goodra hadn't even had a chance to make an appearance. Dylan had been saving Goodra as his trump card, planning to send it out last. After all, Goodra had been with him since he received it along with Toxapex. Besides Meowscarada, it was one of his veterans, but it still hadn't had the chance to shine in an official battle…

Led by Brandon's assistant, Dylan and the others made their way to the guest lounge. However, Brandon pulled Carsey aside.

Carsey, sweating nervously, looked at Brandon, who wore a serious expression with a hint of concealed frustration.

"Carsey, is there something you'd like to explain to me?"

Being questioned by the Battle Pyramid King—what should I do?

---

# 139: Chapter 140: Mother

In the guest lounge of the Battle Pyramid King.

"I heard that you're still searching for Regice, correct?" Dylan took a sip of the tea prepared by Brandon's assistant and asked Brandon.

It wasn't clear what conversation had transpired between Brandon and Carsey while they talked alone outside, but when Carsey returned, he looked shaken and deeply unsettled.

Hmm, probably had a good 'chat' with Brandon.

"That's right. And I've also found some clues regarding the location of Regice's seal," Brandon smiled faintly at the mention of Regice, clearly the most important matter to him at the moment.

Once he found Regice, he could capture the third of the legendary titans.

"Mr. Brandon, you're truly remarkable," Anabel remarked sincerely. Capturing a legendary Pokémon was certainly praiseworthy, especially considering how much time and effort Brandon had invested.

"But I've heard that the 'Legendary Giants' aren't just Regirock, Registeel, and Regice. I wonder if you're aware of that?" Dylan pondered for a moment before smiling at Brandon.

"Oh? Are you referring to Regigigas, Dylan?" Brandon thought for a moment, recalling the legendary Regigigas, which was said to have created the three titans. If there were other 'giants,' it must have been Regigigas.

"No, not Regigigas," Dylan shook his head. Regigigas didn't count as one of the 'Legendary Giants.'

"Then there are other giants?" Brandon was stunned for a moment, then suddenly became excited.

"Yes, I've taken an interest in the legends from various regions, so I know a little about them," Dylan replied humbly.

"According to an ancient text I once read, the 'Legendary Giants' are also known as 'Eternal Pokémon.' This term refers to Regirock, Regice, and Registeel collectively, but in the distant Galar region, there are two more giants!" Dylan smiled as he spoke.

Brandon stared intently at Dylan, eager to hear more. He had spent so much time searching for the 'giants' because of his love for the Legendary Giants.

"Regieleki and Regidrago are the names of these two giants. They were created by Regigigas but were sealed away by the ancient people of Galar, so their existence is not widely known," Dylan explained with a smile.

"Regieleki and Regidrago…" Brandon and the others, including Anabel and Carsey, were mulling over these unfamiliar names.

"Dylan, what type are these giants?" Brandon was clearly excited, deeply intrigued by the mention of these new giants.

"The Legendary Giants were crafted by Regigigas from crystallized rock, ice, magma, electricity, and dragon energy," Dylan smiled as he answered, though he still didn't quite understand why the Steel-type Registeel represented magma.

It was just as baffling as why Regieleki and Regidrago, despite their immensely powerful abilities, had a creator like Regigigas, which was hampered by its Slow Start ability.

But these things weren't Dylan's main concern.

"So they're Electric-type and Dragon-type?" Anabel asked curiously. Since they were made from electricity and dragon energy, it seemed clear they'd be Electric-type and Dragon-type, respectively.

"Dylan, do you know where they're sealed?" Brandon's blood was boiling with excitement. He had been wondering what to do after finding Regice—should he then search for Regigigas?

But now, he had a new target. He wanted to find Regieleki and Regidrago.

As for Galar being too far away? That was a laughable concern—love for these Pokémon could not be limited by distance!

"Well," Dylan hesitated for a moment.

"Dylan, rest assured, even if I find where Regieleki and Regidrago are sealed, I won't act impulsively. I believe you're the better candidate to capture them," Brandon assured him, understanding that his earlier question had been somewhat rude.

Legendary Pokémon were rare and valuable, but Brandon had no selfish desires for them. He was willing to risk his life for Pokémon.

Dylan, of course, trusted Brandon. Though he might appear stern and difficult to approach, Brandon was actually passionate, caring for his juniors, and deeply in love with Pokémon—a fantastic senior to look up to.

"It's not that—I just don't know their exact sealing location. But they're most likely sealed in the Crown Tundra of Galar," Dylan speculated. While he knew their location from the games, reality could differ, so who knew if anything had changed?

"If you want to search for them, you could try the area around the Split Plains in the Crown Tundra," Dylan suggested.

"Great! Once I find Regice, I'll head to Galar!" Brandon agreed enthusiastically, filled with determination.

"Dylan, when I find where they're sealed, I'll contact you so you can come capture them," Brandon promised, eager to witness the appearance of these two new giants.

"Thank you very much," Dylan replied warmly, expressing his gratitude. This had been his goal all along.

Regidrago and Regieleki were much stronger than the three original titans, and Dylan was genuinely interested in them.

"No need to thank me. This is your information, after all. It's the best outcome," Brandon smiled even more, his tone much softer now.

He had no doubt about Dylan's abilities, and if Dylan could capture Regieleki and Regidrago, perhaps he'd become the Champion of Kanto someday!

After chatting for a while longer, Dylan, Lillie, and Ilene finally bid farewell and left.

"Kanto is going to produce an extraordinary talent," Anabel remarked as she watched them leave.

"That's a good thing," Brandon shook his head.

"It just means it'll be tough for other trainers of his generation," he added with a hint of pity for those who would have to compete with Dylan.

Brandon didn't mind, though. His time had passed, but…

"Carsey, next time you arrange a battle for me, please make sure you know the challenger's strength," Brandon said sternly.

"If you send me another trainer like him, don't bother contacting me again!"

Brandon's tone was stiff. It was undeniable that he was indeed optimistic about Dylan, but today's battle...

How should one put it? Brandon admired his own cleverness. If he kept fighting, he feared he might genuinely suffer a cerebral hemorrhage.

The battle had been too overwhelming. He had never experienced such a suffocating match before, as if a stifling weight pressed on his chest, leaving him breathless.

"Alright, alright,"

Carsey wiped the cold sweat from his forehead with a handkerchief.

...

In the blink of an eye, another week had passed.

In the battle simulation world, Dylan had already reached the Aether Foundation storyline, where Lusamine, using the power of Nebby, had entered the Ultra Space along with Guzma.

Due to the forced energy release, Nebby evolved from Cosmog into Cosmoem.

At the Moone Altar, it was a deep and quiet night.

"Mother..."

Lillie, now with her hair tied into a high ponytail and her bag slung over her shoulder, carried a composed yet melancholic demeanor.

Lusamine's rejection had dealt her a huge blow, but...

Lillie turned her head and saw Dylan waving at her from another platform. Remembering how much he had helped her along the way, Lillie made up her mind.

She walked to her designated platform, opened her bag, revealing the evolved Cosmoem inside.

Lillie placed Cosmoem on the ground and took out the Sun Flute to play it.

On the opposite platform, Dylan also brought out the Moone Flute and began to play.

As the gentle evening breeze blew, the enchanting sounds of both flutes intertwined, creating a serene and beautiful melody.

In front of the altar, the massive pillar began to move, unleashing a brilliant surge of energy that shot down, directly hitting Cosmoem.

Blinding light erupted, accompanied by a violent gust of wind.

Dylan stood behind Lillie, looking up at the high platform. As the light faded, a pair of bat-like wings, shaped like a crescent moon, unfolded.

With a bat-like appearance, its body was dark purple, its wings adorned with golden moon crescents. Its head was small, with a deep blue section displaying ever-changing star constellations.

It was none other than Lunala, the 'Legendary Pokémon of the Cosmos.'

Lunala let out a raspy cry, flying a lap around the air before gracefully landing in front of Lillie.

"This scene…"

Watching Lillie and Lunala in such a heartwarming and harmonious atmosphere, Dylan couldn't help but feel a sense of déjà vu.

At that moment, a pale white Ultra Wormhole opened, and two figures fell out.

One of them was Lusamine.

"Mother!" Lillie cried out in alarm, rushing toward Lusamine.

However, Lusamine seemed severely injured. When she saw Lillie, she struggled to lift her hand and gently caressed her daughter's face.

"Lillie, run... that thing... is a monster..." Lusamine's voice was weak.

Dylan glanced at Guzma, who lay unconscious not far from Lusamine. The leader of Team Skull had passed out the moment he hit the ground.

Screech!

A piercing sound like claws scraping glass echoed as a giant black claw stretched out from the still-open Ultra Wormhole, gripping the edge.

Lillie gasped.

"Necrozma~!"

With a menacing growl, a massive black Pokémon emerged from the Ultra Wormhole, letting out a roaring cry to the sky.

Its aura radiated a tangible malevolence, and the pressure was overwhelming.

Its entire body appeared to be made of black crystals, with long, slender arms ending in three claws. Its head was large, with a glowing central section of colored matter.

Dylan's eyes lit up instantly—this was Necrozma!

"That's Necrozma. When it awakens, it's extremely violent and destroys everything around it in an instant."

Lusamine squeezed Lillie's hand. After uttering those words, she fell unconscious.

Lillie pressed her lips together, then resolutely stood up, staring at Necrozma in the sky. Lunala appeared behind her.

Dylan, standing behind her, yawned out of boredom. Although it was quite interesting, this cutscene was starting to feel too long.

Necrozma fixated on Lunala, its aura growing stronger.

"Nebby!"

Lillie's expression was resolute—this time, she would not be afraid!

Sensing Lillie's determination, Lunala flapped its wings and shot toward Necrozma in the sky.

For the glory of the moon god!!!

Then…

Necrozma pressed its massive claw onto Lunala's head, pinning it to the ground. Its body swiftly disassembled into black crystals and forcefully merged with Lunala. After a flash of light...

Necrozma's Dawn Wings form emerged!!

A terrifying and oppressive presence filled the air, and with Nebby now absorbed, Lillie stared at the transformed Necrozma in terror, her face pale as she stepped back in fear.

Despair was spreading.

But...

Dylan patted Lillie on the shoulder. Seeing the young face so similar to the real Lillie's, yet more innocent, he gave her a reassuring smile.

Lillie pursed her lips tightly.

Dylan raised his Pokéball, confidently pointing it at the airborne Necrozma in its Dawn Wings form.

Finally, it was time for him to shine!

(End of chapter)

# 140: Chapter 141: Luxury cars? What are those?

A wild Necrozma: Dawn Wings has initiated a battle with you!

As the battle began, Dylan glanced at Necrozma: Dawn Wings' level: Hmm, level fifty.

It's not surprising, considering this is the storyline of the first playthrough, but that's a good thing; otherwise, Dylan wouldn't be able to capture Necrozma.

On Dylan's side, he had a level 64 Zoroark.

And then...

There was nothing more to say. Under the game mechanics, in the face of Zoroark's Dark Pulse, Necrozma: Dawn Wings was just a passing breeze.

With Lunala as an accessory, Necrozma: Dawn Wings' attributes were Psychic and Ghost, making it four times weak to Ghost and Dark. Even with the Prism Armor ability reducing the damage taken, it still faced a threefold weakness.

The growth simulation world is modeled after the game world, and if it were the real world, even a level fifty Necrozma: Dawn Wings would likely wipe the floor with Dylan.

After being defeated, Necrozma: Dawn Wings fled in embarrassment back to the Ultra Space, but Dylan wasn't worried; this was just the first attempt.

However, the sunlight in the Alola region had disappeared, completely absorbed by Necrozma: Dawn Wings.

"Dylan, thank you!"

As Necrozma: Dawn Wings retreated, Lillie's small face was filled with gratitude.

Dylan just smiled and nodded. To be frank, Lillie wasn't as cute as his own little sister, Lillie.

If it were his little sister, who was becoming increasingly cheerful, she would be holding her hands together in front of her, gazing at Dylan with starry eyes.

"Brother, you're amazing~!"

The emotional value and satisfaction from the youthful admiration of a girl couldn't be compared to this somewhat melancholic and mature Lillie.

So Dylan remained calm.

Continuing with the storyline, Dylan arrived at the Ultra Metropolis, riding under the technological prowess of the Ultra Recon Squad.

This was one of the Ultra Spaces, and the homeland of the Ultra Recon Squad—a dark world without light. Ultra Metropolis was the only city in this world.

Dylan reached the top of the Ultra Metropolis Tower and saw Necrozma again.

However, this time, due to absorbing Lunala and a large amount of light, Necrozma had entered a completely new form!

Ultimate Necrozma made its appearance!

An unparalleled pressure weighed down on the air, making it feel incredibly heavy. Its body shimmered with golden light, brilliant to the extreme.

Lv. 60 Ultimate Necrozma has initiated a battle with you!

And then...

After being defeated a second time, Necrozma opened the Ultra Space and fled again.

After a series of events, Dylan embarked on the path to challenge the Alola League. Then, at the midsection of Mount Lanakila, he found a severely weakened Necrozma lying in a pit.

After being defeated, Cosmog had left its body and lost a massive amount of light and energy, returning to its normal Necrozma form.

Lv. 65 Necrozma has initiated a battle with you again!

And then...

"Hahaha~!! Caught it! I finally caught it!"

Dylan looked at the Ultra Ball in his hand that had captured Necrozma, unable to suppress the smile that spread across his face, which ultimately turned into uncontrollable laughter that echoed far and wide.

Caught it! One of the legendary cosmic Pokémon, Necrozma!

According to the official certification from the seventh generation, Ultimate Necrozma's power "surpasses all legendary Pokémon from previous generations!"

It surpassed all past legends! This meant that neither Mega Rayquaza, the Sinnoh Trio, nor the Ice Dragon Kyurem, or even 100% Zygarde could compete with Ultimate Necrozma!

Of course, this statement only included "legendary Pokémon" and did not cover mythical Pokémon. But aside from Arceus, no other mythical Pokémon could match Ultimate Necrozma in combat power.

As for the eighth-generation Eternatus and the ninth-generation "Tera Origin" Terapagos form of Stellar Form, Dylan didn't think they could defeat Ultimate Necrozma either.

After capturing Necrozma, Dylan had been crazy about leveling it up without wasting any time.

He charged into the Alola League, defeating champions Mallow, Lillie, Acerola, and Kahili, before finally defeating his last opponent, Hau.

Dylan successfully crowned himself the champion of Alola!

"Ding~ Congratulations to the host for becoming the champion of the Alola region. The host can choose one Pokémon from the champion team to manifest."

The system's light screen appeared just in time in front of Dylan.

And Dylan's champion team consisted of Necrozma, Darkrai, Zoroark, Mimikyu, Dragonite, and Gengar.

Did he need to choose?

"System, I choose Necrozma."

"Host has chosen Necrozma. Is this confirmation?"

"Confirmed."

"Selection successful. The host may take Necrozma out of the growth simulation."

The system's light screen flickered rapidly.

"The Alola region has been cleared. The host may choose to travel to other regions and may return to the Kanto region at any time."

Dylan looked at the system's light screen and fell into thought.

"System, if I continue with the subsequent storyline, and Necrozma fuses with Lunala to become Dusk Mane Necrozma, can I successfully bring it out of the growth simulation?"

Yes, Dylan was contemplating just that.

After becoming the champion of Alola, if he continued the storyline, Lillie would request him to capture Lunala and travel with her. As long as he obtained Lunala, Necrozma could fuse into Necrozma: Dawn Wings!

"Necrozma: Dawn Wings' master is Necrozma, so yes."

The system's light screen quickly provided a response.

"Sounds great~!"

A smile appeared on Dylan's face. This was excellent; not only did he obtain Necrozma, but it also meant he could get Lunala for free!

Well, there was no need to say more. First, he would level up Necrozma, aiming for it to reach the maximum level as soon as possible, but unfortunately, time was running short, and he would likely have to wait for Lunala.

At eight in the morning, Dylan woke up, washed up, and headed to the backyard.

"Brother~"

Lillie was preparing breakfast for the Drapion and other Pokémon. Upon seeing Dylan, she broke into a pure smile.

"Sure enough, my sister is the best~"

Dylan ruffled his precious sister's golden hair. Although Lillie was cute on the outside, she was ultimately just an "Lillie."

"Eh?"

Lillie instinctively closed her eyes to enjoy her brother's head pat, turning her head in confusion at his words. Brothers always said such nonsensical things; of course, beautiful girls are all fragrant!

"Come here, Lillie. Let me show you something big."

Dylan took out an Ultra Ball, unable to suppress the smile on his face.

"Emmm...?" Lillie curiously looked at the fancy Ultra Ball in Dylan's hand. She had never seen such a luxurious Poké Ball before.

And did her adoptive father send another Pokémon to her brother?

"Necrozma!" Dylan opened the Ultra Ball.

"Lino~"

With a flash of white light and a low, hoarse cry, a deep blue Pokémon appeared in the air.

In the next moment, an unexpected change occurred. The originally clear sky quickly turned dark, as if all sunlight had been completely absorbed by a black hole.

In just a few seconds, night fell!

"This... Brother?"

Lillie had been curiously watching the unfamiliar deep blue Pokémon, but with the onset of night, she fearfully moved closer to Dylan.

This was too strange; how could it happen so suddenly?

Meanwhile, Cosmog, who was just about to poke its head out from Lillie's bag, quickly retracted its head back inside, not daring to show itself.

"Meow?"

A plump "Pipi" suddenly appeared in front of Dylan, its eyes gravely looking at Necrozma, then turning to Dylan, letting out a questioning cry.

"This is my Pokémon; don't worry." Dylan comforted "Pipi," understanding what it meant.

"Lillie, don't be afraid. Necrozma, as long as there's enough light, don't absorb anymore."

Dylan gently patted the girl's shoulder and smiled at Necrozma.

Indeed, the sudden darkness was Necrozma's doing. Light was the source of Necrozma's life; without light, it would be in immense pain.

This was also why in the original story, it hunted Solgaleo and Lunala, because both Solgaleo and Lunala contained a vast amount of light and Ultra energy, which was exactly what Necrozma needed.

Just now, Necrozma had absorbed all the light in this vast area, causing darkness to envelop the sky.

"Lino~"

Necrozma replied with a sound, slowly landing in front of Dylan and the others, retracting its imposing aura.

Lillie hid behind Dylan, her big eyes filled with curiosity and a hint of fear.

According to her brother, the strange phenomenon of day suddenly turning to night was caused by this Pokémon!

"This is truly incredible. Necrozma, do you feel anything unusual?"

Dylan observed Necrozma in front of him, his eyes shimmering with a hint of excitement.

Because the Necrozma before him was actually deep blue! This was the color of Shiny Necrozma.

Is it because of the system's manifestation? In the growth simulation, Necrozma was normally black.

Necrozma let out a low hum to indicate that everything was normal.

"That's good."

Dylan nodded, feeling relieved; as long as there were no abnormalities, that was fine. As for why his Necrozma suddenly shone, Dylan inquired with the system.

'Pokémon manifested from the growth simulation have a chance of becoming alternate-colored? That's a surprise.'

Dylan raised an eyebrow at the system's response; this was indeed a significant surprise. Legendary Pokémon were already rare, not to mention a Shiny variant.

Moreover, if Dylan remembered correctly, the Dawn Wings and Dusk Mane of Shiny Necrozma were both pink, while its Ultimate Form was an incredibly cool gray-blue.

"Brother, what kind of Pokémon is this?" Lillie quietly asked Dylan.

From both its imposing aura and the fact that it absorbed all the sunlight upon appearing, there was no doubt that this Pokémon was extraordinary.

"This is Necrozma, the Pokémon I just captured last night," Dylan laughed.

"Rotom." Dylan called out to the Rotom Pokédex.

"Coming, coming, Rotom~"

The Rotom Pokédex flew over and pointed at Necrozma.

'Ding~ Necrozma, Psychic type, Prism Pokémon.

Ability: Prism Armor Signature Moves: Prismatic Laser, Photon Geyser, Power Gem, Iron Defense, Psycho Cut, Sucker Punch, Wide Guard...

Description: Light is its source of energy. If it doesn't consume light, impurities will slowly accumulate, causing it to darken and become unable to move.'

Listening to the Rotom Pokédex's introduction, Lillie blinked. This Pokémon actually feeds on light? But that seems like an insatiable appetite!

Lillie gazed into the distance, where the vast sky remained shrouded in darkness, showing no signs of an end.

How much light has Necrozma consumed?

"Necrozma has already stopped 'feeding'; the light should return in no time."

Dylan looked at the deep blue Necrozma with great satisfaction. If Necrozma wanted to 'evolve' into its Ultimate Form in reality, it needed light and energy.

The simplest way to achieve this was to fuse with either Solgaleo or Lunala and then provide a large amount of light to transform into its Ultimate Form.

In the game, during a Solar Eclipse (Lunar Eclipse), Necrozma carrying the Ultra Necrozma Z could unleash its Ultimate Burst in battle, becoming Ultra Necrozma.

Both methods were viable, but undoubtedly, they required either Solgaleo or Lunala to serve as a pendant for Necrozma.

Dylan was not in a hurry; he could have Necrozma fuse with Lunala tonight.

Right now...

"Hello? Chairman Goodshow, good morning~ Did you say it's dark here? Yeah, it's strange; it suddenly turned dark for me. This should be a total solar eclipse, right? I don't really know."

"That's right, Chairman. I'd like to ask for a few days off to visit the Unova region. Yes, I want to find Reshiram."

"Ha ha~ Thank you, Chairman. Sure, I'll do my best to bring Reshiram back to Kanto. Great! When the time comes, you can take a picture with Reshiram, okay? Could you please let Miss Jenny know?"

"I appreciate it, Chairman. Please wait for my good news."

After ending the call with Goodshow, Dylan patted Necrozma's sturdy arm, feeling quite pleased.

"Lillie, let's go have some fun in Unova for a few days~"

"Sure~! Is brother going to find the real White Dragon Reshiram?"

Lillie smiled happily; she also heard Dylan's conversation with Goodshow.

"Of course! Your brother is going to be a hero this time!"

"Yeah, yeah~ Brother can definitely do it!"

"Hahaha~ I'll borrow Lillie's words. After capturing Reshiram, we'll ride on the White Dragon's back for a joyride."

Dylan laughed heartily and ruffled his precious little sister's head. The White Dragon was so beautiful; there couldn't be a more magnificent mount, right?!

Once he captured Reshiram, he wouldn't need to drive anymore. Luxury cars? What are those?

Is riding on a double-tailed White Dragon comfortable?

(End of this chapter)

# 141: Chapter 142: What is your truth

I Want to Defeat the Black Dragon and Its Hero! What Is Your True Self?

"Recently, the handsome Dylan has mainly been going out to train. Trainers looking to challenge the Gym are advised to come back in a few days."

"Friendly reminder: If you encounter the wild Dylan in the wild, there is a chance he will accept your challenge and reward you with a lovely gift—Rota Gym Notice Board."

Looking at the wooden sign standing in front of the Rota Gym, the bespectacled challenger had a dark expression.

"You get weekends off every week, and you still want to go out to play on weekdays?! That's just too much!"

The bespectacled challenger cursed as he walked away, wondering what kind of team the wild Dylan would use if he encountered him in the wild.

And a lovely gift sounds a bit enticing.

Later, I'll share this news in the group to see if anyone encounters Dylan in the wild. If they do, they must challenge him.

...

At this moment, Dylan was already on a plane to the Unova region with Lillie, not worried about the Gym at all.

In fact, Dylan didn't just put up a notice board; he also sent out a notification through the official account of Rota Gym, which received quite a bit of criticism.

But Dylan didn't care about that; nothing was more important than his desire for the twin-tailed White Dragon.

Castelia City, the commercial and economic center of the Unova region, is known for its "flying clouds" which symbolize auspiciousness.

As the commercial center of Unova, Castelia City is extremely developed, one of the most advanced cities in the world.

Skyscrapers stand shoulder to shoulder with ordinary buildings, soaring into the clouds, creating a spectacular view.

At two o'clock in the afternoon, Dylan and Lillie finally disembarked from the plane and saw this modern city.

"Wow~ This is so high, right?"

Lillie took out her camera and took many photos, unable to suppress her adorable exclamations.

It was her first time seeing such magnificent high-rise buildings; there were just so many of them here.

"Let's go; we'll probably need to rest here today."

"Mm-hmm~"

Lillie's face was filled with joy as she squinted her smiling eyes and wrapped her arm around Dylan's. With her other hand, she dialed Amelia's number.

Because she had turned on airplane mode on the plane, she saw her mother Amelia's message after getting connected.

"Hello~ Mother, I was just on the plane. Yeah~ Hehe, my brother brought me to the Unova region to play! Oh? Are you guys heading back to the Alola region?"

Lillie paused for a moment, recalling that she had already spent over a month in Kanto!

No, it had been more than a month...

Time really flies, doesn't it? She had almost forgotten about it.

"Right, so I wanted to ask you, Lillie, when do you plan to return to the Alola region?" Amelia asked her while she was in Kanaz City with Itto.

"Ah?"

Lillie's expression slightly changed, feeling conflicted.

If it were before, she would have immediately returned to Alola without a second thought, as the Alola region was her hometown, where her real mother and brother were.

She also wanted to stop her mother, Lusamine, from pursuing the dangerous idea of entering Ultra Space.

And she was also worried about her brother Gladion. She had no idea how he was doing.

"If you're not in a hurry, you can continue playing with Dylan. We're not in a rush to return to Alola; it's perfect to explore Hoenn."

Amelia, noticing Lillie's unease, said gently with a smile.

"Is Dylan taking good care of you? If he mistreats you, let your mother know, and I'll help teach him a lesson. Lillie, you're our little princess. Dylan is actually quite attentive, but I'm not sure how well he takes care of girls."

"No, no, my brother treats me very well."

Lillie secretly glanced at Dylan, her delicate cheeks slightly blushing. If her brother heard that, it would be embarrassing.

"That's good. So how about we return to Alola in another month?"

"…Alright then."

Lillie hesitated but agreed. In another month! In another month, she would definitely return to Alola!

By then, she would get her brother to go back with her; surely he would find a way to make her mother wake up.

Anyway… she was currently the goddaughter of her godfather and mother, so she could be considered half a Kanto person.

Staying in Kanto a little longer wouldn't be a problem, right?

Even if she was in Kanto, she would never forget Alola! Nor would she forget her mother and brother Gladion!

That's right, she wasn't that kind of girl!

"Ugh~ Brother, this is so delicious! I've never had such tasty ice cream!"

Lillie squinted her eyes, gently licking the ice cream in her hand, a look of satisfaction on her face, her golden ponytail swaying back and forth.

Since they were out traveling, Lillie wore a casual short-sleeved shirt and a short skirt, looking youthful and pure.

"Right? This Castelia Ice Cream is the most famous ice cream in the Unova region. So if you come to Unova, you definitely have to try it."

Dylan said with a smile. The deliciousness of Castelia Ice Cream had even spread to other regions.

"But because of its fame, there's often a long line in front of the Castelia Ice Cream shop every day, making it not easy to get."

"Ugh~ What a pity! I won't be able to eat this once I return to Kanto." Lillie said, feeling a bit disappointed.

"Don't worry; I've already gotten their contact number. In the future, I'll have them airship it over. I promised Ilene I would bring some back for her too."

Dylan said with a smile, noting that anything that can be solved with money isn't a problem. As long as one is willing to pay, Castelia Ice Cream could be shipped to Kanto.

Before coming to Unova, Ilene had asked him to help bring some Castelia Ice Cream, but unfortunately, she couldn't come along these days.

"That must cost a lot, right?" Lillie hesitated.

"It's okay; it won't cost much. Besides, that old man's money is meant for us to use. He would love for us to spend more. If we don't spend it, his money will rot in the bank."

Dylan replied casually. That old man's money had become just a number; there was no need to worry about it.

Lillie adorably stuck out her tongue. While her mother's Aether Foundation was also very wealthy, compared to her godfather, umm...

"Let's go! It's not often that we're here; let's explore well."

"Okay~"

"There are too many people on this road. Hold onto me tightly; don't get lost."

"Hehe~ Okay, brother."

The girl tightly held her brother's hand, feeling a sense of security, while her other hand held the Castelia Ice Cream, feeling that her entire being was radiant.

How happy~ This life is so blissful~!

The afternoon passed quickly. Dylan and Lillie finally arrived at a famous trendy restaurant to have dinner.

"Brother, where are we going tomorrow?"

After ordering, Lillie asked Dylan. She didn't actually know the upcoming itinerary. She mainly knew her brother came to Unova for the true dragon god Reshiram, so strategies seemed unnecessary.

"Let's check out Icirrus City tomorrow."

Dylan pondered for a moment before speaking. Finding Reshiram should be simple; it should be in the Dragon Spiral Tower, as that was where Reshiram (and Zekrom) had been sealed a thousand years ago.

But at that moment, Dylan suddenly froze.

"Ugh~ Hilda, I dreamt about that again last night." A melodious and crisp voice sounded mournfully and helplessly.

Hilda?

Dylan paused, what a coincidence?

Dylan turned around and saw two girls sitting at a table behind him, but from Dylan's perspective, he could only see the girl who was speaking.

But that was enough; it was a girl with a fair and pure face, wearing a fitted half-sleeve shirt, with her brown hair styled into two buns, leaving two strands that fell like twin tails.

She was wearing yellow shorts and black tights that wrapped around her long, slender legs.

This was Rosa from Black and White 2.

And naturally, the girl sitting opposite Rosa was unmistakably the protagonist from Black and White, Hilda.

What a surprise, encountering two heroines at once!

Dylan's eyes flickered slightly, interesting. It seemed both of these girls were trainers and likely set out on their journey this year

"I feel the same way; I don't know what that voice is about either." Hilda stirred the savory soup in her bowl with a spoon, propping her cheek up with one hand as she spoke, her tone laced with fatigue.

"It's too much! What does it mean to disturb our sleep every day? Don't they know that beautiful girls need plenty of sleep to grow? My dark circles have been getting worse lately."

Rosa complained, looking quite wronged.

No, your development is actually completely sufficient.

Upon hearing this, Hilda's eyelids twitched as she glanced at her best friend Rosa, who had two lumps of fat pressing on the table, and couldn't help but tighten her lips, feeling a pang of jealousy.

So infuriating! I don't know what Rosa has been eating since childhood. We're both sixteen; why is Rosa so much bigger than me?!!

"Every day, it's just questions like, 'What is your truth?' 'What's your truth?' 'I'm waiting for your answer,' such nonsensical stuff."

"Who knows what truth is? I used to dream about so many delicious and fun things every day, but now I'm being disturbed by these mysterious questions daily."

"Hilda, do you think we might be cursed? No! I haven't even had a sweet romance yet, and I haven't become the Champion of Unova!"

Rosa's mouth was like a machine gun, non-stop chatter, but by the end, she seemed to scare herself and looked anxious.

"No, it's impossible to be cursed. I think you should go see a brain doctor; you're such an idiot," Hilda said, a hint of disdain in her voice.

Her best friend was usually so carefree, always coming up with bizarre ideas that made it hard for her to hold back.

I don't know if Rosa is sending all her nutrients to her chest; otherwise, how could she always be so silly?

"Too much, Hilda! How can you call me stupid!" Rosa pouted, quite indignant.

"That voice is either a ghost-type Pokémon playing tricks on us, or…"

Hilda rolled her eyes, too lazy to talk more with this big-chested fool. She looked down thoughtfully at the soup in her bowl.

Recently, she had been traveling to continue her Gym journey when one day, she suddenly heard a vague voice in her ear saying, "What is your truth?"

Later, Rosa called her and said she had heard the same voice. After discussing, the two girls decided to meet in Driftveil City, and now the frequency of this voice was increasing.

Could it be that legendary tale about nation-building? Hilda's mind was sharp, recalling the legendary stories she had heard.

"Hey? Hilda, look over there! That guy is so handsome and stylish!"

Suddenly, Hilda's thoughts were interrupted, and Rosa was nudging her arm, excitedly whispering.

Hilda's lips twitched again. No, that's impossible. If that legend were true, she shouldn't be looking at this silly person unless that dragon god was blind.

But Hilda still looked in the direction Rosa was pointing; after all, she was a girl.

What caught her eye was a boy in black clothes and pants, with a clean aura. From the side, he indeed looked handsome.

"Right? He's my type! And he should be a trainer too."

"Then go ask for his contact information."

Hilda felt a bit speechless but knew her sister was a master at talking big; she had probably said this countless times already.

"I…"

Rosa was about to say something but seemed to notice that the boy in black had turned his head toward them just then.

Rosa immediately turned her head away and pretended to look elsewhere.

Hilda rolled her eyes, then made eye contact with the boy, who smiled and nodded friendly. Hilda returned a polite smile and turned her head away.

"How is it? What's his front profile like?" Rosa immediately leaned over to ask softly.

"He's very handsome. So?" Hilda was genuinely amused. So you can't even look at the front profile after he turned? And you're still nagging me here.

Dylan looked at the two girls whispering and chuckled, but just then, a vague androgynous voice came into his ear.

"Your truth… what is your truth?"

Dylan was taken aback; just now, he was thinking about whether he should speed up because, judging from Rosa and Hilda's conversation, there was no doubt that Reshiram should be selecting its true hero.

As the protagonists of the original story, it wasn't surprising that Hilda and Rosa were chosen by the white dragon, as they certainly had that talent.

But now… the white dragon was looking for him?

Wait, is this hero being selected randomly?

Dylan was a bit shocked; he had just arrived in the Unova region this afternoon. Has Reshiram already noticed him? That's incredible!

Meanwhile, in the northwest of the Unova region, in an ancient, weathered tower.

A white dragon stone glowing faintly red lay quietly at the base of the tower.

"This one is not bad, this will is decent, this person… forget it, let's change to another one."

"Sigh~ When will someone come to find me in the tower? It's too difficult to search for heroes remotely."

The ethereal, melodious mysterious voice echoed, seemingly quite frustrated.

"I'll have to transmit more phrases to those two human girls tonight. Among these dozens of people, these two girls should have the best potential."

"That black dragon has been out for so long and has found such a strong hero."

The mysterious voice was quite troubled. It didn't want to remain hidden; having awakened, how could it not want to leave this tower?

But the key was that it couldn't find a suitable hero!

Zekrom had found the strongest ideal hero throughout history, and its power was on par with Zekrom, meaning it could only compete based on the strength of both heroes.

It needed to find a strong real hero; otherwise, if it went out, wouldn't it be beaten by the black dragon and its hero?

No way, it could lose to anyone but the black dragon!

If it couldn't find a suitable hero, it would rather not go out and continue sleeping. After all, as long as it doesn't come into the world, the fight with the black dragon hasn't even begun.

Sigh~ Let's continue searching for some humans with good qualifications, hoping to find a stronger one to deal with that annoying black dragon and its hero.

The mysterious voice sent its consciousness out again to conduct its 'open selection.'

"This human… tsk~ full of h-colored aura, no saving, just wait to die. This one won't do either; how could someone be scared to death by a little Yorkie? Humans nowadays are too useless."

"Eh? This human… strange, why does the aura feel so unusual?"

The mysterious voice fell into deep thought. After a moment, it cleared its throat and strived to make its voice sound even more mysterious and ethereal, repeating what it had said dozens or even hundreds of times during this time.

"What is your truth… what is your truth?"

(End of chapter)

# 142: Chapter 143: WHO?!!!

"What is your truth…?"

Dylan, though puzzled as to why Reshiram found him so quickly, couldn't help but admire the white dragon's keen eye.

That's right, choosing me is the right decision! I'll take you to beat Zekrom and N! All you need to do is cheer the whole way, and we'll effortlessly claim victory in this 'Truth vs. Ideals' battle.

"My truth? Of course, it's… Hey? White Dragon, are you still there? Where'd you go?"

Dylan pondered seriously for a moment, deciding to use his truth to attract the true dragon god.

However, when he finally thought of some grand words to respond to the mysterious voice, he realized that Reshiram was completely gone, as if the 'voice of truth' he had just heard was nothing but an illusion.

Dylan fell into deep thought. Was this white dragon just messing around and leaving?

"Huh? Who's talking?"

Suddenly, Lillie's surprised voice echoed.

"What's wrong, Lillie?" Dylan asked, startled.

"Brother, did you hear a voice just now?"

Lillie looked around with a puzzled expression, speaking softly.

"What did you hear?" Dylan's eye twitched, but he still held onto a sliver of hope.

"It was something like 'your truth,' I think. I was just checking the route to Icirrus City and didn't listen closely."

Lillie tilted her head in confusion, not noticing her brother's expression had turned blank.

Well done, White Dragon!

Lillie is just a child! She's not even a Trainer, and you're trying to mess with her?

Dylan began to understand that something was off with this truthful white dragon, though he didn't know exactly why.

After finishing their meal, Dylan and Lillie paid the bill and left the restaurant, heading back to their hotel.

"Hey, handsome!"

However, they hadn't walked far before a cheerful voice called out from behind.

Turning around, Dylan saw Rosa and Hilda, with Rosa energetically waving at him and Lillie.

"Hello, may I help you?"

Dylan smiled politely and nodded at the two girls, then curiously asked.

Although he was quite interested in getting to know the protagonists from the Black and White series, he didn't want to force an encounter. But now that the two had approached him, it seemed rather strange.

"Hi~ I'm Rosa from Aspertia City. I just started my journey this year, and my goal is to become the Unova Champion!"

Rosa was lively and cheerful, introducing herself with a bright smile.

"Hello, Mr. Dylan, and this lovely young lady. I'm Hilda from Nuvema Town, also a Trainer who started my journey this year."

Compared to Rosa, Hilda was more composed and spoke politely to Dylan and Lillie.

"So, you've recognized me? Has my reputation already spread to Unova?"

Hearing Rosa and Hilda's introductions, Dylan understood that they must have found out about his status as a Gym Leader.

But this was odd. Although the Rota Gym was quite famous in Kanto, it shouldn't be known all the way in Unova.

Let alone being recognized by someone on the street.

"Hehe~ Someone actually posted about you online, Mr. Dylan," Rosa said mischievously, her laugh sounding adorable.

"?" Dylan and Lillie were taken aback.

"It's like this, we saw a post on the forum while we were eating. It was titled 'Chance Encounter with a Wild Rota Gym Leader, Dylan.'"

Hilda blinked and took out her phone, explaining to Dylan.

The post included an official photo of Dylan and some basic information about his preferred battle styles and Pokémon types.

Hilda immediately recognized the young man dressed in black sitting at the table behind them as Dylan. With his handsome features and signature black clothing, he was quite recognizable.

So, she and Rosa decided that it would be a missed opportunity not to say hello, especially since encountering a Gym Leader from Kanto in Unova was quite a rare occurrence!

"???"

Dylan glanced at the phone, unable to resist twitching his mouth.

The challengers in Kanto were getting more and more outrageous! He had just been out for a few days, and they had already posted a 'missing person notice' in another region's forum?

"This… Brother?" Lillie was shocked but also found it amusing.

It didn't really matter; if anything, it was increasing Dylan's fame in a different way.

"No problem. So, what brings you two here?"

Dylan smiled at Lillie, then turned to Rosa and Hilda.

"Of course, we want to challenge you! Since you're here in Unova, don't you want to experience the authentic Unova challengers?"

"Hehe~ Let us show you the difference between Trainers from Unova and Kanto," Rosa said, her expression becoming both bold and determined.

There was no way they could let such a rare encounter with a Kanto Gym Leader go to waste! They had to seize this chance.

"Oh?" Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"We've never left Unova before, so we're curious about Gym Leaders from other regions. Please give us a chance to learn from you, Mr. Dylan."

Compared to the straightforward Rosa, Hilda was more mature and articulate.

"It's not impossible, but we just arrived in Unova today. What do you think, Lillie? Or would you prefer to rest?"

Dylan didn't immediately agree, instead turning to Lillie for her opinion. After all, he wasn't traveling alone and needed to consider his sister's feelings.

"It's fine. I'd love to see how strong the Trainers in Unova are." Lillie's eyes sparkled as she flashed a playful but reserved smile.

Even though she was a bit tired, the thought of seeing Unova's challengers battle her brother was too tempting to pass up!

"Alright then, let's find an indoor battle arena."

Dylan agreed, smiling warmly at Hilda and Rosa.

This Gym Leader is so gentle!

Both Rosa and Hilda had the same thought. Dylan's attentiveness to his companion showed he was a kind and considerate person.

Who knows how strong he is, but he must be good if the people in Kanto are posting about him.

With Rosa and Hilda leading the way, the four headed to an indoor battle arena, as it was already late and outdoor battles weren't convenient.

"So, who's up first, ladies? I should warn you, my badge won't be easy to earn."

Dylan turned around to look at the two youthful and charming girls across from him, smiling as he spoke slowly.

"Ha? I just love challenging Gyms with a bit of difficulty! Hilda, step aside. I'll go first and set an example for you!"

Rosa stretched her limbs, then forcefully pushed her best friend out of the challenger's area, grinning widely.

"You better not mess this up," Hilda said, twitching her lips but still teasing her friend.

"Huh? Mess up? What nonsense are you talking about, Hilda?"

Rosa pouted. It was just a Gym challenge—how hard could it be? Even if it was a Gym Leader from Kanto, there shouldn't be any issue!

Could it be that the Gyms in Kanto are somehow more prestigious than those in Unova?

"Gym Leader Dylan, let me remind you not to underestimate us. You'd better give it your all, or I won't hold back!"

Rosa gripped a Poké Ball tightly, her smile radiant and her tone full of confidence.

"I see. I'll keep that in mind," Dylan raised an eyebrow and then held up four fingers on his left hand toward Rosa.

"?" Rosa tilted her head in confusion. What did that mean?

"Actually, my Gym currently offers four different type-based teams for challengers to face, plus a special team. However, that special team isn't quite convenient to use without the rotation gimmick."

"So, Miss Rosa, which of these four types would you like to challenge?"

Dylan explained in a gentle voice.

Hearing this, both Rosa and Hilda were taken aback. But then they recalled that the Striaton Gym had three different Gym Leaders for different types, which was somewhat similar.

"Then I'll choose the third one." Rosa thought for a moment and opted for the third option.

"The third one is the Ghost type. You're in luck, Miss Rosa," Dylan chuckled softly.

"Ghost, huh… troublesome type." Rosa's delicate brows furrowed but soon relaxed as she grew eager for the challenge.

It wasn't that troublesome; it was just a Gym battle, after all. She could easily win this!

"Now, we'll begin the Gym battle for the Rota Badge. The match will be between Gym Leader Dylan and Miss Rosa."

"The battle rules are 3 vs. 3. Gym Leader Dylan will send out his Pokémon first, and he cannot switch them out. The first to have all three Pokémon unable to battle will lose."

"Let the battle begin!"

Lillie raised her hand to announce the battle rules. Though her voice wasn't loud, it was clear enough for both Dylan and Rosa to hear.

"Now that the battle is starting, Miss Rosa, allow me to formally introduce myself."

Dylan gracefully adjusted the collar of his shirt, a habit of his before every match. His unhurried and confident demeanor was captivating.

"My name is Dylan, the Gym Leader of Rota Gym, and I'm a multi-type Trainer."

"Today, I'll be using Ghost-type Pokémon to battle you. Are you familiar with Ghost types?"

Dylan took out a Poké Ball, his smile warm yet with a hint of mystery.

"Their battle techniques are unpredictable and varied. Even without overwhelming power, they can toy with their opponents effortlessly."

Rosa scrunched her pretty face. His lines made him sound so impressive...

It seemed like she'd need to get a bit more serious!

"Please experience it for yourself, Miss Rosa. Here's my first Pokémon."

Dylan casually tossed the Poké Ball in his hand.

"Mismagius, prepare for battle!"

In a flash of white light, a purple Pokémon floated in front of Dylan, its golden eyes gazing eerily at Rosa.

It looked as if it was wearing a hat resembling a wizard's and had a slender body draped in what appeared to be a cloak, with three red gems embedded in it.

It was the evolved form of Misdreavus, Mismagius!

'Beep~ Mismagius, Ghost type, Magical Pokémon.

Ability: Levitate

Moves: Perish Song, Shadow Ball, Mean Look, Pain Split, Protect, Hex, Will-O-Wisp, Curse, Destiny Bond…

Description: Feared for its mischief and ability to spread curses, Mismagius also whimsically uses runes to help humans.'

Reading the Pokédex entry, Hilda's pupils dilated slightly. Were those really Mismagius's usual moves??

Rosa!!!

(End of the chapter)

---
