# 143: Chapter 144: How strange

"It seems like Rosa has her work cut out for her," Lillie said, gazing at the quiet and rather shy-looking Mismagius.

"Lillie, are you and Dylan a couple?" Hilda asked curiously, as the two seemed very close. Plus, traveling all the way from Kanto to Unova together wasn't something normal friends would usually do.

"Huh? No, no..." Lillie was taken aback by the question, and her fair face immediately turned bright red. She shook her head vigorously, causing her golden ponytail to almost hit Hilda in the face.

"My brother is just my brother, not my boyfriend. I'm...I'm his step-sister, not his real sister...wait..." Lillie's speech became hurried and flustered, and she seemed unsure of what she was even saying.

"Alright, alright, I get it. Lillie, calm down. There's no need to be embarrassed..." Hilda said, trying to comfort the red-faced girl, worried that Lillie might overheat if she blushed any more.

"Ugh...I'm really not dating my brother," Lillie whimpered, covering her face with her hands. Oh no, my brother's reputation...

Meanwhile, on the battlefield...

"A pure Ghost-type Mismagius, huh?" Rosa tilted her head slightly. Being a pure Ghost-type Pokémon, it was only weak to Ghost and Dark-type moves.

"In that case, you're up! Bisharp!" Rosa quickly made her decision and boldly threw her first Poké Ball.

A flash of white light revealed a humanoid Pokémon landing on the ground and slowly straightening up, its cold gaze locking onto Mismagius.

The Pokémon resembled a stern warrior, with blades on its head, limbs, and abdomen gleaming coldly, exuding an aloof aura.

This was Bisharp, which had evolved from Pawniard!

'Beep~ Bisharp, Dark/Steel-type, the Sword Blade Pokémon.

Ability: Defiant.

Moves: Night Slash, Slash, Pursuit, Taunt, Swords Dance, Scary Face, Iron Head, Sucker Punch (Egg Move)…

Description: A terrifying Pokémon that can slice its prey into two with its blades after several Pawniard have weakened it.'

What a fierce-looking Pokémon!

Lillie, having recovered a little, looked at the Pokédex entry and thought, "This girl is really strong. She's just a rookie Trainer who started traveling this year."

"Using Bisharp against Mismagius? Not a bad choice," Dylan nodded. Bisharp's typing had its advantages, despite being four times weak to Fighting. It only had a few other weaknesses, including Ground and Fire.

However...that won't matter.

"Heh, my Bisharp is really strong!" Rosa said proudly. "A naturally wicked Ghost-type like Mismagius is no match for me!"

"Bisharp! Use Swords Dance!" Rosa commanded.

Bisharp raised its arms and stood straight like a sharp blade, radiating an imposing aura.

Swords Dance! It could significantly boost its Attack!

"Mean Look," Dylan said calmly, a faint smile on his face.

Mismagius's golden eyes gleamed with an eerie light, and a shadowy aura enveloped Bisharp's silhouette.

Under the effect of Mean Look, Bisharp could no longer be recalled.

"Mean Look? Really?" Rosa was surprised. What kind of strategy was this? Bisharp's attack power was incredibly high after using Swords Dance.

Moreover, Bisharp had Sucker Punch, a Dark-type priority move that was effective against Ghost-types!

Even if it was trapped by Mean Look, it didn't matter. As soon as Mismagius used an attacking move, Bisharp could strike first with Sucker Punch!

"Curse," Dylan continued, his tone as calm as ever.

"Bisharp, use Pursuit to stop it!" Rosa's face tightened. With Bisharp trapped on the battlefield, being hit by Curse would be disastrous.

She had to stop it! If she could defeat Mismagius, Mean Look would no longer have any effect.

Bisharp's arm blades shone with dark energy as it rushed toward Mismagius, but Mismagius moved away and ascended faster, dodging Bisharp's attempt to strike.

Seeing this, Rosa's expression darkened. The Curse was going to land for sure.

A sinister-looking nail appeared in front of Mismagius, emanating a foreboding aura.

Clink! Clink! Clink!

The Curse turned into a nail that slowly drove itself into Mismagius's body. Mismagius let out a pained yet delighted shriek, its golden eyes filled with pleasure.

The Curse!

Gray energy rapidly shot from Mismagius toward Bisharp, seeping into its body. The next moment, Bisharp bent over in pain, enduring the first wave of the Curse's effect.

"Bisharp!" Rosa shouted in alarm. This was bad! Being cursed now was not good at all.

"Pain Split."

But Dylan's offensive didn't stop there. Mismagius began to glow with white light.

"!!!" Rosa's pupils contracted. "No way, how could you do this?"

Just as the Curse's effects subsided, white light spread from Bisharp, flowing into Mismagius as Pain Split took hold.

Under Pain Split, Mismagius and Bisharp's health merged and was then divided evenly between them.

But clearly, this Pain Split was simply Mismagius leeching Bisharp's health.

Rosa gritted her teeth, but there was nothing she could do. Mismagius had the Levitate ability and was much faster than Bisharp.

If she didn't use Sucker Punch, Bisharp wouldn't be able to get close enough to attack Mismagius. So…

Is Mismagius going to attack now? Sucker Punch only worked if the opponent used an offensive move.

Rosa kept her eyes on Mismagius, holding back and waiting for it to launch an attack.

"No need to stare at Mismagius like that, Rosa. Your Bisharp will use Sucker Punch, right?" Dylan chuckled, addressing Rosa directly.

"..." Rosa was speechless, her mouth opening, but she didn't know how to respond.

"Don't worry. My Mismagius doesn't need to attack to defeat its opponent, so there's no point in waiting."

Rosa's face was full of question marks.

"Mismagius, use Taunt."

Mismagius let out a sharp, piercing laugh in the air, and mysterious waves began to emanate from it.

A Dark-type Taunt.

Visibly, Bisharp's previously cold gaze began to fill with anger, and its blade-like arms lifted, gleaming with a dark light.

"Wait! Bisharp, calm down!"

Rosa hurried to reassure Bisharp. Under the effect of Taunt, Pokémon could only use attack moves and not status-altering ones.

"Rosa is completely suppressed," Hilda, who was watching the battle, couldn't help but frown at the scene. "Is this Kanto Gym Leader really that strong?"

Rosa was no slouch, and her skills were formidable, but her ace, Bisharp, was being toyed with by Mismagius.

Not long after, the second wave of Curse hit, and Bisharp groaned in pain once again.

At this point, Bisharp was in bad shape, and Rosa knew she couldn't drag the battle on any longer.

"Bisharp, use Pursuit!"

Rosa gritted her teeth and ordered Bisharp to attack. If it didn't act soon, Bisharp would be done for.

Bisharp charged toward Mismagius, but it was no use. Bisharp couldn't catch up to Mismagius.

To make things worse, Mismagius even used Taunt again, spurring Bisharp to chase it more aggressively.

In the end, Bisharp eventually collapsed due to the Curse on its way to pursue Mismagius.

"Bisharp is unable to battle. Mismagius wins," Hilda announced the result and couldn't help but glance at Dylan's Mismagius.

Throughout the entire match, Bisharp hadn't even touched Mismagius, but Mismagius had cleanly defeated Bisharp.

And Mismagius hadn't even used a single offensive move! This…

"Well? Rosa, do you now appreciate the charm of Ghost-types?" Dylan said with a playful smile, looking at Rosa, whose face was tense.

Rosa's face darkened upon hearing this. This guy...he won and still had the nerve to taunt her! Charm? What charm could there possibly be in such an annoying battle style?

"Hmph! It's nothing special. I was just careless earlier."

Rosa stubbornly refused to admit defeat. These were just underhanded tricks; a truly strong Trainer wouldn't be afraid of them!

"Oh? So, Rosa, do you already have a strategy to deal with my Mismagius?" Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"Of course! Don't underestimate me!"

Rosa confidently puffed out her chest, her posture causing Lillie and Hilda to both glance away slightly, surprised by her boldness.

"Then I look forward to it," Dylan smiled, gesturing for her to continue.

"Go, Reuniclus!"

Rosa once again dramatically threw out her second Poké Ball. These annoying tactics? They were meaningless in front of her Reuniclus!

A flash of white light revealed a green Pokémon floating before Rosa.

Its body was light green, entirely encased in a jelly-like membrane, giving it a rather cute appearance.

This was Reuniclus, the final evolution of Solosis.

'Beep~ Reuniclus, Psychic-type, Amplification Pokémon.

Ability: Magic Guard.

Moves: Hammer Arm, Psychic, Pain Split, Reflect, Light Screen, Protect, Recover, Shadow Ball...

Description: Using its psychic power, it manipulates its liquid arms to smash rocks.'

So it had Magic Guard, huh? No wonder Rosa chose a Pokémon with a disadvantageous typing.

Lillie looked at Reuniclus's information and understood Rosa's plan.

"It must be because of its Magic Guard ability," Dylan nodded. He didn't need to look at any data to know what Rosa was thinking.

Magic Guard: The Pokémon is immune to all damage except direct attacks.

That meant Reuniclus was completely immune to secondary effects like Poison or Curse.

"That's right! It can perfectly counter your evil Curse!" Rosa said proudly, lifting her chin.

Without Curse and with Reuniclus being adept at special attacks, along with its floating ability, it wouldn't have trouble dealing with Mismagius. It was the perfect counter!

She was sure she would win this time!

Why was she calling Curse evil again?

Dylan found Rosa's attitude amusing. This girl really was quite cute.

"Alright then, let's see what you've got."

Meanwhile, unbeknownst to the four Trainers, a mysterious consciousness was also watching them.

"These four seem to be among the candidates... Once the result is decided, I'll guide one of them to come closer to the tower for observation."

The mysterious entity pondered. It had no intention of casting a wide net; it was more interested in these three—two girls with exceptional willpower and potential, and the peculiar boy.

How strange... Why couldn't it perceive the boy's inner self? It was as if something was obscuring him from view.

No rush. If he could defeat that girl, it would invite him to the tower first. Observing from a distance wasn't as effective, but if he came close, it could gain a clearer understanding.

What the entity didn't know was that its lack of malice had spared it from being torn apart by Necrozma's psychic powers.

(End of Chapter)

Merry Christmas!!!

# 144: Chapter 145: Being a bit too cryptic

"The Poison-type Gym gained a lot of fame, and then they introduced the Dark-type. Along with the 'Golden Legend' mechanism in three different categories, this became known as the 'Vicious' Gym, receiving widespread acclaim."

"Afterward, they introduced the Ghost-type, which earned the name 'Vicious Ghost Gym.' Recently, they added Dragon-type Pokémon, and now the gym is considered the 'Vicious Dragon Ghost Gym.' It's one of the must-challenge gyms for any adventurer traveling through Kanto."

Hilda returned to the spectator seats and immediately jumped online to check the official Kanto website for information on the Rota Gym.

And she was shocked!

Every single title for the gym was anything but normal, yet it received widespread praise?

Is there something wrong with the mental health of the trainers in Kanto?

On the battlefield.

Dylan and Rosa's battle was still ongoing, though thanks to the Magic Guard ability, Curse was rendered useless, but it wasn't a big deal.

Perish Song began its preparation. Dark, ominous notes sank into Mismagius and Reuniclus.

The countdown to doom had started!

Rosa's face was tense, but it didn't matter. As long as she defeated Mismagius and recalled Reuniclus, she'd be fine.

In the next moment, black shadows enveloped Reuniclus. Reuniclus could no longer be recalled normally.

At the same time, Reuniclus's Shadow Ball hit Mismagius. However, Mismagius's special defense held up well enough that the attack was still manageable.

Black musical notes began to appear on Mismagius and Reuniclus's bodies, a sign that the effects of the Perish Song were taking hold.

Rosa commanded Reuniclus to launch a fierce assault. Under Reuniclus's barrage, Mismagius's condition began to deteriorate rapidly.

But time was running out, and the Perish Song's markings on Reuniclus deepened.

"Rosa's fallen into the trap," Hilda sighed from the spectator's stand. The more Rosa rushed to defeat Mismagius, the closer she moved towards her own defeat.

Having seen Mismagius's moves before, Hilda knew very well that Mismagius had a trump card.

A calm and collected Rosa would have realized this too, but there was no time to stay calm. The Perish Song's timer was ticking, and Reuniclus didn't have much time left.

"Reuniclus, Shadow Ball!" Rosa commanded decisively.

A pitch-black Shadow Ball shot from Reuniclus toward Mismagius, hitting its target directly.

Mismagius didn't dodge and took the full hit, collapsing to the ground unconscious.

"Come back, Reuniclus...?"

Rosa breathed a sigh of relief and hurried to recall Reuniclus, but before she could finish speaking, a dark, eerie energy erupted from Mismagius's body, sinking into Reuniclus.

Rosa's eyes widened. This... was Destiny Bond?

Reuniclus let out a pained cry before falling to the ground, knocked out and unable to continue battling.

It was indeed Destiny Bond!

Rosa gritted her teeth, glaring at her defeated Reuniclus. "Argh! I just managed to take down Mismagius, and I don't even get to enjoy the victory."

"It seems you were too focused on the Perish Song," Dylan said, shaking his head with a wise yet gentle tone.

Rosa's eye twitched, glaring at Dylan through gritted teeth.

This cunning and devious man! As if Perish Song and Mean Look weren't enough, he had Destiny Bond prepared as well? What kind of gym leader does that?

"Dylan, I think it's a shame you're just a gym leader. You're too talented for that," Rosa said, a forced smile on her face.

"One must find contentment in life. I actually quite enjoy being a gym leader," Dylan replied with a polite smile.

Rosa: (Cue the "confused guy" meme)

Enjoy? How could he enjoy this? His joy comes from causing others pain! Challenging his gym probably takes years off someone's life!

I worked so hard to defeat Mismagius, and then he just takes me out with Destiny Bond. Who can accept that?

"Serperior!"

Unable to hold back any longer, Rosa called out her strongest Pokémon, her starter.

With a flash of white light, a regal and elegant Serperior raised its upper body, its gem-like eyes fixed on Dylan.

This was Serperior, the final evolution of Snivy, one of the Unova region's starter Pokémon.

"Wow, what a beautiful Pokémon!" Lillie's eyes lit up as she immediately pulled out her Pokédex to scan Serperior.

'Beep~ Serperior, the Regal Pokémon. Grass-type.

Ability: Overgrow Moves: Frenzy Plant, Giga Drain, Leaf Blade, Slam, Mega Drain, Leech Seed, Magical Leaf, Synthesis (Egg Move)...

Description: Serperior only fights seriously against strong opponents who show no fear despite its noble gaze.'

It looked strong!

"This Serperior is Rosa's starter Pokémon and her strongest partner," Hilda whispered.

"So graceful and elegant, it's absolutely stunning," Lillie was completely mesmerized by Serperior's regal appearance. It was just so beautiful!

Comparing it to the Decidueye from the Alola region or Kanto's Venusaur, there was no competition—Serperior was in a league of its own.

"A well-raised Serperior," Dylan commented after sizing it up. He couldn't help but nod in approval.

As expected of Rosa, the protagonist of "Black and White 2." Her ability to raise Pokémon was exceptional. This Serperior was in peak condition and quite powerful.

"If this is your ace, then I'll prepare a strong opponent as well," Dylan said, still maintaining his polite demeanor as he threw out another Poké Ball.

"Annihilape, get ready to battle!"

With a low, menacing growl, a wild-looking Pokémon with tangled white fur appeared in front of Dylan. Its blood-red eyes locked onto Serperior, its gray and black body exuding a violent, primal energy. White fur twisted around its head like a mane, and dark cuffs wrapped around its wrists and ankles, giving it a feral appearance.

This was Annihilape, the evolved form of Primeape that Dylan had recently captured.

'Beep~ Annihilape, Fighting/Ghost-type. The Rage Monkey Pokémon.

Ability: Defiant.

Moves: Earthquake, Close Combat, Rage Fist, Outrage, Pursuit, Cross Chop, Swagger, Shadow Punch, Shadow Claw...

Description: It condenses its deep-seated rage into its fists, its power reaching deep into an opponent's bones, crushing them.'

This Annihilape was strong!

Hilda's eyes flickered with surprise as she read through Annihilape's data. Unlike Mismagius, Annihilape represented the opposite extreme—it was a powerhouse of pure offense!

"A Fighting and Ghost-type Annihilape?" Rosa's expression grew serious. The oppressive aura from Annihilape was undeniable.

This Annihilape was incredibly powerful! But...

"Serperior! Charge in with Leaf Blade!"

Without hesitation, Rosa commanded an aggressive attack.

Serperior let out a cool, elegant cry as its green form shot toward Annihilape. Despite being a serpent, its slithering movements were incredibly fast.

"Let it come," Dylan smiled faintly.

Annihilape tilted its head slightly, watching the green serpent rush toward it.

Serperior closed in, raising its tail high, which glowed with the sharp green light of Leaf Blade, before slashing down toward Annihilape.

A Grass-type Leaf Blade!

"Cross Chop to block it!"

Annihilape raised its arms, crossing them to perform a Fighting-type Cross Chop.

Bang!

Leaf Blade slammed heavily against Annihilape's crossed arms, the impact strong enough to crack the ground beneath it, but...

That was all it did!

"What?" Rosa's eyes widened. Her Serperior's STAB Leaf Blade was just blocked like that?

Even though Annihilape had taken some damage, it was minimal. Instead, the bloodlust in its eyes deepened.

Rage +1!

"Grab it," Dylan ordered.

The Annihilape swiftly grabbed Serperior's tail with a reverse hand motion, holding it tightly in its grasp.

"Serperior! Wrap around it!" Rosa reacted quickly, immediately giving the command to Serperior.

Serperior lunged forward, its snake-like body coiling several times around the Annihilape, tightly binding it and squeezing with force.

It was like the legendary constriction of a python!

The Annihilape let out an angry growl, its eyes flashing with a blood-red gleam as it struggled to break free from Serperior's bind, but Serperior resisted with all its strength.

Rage x2!

However, there was a difference in strength between the two. Serperior's constriction was gradually being broken apart by the Annihilape, and Serperior could no longer maintain its hold.

"Serperior! Use Giga Drain!" Rosa immediately commanded.

A deep green light shone from Serperior, and small green particles began to be drawn from the Annihilape into Serperior's body.

The Grass-type move, Giga Drain!

The Annihilape roared furiously, exerting great force to break free from Serperior's bind. It then grabbed Serperior with its right hand and started spinning it wildly.

Rage x3!

Under the intense dizziness, Serperior had no way to resist. Its entire body was being swung around by the Annihilape like a windmill.

"Serperior!" Rosa's expression changed.

"Annihilape, Rage Fist," Dylan's lips curled into a smile.

With a low growl, the Annihilape gathered its strength and slammed Serperior into the ground.

Boom!

Serperior was smashed heavily into the ground, pain surging through its body, causing it to tightly shut its eyes and whimper.

Even though this wasn't a special move, the impact of being slammed to the ground was excruciating.

But it wasn't over yet. The Annihilape clenched its left fist and raised it, a massive amount of eerie blue energy forming a field around it and surging into its body.

A Ghost-type Rage Fist! This was the Annihilape's signature move!

In terms of game stats, this was a move that grew stronger the more the Annihilape was attacked. Its base power started at 50, but with each hit the Annihilape took, the power of Rage Fist increased by 50 points.

Since Serperior had just attacked the Annihilape three times, Rage Fist's power had increased by 150 points, making the total power 200 points!

This was even more terrifying than moves like Hyper Beam, Giga Impact, or Frenzy Plant.

"Wait! Serperior! Use Protect!"

As the Annihilape's aura grew more intense, Rosa urgently called out to Serperior.

But it was clear that the still-reeling Serperior had no time to use Protect.

With a low, violent growl, the Annihilape unleashed a powerful punch, and a massive ghostly blue shockwave erupted.

Rage Fist!

Boom!

The shockwave from Rage Fist struck Serperior squarely, creating an explosive sound that echoed loudly. The fierce blast of energy surged like a storm.

"This…"

Rosa struggled to shield herself with her arm as her ponytail whipped in the wind. Her heart sank—such power…

As the dust and wind settled, Serperior lay collapsed in a large crater, though it hadn't completely lost its fighting ability. However, it was close to being knocked out.

Serperior had great defense, so Dylan wasn't surprised by this.

"Stomp."

With one final stomp from the Annihilape, Serperior fainted.

Rosa bit her lip, looking at her fainted Serperior, her eyes filled with confusion. Had she… lost?

She had lost to a wild Gym Leader she'd happened to meet, and the loss had been so decisive. How could this be…

The girl named Rosa lowered her head, spiraling into self-doubt.

"Serperior is unable to battle. Annihilape wins. Since all three of Rosa's Pokémon have been defeated, the winner of this battle is Gym Leader Dylan."

Hilda announced the result of the battle, then walked over to the dazed Rosa.

"Well done," Dylan walked over to the Annihilape and patted its furry head in praise.

The Annihilape tilted its head slightly, its blood-red eyes giving no hint of its current mood.

"How is it so strong… It doesn't make sense. Am I too weak?"

"Rosa, are you okay?"

Hilda's lips curled slightly at Rosa's confused muttering, but she quickly straightened her expression, pretending to be concerned as she spoke to Rosa.

"Rosa??"

Seeing no response, Hilda grabbed Rosa's shoulders and shook her.

"Hilda? Hilda! Hilda!!"

Rosa finally snapped out of it, her eyes slowly brightening before she suddenly burst into tears and threw herself into Hilda's arms.

"Oh no! Hilda! That guy's such a bully!" Rosa wailed about her unfortunate encounter.

"There, there. It's okay." Hilda calmly patted her head, clearly used to this situation.

"Then Hilda, go get revenge for me!"

"Huh?"

"You can definitely beat him, right? Aren't you going to finish what I started?" Rosa asked in confusion. Wasn't the plan for her to battle first, and then Hilda would take over?

"Rosa, why are you always so obsessed with fighting? Gym Leader Dylan and Miss Lillie just arrived in Unova today. They haven't even had a chance to rest, and now you want to bother them with another battle?"

Hilda quickly distanced herself from Rosa and coughed, taking on a serious tone as she lectured her.

"Huh?" Rosa looked puzzled.

"What 'huh'? It's late, let's head back and get some sleep."

Hilda said seriously. Challenge Dylan? No, no, Hilda didn't think today was the right day.

Just as Dylan was about to approach the two of them, that mysterious, ethereal voice echoed in his ears once more.

"The truth… Your truth… Come… to Dragonspiral Tower…"

Dylan paused, his expression becoming slightly odd. Why did it feel like this White Dragon was being a bit too cryptic?

Merry Christmas!!!

# 145: Chapter 146: The World is the World

"At some point, come to Kanto and try out the spinning attraction at my gym. It's different from the ones outside."

At the entrance, Dylan smiled and said goodbye to the two girls.

"Sure, if we get the chance, we'll definitely give it a try."

Hilda responded politely. Truth be told, she was genuinely intrigued by the spinning attraction at the Rota Gym.

It randomly selected an attribute, and Dylan would use whatever type was chosen. Pretty interesting, right?

Meanwhile, the girl with the bun hairstyle beside Hilda was glaring at Dylan.

Stare...

"Rosa, no need to stare at me like that. If fate allows, next time we battle, I won't use a Ghost-type team against you."

Dylan chuckled. Rosa really was an adorably stubborn one.

"Tch! You talk like I was scared of them! They're just inherently evil Ghost-types," Rosa pouted with disdain, but then quickly changed her tone.

"Are you serious though? A real man keeps his word!"

"Of course. An unbreakable promise, as solid as a four-horse chariot," Dylan said with a faint smile.

"Yay~! Ahem, then next time, you must witness my true strength!" Rosa cheered excitedly but quickly regained her composure, coughing and addressing Dylan seriously.

"Alright, see you next time."

Dylan smiled, waved goodbye to the two girls, and left with Lillie.

"He's quite the impressive person~"

Watching Dylan and Lillie's figures disappear from view, Hilda couldn't help but sigh.

"Tch~ Next time, I'll definitely defeat him! No way am I leaving without a gym badge," Rosa huffed indignantly.

"And you, Hilda! You were terrible! You gave up without even trying—I'm so disappointed in you!"

"Oh, go on! Do you think I'm as brainless as you are with that big chest of yours? Challenges require caution."

Hilda rolled her eyes.

"Hey?! Did you just insult me? You totally insulted me!"

"No, I was complimenting your figure," Hilda said nonchalantly, changing the subject.

"After we finish challenging all the gyms in Unova, what do you say we take a trip to Kanto?"

"Yes! I've been dying to see Kanto!" Rosa's eyes lit up, easily distracted.

"Then let's pick up the pace. I only need two more badges to qualify for the Vertress Conference."

"Hehe~ What a coincidence, bestie! I need just two more as well. Once the Vertress Conference begins, I'll personally make sure you're knocked out! You can sit in the audience and watch me take on the Elite Four and Champion Alder."

"Hahaha~ Thanks a lot?"

The two girls continued chatting as they walked away, the epitome of rivals and close friends.

…

The next day, Dylan and Lillie woke up early and flew to Icirrus City.

Icirrus City is located in the northwest of the Unova region, where the seasons change drastically. During the winter, thick snow blankets the city, transforming it into a white wonderland.

Of course, Dylan didn't come to Icirrus City for sightseeing, especially since it wasn't winter.

He was here because this city is the closest to the Dragonspiral Tower.

After leaving Icirrus City, Dylan and Lillie flew north on Articuno. Before long, they spotted the ancient tower.

"We've arrived."

Articuno landed as Dylan gazed at the ancient tower ahead, his eyes flickering with determination.

"Is this the place, big brother? The place where Reshiram slumbers?" Lillie curiously looked around.

"Yes, this is it." Dylan seemed to hear the call even more clearly now.

'Come… I've been waiting for you…'

This white dragon would make a great fortune-teller.

"Let's go, Lillie. We're heading inside."

Dylan and Lillie crossed the bridge to the entrance of Dragonspiral Tower and went inside.

Dragonspiral Tower is the oldest tower in the Unova region. No one knows who built it, but it's the most ancient structure in the region.

The interior of the tower was filled with the weight of centuries, with collapsed pillars visible everywhere. Dylan paid little attention to this as he and Lillie made their way toward the top.

"Lillie, do you know the legend of Unova's founding?" Dylan asked, perhaps to lighten the quiet atmosphere.

"Um~ Does big brother know?" Lillie thought for a moment. She had read up on some of it, but her knowledge wasn't complete.

But that didn't matter. Her brother was knowledgeable and well-versed in the myths and legends of every region.

"Zekrom and Reshiram, the 'Legendary Dragon Pokémon,' were once one being," Dylan began.

"According to legend, around 2,500 years ago, Unova was founded by twin brothers and a single dragon. The older brother sought truth, while the younger brother pursued ideals."

"As their goals diverged, so did their relationship, eventually leading to war. The dragon split into Reshiram, representing truth, and Zekrom, representing ideals."

Hearing this, Lillie couldn't help but feel sorrow. Differences in ideals had led to such a tragic outcome.

"But the war couldn't be won. Neither Reshiram nor Zekrom could defeat the other, and both exhausted themselves. The twin brothers, realizing their mistake, ended the conflict and restored peace."

"However, after the brothers passed away, their sons reignited the war. Enraged, Reshiram and Zekrom burned the country with fire and lightning, destroying their physical forms and turning into the Light Stone and the Dark Stone."

By this time, Dylan and Lillie had reached the top of the tower. Dylan looked around and continued.

"And according to legend, the Light Stone and Dark Stone rested here after that war. N awoke the black dragon, Zekrom, from this very place and took it with him."

Dylan gently ran his hand along the tower's ancient walls, feeling the dust of time and the marks of history.

Yes, N had awakened Zekrom here and taken it away. And today...

It was Dylan's turn!

"Truth~ What is your truth?"

A voice, neither male nor female, echoed clearly in Dylan's mind as the gaze of the truth dragon, Reshiram, focused on him.

"My truth?"

Dylan chuckled softly.

"Reshiram, don't you think you should come and experience it for yourself?"

Reshiram?

Lillie blinked in confusion, glancing around. Where was the white dragon? Why hadn't she heard it?

"The world is the world. This world, as it stands now, is undeniably real. I've always been able to clearly feel its existence. Its weight... I can carry it! Reshiram~"

"The world is the world. This world, as it stands now, is undeniably real. I've always been able to clearly feel its existence. Its weight... I can carry it! Reshiram~"

Dylan's eyes scanned the surroundings. He didn't know exactly where Reshiram was at the moment, but there was no doubt Reshiram was here, somewhere in the tower.

"You brought me here, but can't you sense this? N's ideals, the things he pursues, are easily shattered."

Dylan's voice echoed through the Dragonspiral Tower.

"Come with me, Reshiram. We'll be the best partners. I'll lead you to defeat the Black Dragon and its hero."

"We can show them clearly that their ideals can't surpass our reality!"

The tower remained silent. Besides the sound of Dylan's and Lillie's breathing, nothing stirred.

One minute, two minutes, three minutes...

"Big brother..."

Still no response. Lillie tightly pressed her lips together, her eyes filled with concern as she gazed at Dylan. It seemed like... he hadn't awakened the White Dragon. This... the White Dragon had no taste!

"Did I fail?"

Dylan furrowed his brow slightly. If he couldn't earn the White Dragon's favor, then so be it. Time for Plan B.

"Necrozma, can you sense Reshiram's presence?"

"I can."

A deep, raspy telepathic voice echoed in Dylan's mind—it was Necrozma.

"Alright then, if it doesn't want to do this the easy way, we'll help it."

Dylan smiled faintly. Initially, he'd planned to take Reshiram in a gentler manner, but if Reshiram was unwilling, it would just have to bear a bit of pain.

"Necro..."

Dylan took out the shrunken Ultra Ball hanging from his chest, preparing to let Necrozma bring Reshiram out.

Suddenly, the entire Dragonspiral Tower began to shake violently.

"Big brother?"

"Let's get out of here."

Lillie quickly grabbed onto Dylan's arm to keep herself from falling—the tremors were intense.

"Necrozma."

Dylan opened the Ultra Ball towards the nearby window, and the deep-blue Necrozma appeared, tearing through the wall with a single claw. It then used its psychic powers to lift Dylan and Lillie out of the tower.

"What's going on?"

Once they were on the ground, Dylan looked across the lake at the violently shaking tower. This was strange.

"It's beginning to awaken."

The deep telepathic voice came again as the colorful parts on Necrozma's head began to flash.

"Awaken?" Dylan raised an eyebrow upon hearing this. Could it be that things weren't going as he had thought?

The tower continued to tremble, causing a large amount of dust to fall, making it seem as though the ancient structure was on the verge of collapse.

Amid the dust, a bright red Dragon energy shot straight up from the base of the tower, coming to rest above its peak.

"The Light Stone."

Dylan stared at the radiant Dragon Stone. This was the form Reshiram had taken after its body was destroyed long ago.

But now, its dragon body was beginning to reassemble!

"Roar~!"

A clear and melodious dragon cry echoed through the sky as the Light Stone expanded and transformed in flames.

In the blink of an eye, a magnificent white dragon spread its wings and soared high.

"This is... the legendary dragon god of truth, Reshiram!"

Lillie stood in awe, staring at the snow-white dragon. My goodness, it was... so beautiful!

Its overall appearance was white, a fusion of a white bird and a large, winged western dragon. Its brilliant blue eyes were stunning, with long, flowing fur like clouds trailing behind its head.

The entire body was covered in soft, feather-like white fur. Its face resembled that of a white wolf, with massive white wing claws extending from its sides—holy and divine!

If the Black Dragon Zekrom was imposing and cool, then the White Dragon Reshiram was elegant, noble, and grand.

Lillie had imagined what the White Dragon might look like, but she never thought that the legendary Reshiram would be this breathtaking!

This was a Dragon-type legend, the dragon god of truth that had once burned kingdoms to ash with its flames!

Reshiram's cry rang out once more. Its dragon cry was different from the fierce, rough cries of other Dragon Pokémon—it was much more graceful and delicate.

Reshiram's brilliant blue eyes turned toward Dylan on the ground, and with a single flap of its white wings, it was hovering above Dylan and Lillie in an instant.

At that moment, Reshiram's gaze was fixed solely on Dylan.

Thud~!

The White Dragon landed in front of Dylan.

"I have sensed your truth~"

A soft, androgynous telepathic voice rang out as the legendary White Dragon gradually approached the boy in front of it.

"Dylan~ I will ignite the path you build toward the real world, and I will... give it my all!"

Reshiram stood in front of Dylan, lowering its head slightly, its brilliant blue eyes reflecting the image of the young man before it.

One person and one dragon stared at each other, standing on the green grass, beside the blue lake, with the ancient Dragonspiral Tower in the background, forming a scene of perfect harmony.

Lillie covered her mouth, her eyes brimming with excitement as she gazed at the pair. She had known it all along!

She had known that her brother wouldn't be rejected by the White Dragon!

Her brother had successfully awakened the true Reshiram! The world would change because of this day!

---

---

---

# 146: Chapter 147: Do you even know who I am?

We Need to Find a Rival for Reshiram! Brother, You're So Sneaky

In an unknown forest, a green-haired young man named N, wearing a cap, smiled gently as he fed a Pansage.

How peaceful...

N gazed at the smiling Pansage with its squinted eyes, feeling healed from within.

Since their previous attempt to negotiate with Team Rocket in Kanto was disrupted by the Kanto League, Zekrom had taken N and Colress back to Unova.

Ghetsis was quite displeased with this turn of events but did not blame N too much. After all, it was Team Rocket's fault that the meeting location was discovered by the Kanto League. N was free to act after that.

"N." A deep voice echoed in N's mind.

"What is it, Zekrom?"

N was puzzled. Zekrom usually only contacted him in special situations.

"The White Dragon has awakened. I can feel it," Zekrom said concisely.

N's eyes flickered at the news. The Real White Dragon... has it finally awakened? He had been waiting for this moment for quite some time.

Idealism and truth were completely different ideologies. Zekrom would aid the Hero of Ideals to create an ideal world, while Reshiram would support the Hero of Truth in constructing a world of truth.

From the moment of their creation, the Dragon of Ideals and the Dragon of Truth have been in conflict, and so it has been throughout history.

Whether by chance or fate, the Heroes of Ideals and Truth would always clash and eventually meet.

"I hope your truth won't hinder my path to ideals," N muttered, his eyes gradually losing focus. Otherwise, I will have no choice but to defeat you!

Dragonspiral Tower.

'Reshiram, Dragon/Fire type, Vast White Pokémon.

Ability: Turboblaze

Moves: Blue Flare, Outrage, Imprison, Fusion Flare, Flamethrower, Hyper Voice, Fusion Fire, Dragon Pulse, Extrasensory…

Pokédex: This mythical Pokémon can shoot fire from its tail, burning everything to ashes. It is said to help those who seek to create a world of truth.'

Listening to the introduction from Dylan's Rotom Pokédex, Lillie couldn't help but exclaim. Not only is it beautiful, but Reshiram is also incredibly strong!

"I thought you weren't going to choose me," Dylan chuckled, gently patting Reshiram's feathers. They're so soft! Perfect for riding!

"Their path to ideals has already begun, Dylan. We cannot afford to lag behind," an ethereal voice echoed telepathically.

Reshiram gazed up at the clear sky, its dragon eyes seemingly piercing through time and space, locking onto Zekrom and its hero in the distance.

Proud, sacred, beautiful!

Lillie's eyes sparkled as she looked at Reshiram's majestic profile. So beautiful! The White Dragon is simply stunning!

"This is natural," Dylan smiled lightly, showing no concern for N and Zekrom.

For him, the only edge N had was Zekrom. But now, Dylan wasn't just the Hero of Truth—he also had Necrozma!

After tonight's training simulation, once he obtained Cosmog's Lunala form, with Necrozma's Dawn Wings, ha! How could N even dream of beating him?

"?"

Reshiram turned its majestic head towards Dylan, exuding a cold and noble aura.

"I recently encountered two girls in Castelia City, Rosa and Hilda. They often mention hearing things about 'truth' at night. Do you have any idea what that's about, Reshiram?"

Dylan squinted his eyes slightly, smiling as he observed the aloof yet breathtakingly beautiful White Dragon.

Reshiram's dragon eyes froze slightly as it gazed at its chosen hero, lost in thought.

After a while, Reshiram's ethereal, nonchalant telepathic voice returned.

"Maybe they were just hallucinating?"

Yes, definitely can't admit it!

Can't admit I've been casting a wide net! How would I maintain my dignified image?

I am the magnificent White Dragon, not some... player!

"Oh?" Dylan's smile deepened.

"And what about my sister, Lillie?" Dylan glanced at Lillie, who blinked in confusion.

Reshiram's gaze flickered to Lillie. Hmm, I do vaguely remember reaching out to her.

"It must have been a mishearing."

Reshiram remained calm.

"And what about Liam?"

"Who's Liam? I don't recall anyone by that name."

Reshiram seemed confused. Why didn't it remember Liam?

But Reshiram quickly caught on, staring at its hero in disbelief. What a shameless human! Trying to trick a dragon!

"White Dragon, you know, being fickle isn't a good quality," Dylan teased softly. He knew something was off.

"Ahem, Dylan, you know... the current Black Dragon's hero is pretty strong, so I just wanted to consider my options more carefully," Reshiram coughed awkwardly.

"And I swear, you're the only one who's entered the tower! I didn't summon anyone else."

"Well, I feel honored," Dylan chuckled. This White Dragon is something else.

"But it doesn't matter now, Dylan. As long as we join forces, that Black Dragon is no match for us!"

Reshiram's ethereal voice was filled with confidence, exuding an aura of unrivaled pride.

The source of that confidence was none other than the dark blue Pokémon standing behind Dylan, Necrozma. Though Reshiram didn't know much about Necrozma, the strong sense of dread it felt was unmistakable. This Necrozma was a force to be reckoned with!

Dylan is truly powerful! With such a strong Hero of Truth, what's there to fear from the Black Dragon?

If they met, the Black Dragon's head would be shattered! The White Dragon stands for truth and nothing but the truth!

"Naturally," Dylan nodded confidently.

"Well then, Reshiram, I'll be counting on you from now on."

Dylan took out a deep crimson luxury ball and held it toward Reshiram. The White Dragon lowered its head gently, touching the ball.

The luxury ball opened, and Reshiram was absorbed into the ball in a flash of red light.

The indicator barely blinked before stopping.

"Captured. Reshiram," Dylan smiled as he looked at the luxury ball in his hand. He couldn't help but grin. He had personally captured Reshiram!

"Brother! You're amazing!!"

Lillie ran up to Dylan, her emotions a mixture of excitement and admiration, overwhelming her heart.

This was the Legendary Dragon of Truth from Unova's creation myth!

Seeing this sacred White Dragon enter Dylan's Pokéball, Lillie could barely contain her excitement.

"This is only the beginning," Dylan said as he gently ruffled Lillie's hair, though he couldn't hide his own joy.

'Dylan, I can sense the Black Dragon's location. Let's go and defeat it!'

Reshiram's telepathic message resonated in Dylan's mind, full of eagerness.

"Not so fast, there's something important I need to take care of first." Dylan slowly rotated the luxury ball in his hand.

"Oh... well, after you're done, can we go beat the Black Dragon?" The White Dragon's tone shifted from disappointment to excitement once more.

Really? I know you and the Black Dragon don't get along, but do you really want to fight it that badly? Dylan's eyelid twitched slightly.

"Don't worry, Reshiram. We'll deal with the Black Dragon and its hero eventually," Dylan reassured Reshiram.

"Come, Lillie, let's try riding the White Dragon."

Dylan recalled Necrozma, shrinking its Ultra Ball back to pendant size and hanging it on his necklace. He smiled gently at Lillie.

"Really?" Lillie's eyes lit up with excitement and anticipation.

Mom, I've made it! I'm going to ride the legendary White Dragon!

"Of course it's true. Come on out, Reshiram!"

With a flash of light, the majestic and graceful Reshiram appeared once more in front of the two.

"Let's go, Reshiram. Take us back to Icirrus City."

"Roar!"

The ethereal and melodious dragon roar echoed as the white dragon flapped its wings, carrying Dylan and Lillie toward Icirrus City.

Dylan had only one thought: with its stable flight and soft feathers, Reshiram was undoubtedly the best choice as a mount! This trip to Unova was absolutely worth it!

From now on, his airborne ride would definitely be the white dragon. When he had the chance, he'd make another trip to Paldea and collect those two motorcycles, so he'd have a perfect ride for the ground too!

Outside Icirrus City, the white dragon descended, and Dylan recalled it into a valuable Poké Ball. In fact, even in the Unova region, there weren't many people who could recognize Reshiram.

Most people in Unova grew up hearing stories of the region's founding legend, but they regarded it as just that—a legend. Besides, the black dragon and white dragon had been dormant for a thousand years, so no one knew what they looked like.

However, flying Pokémon weren't allowed in the city except under special circumstances.

"Brother~ Where are we going next?" Lillie asked, holding Dylan's arm and walking with a spring in her step, clearly in a great mood.

Of course, she was happy—she had just flown in on Reshiram, and it was super comfortable! Unlike the icy cold feel of Articuno, Reshiram's back was soft and warm, making the ride absolutely wonderful!

"Since we've captured Reshiram, naturally, we should test its combat power," Dylan replied with a gentle smile.

"Find an opponent for Reshiram?" Lillie blinked. She had witnessed Zekrom's power firsthand in Saffron City, and Reshiram, being equally matched with Zekrom, surely had formidable strength.

An ordinary opponent wouldn't be able to challenge Reshiram, right?

"Are you going to challenge that N with Zekrom?" Lillie asked curiously.

"No, a battle with N can wait. I have a more suitable target in mind, an old acquaintance of the white dragon. It will be quite grateful for this." Dylan grinned. If he wanted to test Reshiram's power, then the black dragon would certainly be the most appropriate choice, though Dylan thought even the black dragon might not be strong enough.

But no worries, Unova didn't only have the black and white dragon gods. There was also the 'strongest dragon on the ground' after all (as mentioned in the Keldeo movie, 'the strongest dragon on the ground').

"Huh?" Lillie looked puzzled; she had no idea who Dylan was referring to.

"You'll find out tomorrow." Dylan couldn't resist pinching Lillie's soft cheek because her confused expression was just too adorable.

"Ugh~ Brother, you can't keep pinching me like that. What if my face gets stretched out?" Lillie rubbed her cheek, half-complaining, half-joking.

"Sorry, Lillie is just too cute," Dylan apologized with a mischievous smile.

"You're so sneaky... saying things like that..." Lillie's cheeks turned bright red, and she lowered her head, playing with her fingers, speaking softly.

Seeing Lillie shyly looking down, Dylan chuckled. Though his dear sister was becoming more cheerful, she was still easily embarrassed sometimes.

But that made her even cuter.

The two of them were about to find a restaurant for dinner, but as they passed a small park, they noticed a crowd gathered there.

Dylan glanced over and saw a middle-aged man with pale green hair dressed in flowing robes giving a speech.

"My name is Ghetsis, of Team Plasma. Today, I'm here to talk to you about Pokémon liberation."

"Humans and Pokémon have lived together until now, believing they are indispensable partners. But is that really true? Could it be that this is just wishful thinking on our part? Have you ever wondered if that's the case? Trainers freely order Pokémon around, and even treat their working partners with disdain. Who can definitively say such things don't happen?"

"So, what should we do? Pokémon are different from humans; they possess unknown potential within them. There is much we can learn from them. We must... liberate Pokémon! Only then can humans..."

Ghetsis was passionately delivering his speech, becoming increasingly animated. But just then—

"Hello, Officer Jenny? Yes, I'd like to report someone promoting illegal and harmful ideas here, trying to deceive innocent citizens. Such a person should be severely punished! Yes, I'm currently at... please come quickly, I'll keep an eye on him until you arrive."

The call interrupted Ghetsis's speech, and his words came to an abrupt halt.

"??"

Ghetsis's face instantly darkened, and his cold gaze fell on the young man in black who had made the call.

"What's the matter? Why are you staring at me? Go on," Dylan said, popping sunflower seeds and gesturing for Ghetsis to continue.

"???" Ghetsis's expression grew even darker.

"Why did you stop, Ghetsis? You were doing great. Don't worry, you won't be going anywhere until Officer Jenny arrives. You should finish your performance," Dylan said as he took a folding chair from his backpack, sat down, and crossed his legs.

Ghetsis—one of Team Plasma's Seven Sages and N's adoptive father. If he could catch this guy, it wouldn't be too much to ask the Unova League for a Deino, right?

Lillie, ever attentive, stood behind Dylan and gently massaged his shoulders.

At first glance, Dylan's aura of arrogance instantly came to life.

"Let's go." Ghetsis shot Dylan a cold look, whispered to the two grunts behind him, and prepared to leave.

Curse it! Curse it! Curse it!! Ghetsis's eyes were filled with malice. If Officer Jenny arrived, things would become troublesome. He had to leave quickly, but once he got back, he would find someone to deal with that arrogant brat and settle the score!

"Tsk~ Did I say you could leave?"

A lazy voice came from behind.

"Kid, don't go too far! Do you even know who I am?" Ghetsis's face darkened further as he turned to glare at Dylan, rage turning into a twisted smile.

Such ignorance. Ghetsis thought that if he didn't put this kid in his place, the boy would continue to act insolent.

It seemed a lesson was indeed necessary to make the boy understand the meaning of 'knowing one's place.'

(End of chapter)

---

# 147: Chapter 148: Really?

Ghetsis was not in a good mood! His speech had been going well, and he was just about to reach the climax.

Then, out of nowhere, some clueless kid called Officer Jenny, and now this kid wanted to stop him from leaving?

"Well, well~ Ghetsis, you're certainly throwing your weight around."

Dylan stood up, casually sizing up Ghetsis with a look of astonishment.

"I wonder what kind of reward I'd get if I turned you over to the Unova League? But considering your worth, I suppose I'd be lucky to get a Deino in exchange."

"???" Ghetsis's face darkened to the point where it looked like water could drip from it.

This brat!!

"Ghetsis, shall we deal with him? Officer Jenny is probably already on her way," one of the two Plasma Grunts behind Ghetsis whispered. Although they were furious about Dylan insulting their Sage, time was pressing.

It would be trouble if Officer Jenny arrived.

Ghetsis understood the urgency, and after glaring at Dylan with an extremely cold expression, he let out a cold snort and prepared to leave.

"Oh? I was expecting something more from you, Ghetsis. Turns out, you're just planning to slink away?" Dylan's mocking tone stopped Ghetsis in his tracks.

"Even if Alder were here today, he couldn't save you! You disrespectful little punk!!"

Ghetsis whirled around, his cold, sinister voice sending chills through the air as he opened a Poké Ball.

A Hydreigon appeared before Ghetsis, its black and red eyes fixed on Dylan and Lillie.

A Hydreigon, huh?

Lillie clutched Dylan's sleeve, cautiously eyeing the ominous Hydreigon. Though it seemed darker than Zinnia's Hydreigon, it clearly lacked Zinnia's Hydreigon's presence.

"Oh? Big words, Ghetsis. If Champion Alder were here, you'd probably be begging for mercy." Dylan continued to taunt lightly.

Ghetsis's face twitched, his expression darkening further.

Never! No one had ever dared to provoke him like this before!!

"Kid, I've decided. After I break your mouth, let's see if you still have the nerve to talk."

Ghetsis's icy gaze never left Dylan. Being an arrogant and conceited man, how could he endure such humiliation?

"Come on, Ghetsis. Show me what you're made of. I hope your bite is at least half as tough as your bark."

Dylan chuckled dismissively as he released a Salamence, whose violent aura filled the air as it spread its wings and looked down at Hydreigon.

A Salamence?

Ghetsis's expression flickered. A Salamence was no ordinary Pokémon; could this kid actually be powerful?

Perhaps he shouldn't act rashly—after all, they were still in Icirrus City.

"Relax, Ghetsis. Whether you want to fight or not, Salamence won't let you leave. After all, I still plan to trade you to the Unova League for a Deino." Dylan smirked, as if he could read Ghetsis's thoughts.

"Arrogant!!"

Ghetsis's expression changed instantly.

"You brat! If you insist on courting death, I will gladly oblige!"

Ghetsis's menacing words prompted a nonchalant shrug from Dylan.

"Your mere Salamence is nothing in front of my Hydreigon!"

Yes! As the true leader of Team Plasma and even the mastermind behind the hero chosen by the dragon of ideals, he could surely defeat an arrogant and foolish boy.

Hydreigon versus Salamence—how could he lose?

He would win!!

But then…

"W-What? How is this possible?"

Ghetsis staggered back, pale-faced, as he watched his Hydreigon fall effortlessly.

How could it be so strong?!

"I can't lose to you! I am the perfect human who founded Team Plasma!!"

Ghetsis's twisted and resentful face showed he was on the verge of a breakdown.

"What a pathetic man, unable to even accept reality," Dylan remarked in disdain, glancing at the ashen-faced Ghetsis.

"Congratulations, you're even weaker than I expected, and to think you call yourself a 'perfect human.'"

Dylan's taunts showed no mercy.

"Don't get cocky! This isn't over! Just you wait!!"

Ghetsis was seething with hatred. This brat's strength had caught him off guard, even his prized Hydreigon was easily defeated.

But it didn't matter! He still had his adopted son, N, who was followed by the dragon of ideals. As long as N was around, no one in this world was his match!

N was unbeatable!!

"Are you not fully developed intellectually? You can still say things like 'just you wait'?" Dylan's eyes gleamed with amusement.

"You!!" Ghetsis's eyes were bloodshot.

But Dylan could no longer be bothered with this defeated dog. Under Salamence's intimidation, Ghetsis, though bristling with anger, reluctantly laid all his Poké Balls on the ground.

Before long, a police car arrived, and Officer Jenny from Unova stepped out.

"I'm Dylan, Gym Leader of Rota Gym in Kanto, here on a trip with my sister. I just happened to catch this guy spreading the ideology of an evil organization."

"As a good citizen who upholds the values of the League, I couldn't tolerate such heresy."

Dylan gave a brief explanation to Officer Jenny.

"Thank you, Gym Leader Dylan. We've been on the lookout for Team Plasma operatives recently. You've done us a great service." Officer Jenny saluted Dylan gratefully.

Ever since Zekrom appeared in Saffron City, Goodshow had contacted the Unova League, and they had begun a crackdown on Team Plasma members, though no high-ranking officials had been caught.

But now, capturing one of the Seven Sages, Ghetsis, was indeed a big win for Officer Jenny.

"No need for thanks. I was just doing my duty."

Dylan nodded with a smile, then handed Ghetsis's Poké Balls to Officer Jenny.

"Please be careful with the detention, Officer Jenny. Ghetsis is a highly dangerous Elite-level Trainer."

"Rest assured, Gym Leader Dylan!"

Officer Jenny's expression became serious. This guy was an Elite-level Trainer? What about Dylan, who defeated Ghetsis?

"I'll report this matter to my superiors. The Unova League will not forget your contribution, Gym Leader Dylan."

Officer Jenny, being astute, immediately addressed Dylan.

"I was just doing what I should," Dylan replied with the same line.

With that, Officer Jenny handcuffed Ghetsis and escorted him into the police car, which then drove toward the station.

Hopefully, Ghetsis would enjoy the meals in Icirrus City's jail because he would soon be transferred to Opelucid City.

Would N break into the Unova League to rescue Ghetsis? That would surely be an interesting spectacle.

"Big brother, who was that man?"

As the police car drove away, Lillie curiously asked Dylan.

It was her first time seeing her brother mock someone so mercilessly. He usually wasn't like this, which showed just how much he disliked Ghetsis.

"Ghetsis, the founder of Team Plasma and N's adoptive father. The reason N is the way he is now is because of him."

Dylan casually explained. He really didn't think much of Ghetsis, unlike other leaders of villainous teams who had ambitions and reasons.

Only Giovanni and Ghetsis were driven purely by ambition, though Giovanni at least had the aura and qualities of a hero and could accept defeat, as he temporarily disbanded Team Rocket after losing to Red to train and grow stronger.

But Ghetsis was different. He resorted to all kinds of means for his ambitions, even manipulating a child's worldview with a false reality.

He raised N, treating him as a monster while pretending to be a guide and loving father. After being defeated, he became deranged, unable to accept his failure.

All of this showed that Ghetsis was a pathetic and incapable man.

Just like Archie and Maxie—nothing but trash.

Dylan didn't dislike ambitious villains. In his view, the world was colorful and dazzling precisely because of the various people who existed and intersected, making it a vibrant place.

But at least villains needed to be outstanding and charismatic, right? Only such ambitious individuals could create an exciting story.

"Let's go, Lillie. We'll get something to eat, and tomorrow we'll head to Lacunosa Town."

"Alright~!"

The next day, Dylan and Lillie flew to Lacunosa Town on Reshiram.

Lacunosa Town, located in the southeastern part of the Unova region, gets its name from the lattice pattern used in woven bamboo baskets, known for warding off evil spirits with its triangular shape.

After about two hours of flying, Dylan and Lillie arrived at Lacunosa Town. Reshiram wasn't flying at its maximum speed, yet they made good time.

"Huh? What are these walls?" Lillie was surprised as she noticed the walls surrounding Lacunosa Town. Walls? In this day and age?

"It's an old custom of theirs, nothing to be puzzled about," Dylan said as he recalled Reshiram. After surveying the surroundings and determining the direction, he led Lillie toward the northwest.

"Do you know why they built these walls?" Lillie asked, curious. It felt like Dylan knew everything.

"It's a local legend passed down in Lacunosa," Dylan explained. "A long time ago, a meteor fell from space, carrying a terrifying beast. They say the beast would appear at night, accompanied by a cold wind, to snatch away people and Pokémon to eat them."

"What?" Lillie was stunned.

"So, the townspeople built these walls to encircle the town and even established a rule prohibiting people from going outside at night, encouraging everyone to stay indoors. Even today, some residents stay home at night, though not everyone believes in the legend anymore."

Dylan chuckled softly. Indeed, this was the story passed down in Lacunosa Town.

The "terrifying beast" from the legend was none other than the Ice Dragon, Kyurem. Compared to its two counterparts, the Black Dragon and the White Dragon, the Ice Dragon was less famous. Its fierce appearance only added to Kyurem's negative reputation among the locals.

Saying Kyurem has a bad temper is certainly true, but saying it eats humans and Pokémon?

(Confused emoji)

Dylan was more inclined to believe that Kyurem had frozen some unfortunate individuals who accidentally provoked it. After all, in the Sacred Sword movie, Kyurem did freeze the three Swords of Justice in an instant.

Furthermore, Lacunosa Town's legend was fundamentally incorrect. Kyurem originated from the remains left behind after the original dragon split into the Black Dragon and White Dragon, not from some meteor.

However, there was one element of truth in the legend: Kyurem's lair wasn't too far from Lacunosa Town.

The Giant Chasm.

Located in the far northeastern corner of the Unova region, the Giant Chasm is a massive crater said to be Kyurem's home.

Dylan took out the winter clothes they had prepared in advance, and with Lillie, headed into the depths of the Giant Chasm.

Guided by Necrozma's sense of direction, they continued onward. As they ventured deeper, the temperature noticeably dropped, and both Dylan and Lillie were now bundled up in thick winter coats.

"Dylan, I think I sense a familiar presence. Where have you taken us?"

Abruptly, Reshiram's solemn telepathy echoed in Dylan's mind. Even while inside its Poké Ball, Reshiram could feel the aura of the Ice Dragon growing stronger.

Where had Dylan brought them now?

"Let's turn back, Dylan. I can sense we're getting closer to a terrifying creature," Reshiram urged its Hero of Truth.

However, the brave Hero of Truth wasn't about to heed the White Dragon's advice.

"That's right, Reshiram. My target is none other than the legendary Ice Dragon, Kyurem."

Dylan and Lillie emerged from a cave to find themselves in a breathtaking world of ice and blue hues.

It was magnificent—truly magnificent!

They had entered a pristine, glacial canyon. On the ground ahead, a gray dragon was lying down, leisurely napping.

It was Kyurem, the Ice Dragon! And why did it look so content?

Because above Kyurem, a multitude of Cryogonal were slowly floating around, while flakes of icy snow drifted down.

Yes, these wild Cryogonal were creating "artificial snowfall" for Kyurem, suggesting that the Ice Dragon knew how to enjoy itself.

"Huh?"

Reshiram was taken aback.

"No, Dylan, you may not understand what Kyurem represents! Its temper is utterly horrible; it's best we don't provoke it."

"How about we go after the Black Dragon instead?" Reshiram quickly recovered and started negotiating with Dylan.

"Kyurem is a legendary dragon, and it's an Ice Dragon. But Reshiram, as a Fire Dragon, you should be able to defeat Kyurem, right?"

"I'm supposed to fight Kyurem???" Reshiram's ethereal telepathy was filled with bewilderment.

Who could understand? It had finally found a strong Hero, and now this Hero wanted it to fight Kyurem right away? If Reshiram had that kind of power, it would have already scattered the Black Dragon's ashes without needing to choose a worthy Hero of Truth.

(End of chapter)

Patreon: RockyRoad891

# 148: Chapter 149: Merely an empty shell

"Don't be so afraid, Reshiram. You are the True Dragon God, after all, and Kyurem is just an ice dragon."

"No, no, no, Dylan, I don't think you understand."

Dylan could almost imagine Reshiram shaking its head frantically.

"At that time, because of differences in ideals, I and the black dragon separated from the body of the Original Dragon. We inherited the inner power of the dragon, but its shell was left behind, which eventually turned into Kyurem."

Reshiram explained the origin of the Three Dragons of Unova to Dylan.

"Do you understand what I mean, Dylan? At the root, both Zekrom and I are parts of Kyurem that were lost. It can capture me and the black dragon, then reintegrate us back into itself to restore the body it lost."

"Being originally just a shell, Kyurem possesses power that surpasses mine and the black dragon's. Although we separated and took some of the power, a portion of the 'inner essence' still remains within Kyurem."

"Its true power is far beyond what I can compare to now."

Reshiram spoke softly, revealing why both it and Zekrom preferred to stay hidden from Kyurem.

It wasn't just because of the overwhelming difference in strength; the most terrifying thing was that Kyurem was a "hero waiting to fill its lost body with truth and ideals!"

The consciousness of the Original Dragon of Unova had long since perished, splitting into three: Zekrom, Reshiram, and Kyurem.

But Kyurem was special! If any of the three dragons could return to their original form as the Original Dragon of Unova, only Kyurem had the potential to do so!

"So, you're saying Kyurem is incredibly strong?" Dylan leaned on his hand, gazing at the massive gray dragon in the distance.

"Yes, extremely strong!" Reshiram replied seriously. It seemed that its hero finally understood the gravity of the situation, which brought Reshiram some relief.

"Tsk~ That only makes it more appealing. Now, I really want to capture it."

Dylan's eyes glinted with excitement. From Reshiram's words, he had grasped a crucial piece of information.

When the Original Dragon split, its shell still retained some of the 'inner essence'? In that case, this Kyurem could be just like the one in the movies!

"???" The white dragon was confused.

"Don't worry, Reshiram. Be brave. As your hero, I'll always be your strongest support!"

Dylan reassured the suddenly nervous white dragon.

"I'll have Necrozma ready to assist you at any time. If you can't handle it, we'll gang up and show 'truth' what's what!"

"Are you sure, Dylan?"

Reshiram was skeptical about the sincerity of Dylan's words, especially since fighting the ice dragon was…

"Of course, I'm sure. You're my white dragon, after all. Do you think I'd just stand by and watch you get beaten up by an ice dragon?"

"Then, Dylan, make sure Necrozma is on standby at all times, okay? Watch closely."

Reshiram was still a little uneasy and kept reminding Dylan repeatedly.

"Relax, relax~ You can count on me." Dylan's eyelid twitched slightly. This white dragon was too much.

Then, Dylan looked towards Kyurem in the distance. His Rotom Pokédex flew out, containing the legendary Pokémon data he had compiled.

'Beep~ Kyurem, Dragon/Ice-type, Boundary Pokémon.

Ability: Pressure.

Moves: Sheer Cold, Glaciate, Outrage, Blizzard, Hyper Voice, Ice Beam, Dragon Pulse, Freeze-Dry, Scale Shot...

Description: A hero waiting to fill its lost body with truth and ideals, the legendary Ice Dragon Pokémon.'

So strong!!

Lillie listened to the Rotom Pokédex's introduction and gripped Dylan's clothes tighter. This was the legendary Ice Dragon, Kyurem.

"The strongest dragon on land."

Dylan held Lillie's small hand to reassure her, then walked towards Kyurem with her slowly.

Buzzing~

It seemed that the geometric snowflakes swirling in the sky noticed the two uninvited guests, emitting a low hum before calming down again, as if afraid of disturbing something.

But it was already too late.

Dylan could feel Lillie's hand suddenly tightening around his. That was because the massive gray dragon ahead had opened its eyes at some point, its golden, majestic gaze fixed on the two of them.

Kyurem had awakened!

The entire glacial valley seemed to fall silent at this moment!

Under the overwhelming, oppressive atmosphere, even the air seemed to grow thick. Lillie felt like she had to use all her strength just to breathe—the pressure was immense!

"Roar~!"

A ferocious roar echoed across the sky, deafeningly loud. The sound waves generated by the roar surged forward, causing the entire glacial valley to tremble slightly.

So terrifying! This was just a roar!

Lillie shielded her face with her hand, the biting wind stinging her cheeks.

The gray dragon slowly rose, towering over the two humans who dared to approach it.

Kyurem's golden dragon eyes glowed with a frightening light as it stared directly at Dylan. It could feel it! It could sense it clearly!

"White Dragon! You dare to appear before me!"

The hoarse, deep voice resonated telepathically throughout the valley. It was Kyurem's voice.

It had sensed the presence of the white dragon emanating from this human.

"Has my kindness for the past two thousand years made you too bold?"

Kyurem lowered its head slightly, its face twisted into a terrifying snarl, enough to make a child cry.

It hadn't gone out of its way to find and devour the black and white dragons, which was its act of mercy toward them. And now, the white dragon dared to come before it on its own. Was this a provocation?

"Don't be angry, Kyurem. We don't intend to disturb your slumber."

Dylan smiled as he spoke, releasing a gentle aura, but Kyurem's demeanor remained hostile, though at least it hadn't attacked outright.

That was already a good outcome—after all, this was the legendary Ice Dragon, Kyurem.

"I am Dylan, Reshiram's partner. We came here specifically for you, Kyurem."

"Hah~"

Kyurem sneered. It could already guess that Zekrom and Reshiram would normally avoid it at all costs, so why would they suddenly seek it out?

"Kyurem, the strongest dragon of Unova's legends, sleeping in a place like this for so long… Don't you think that's a waste?"

Dylan glanced around at the surroundings. The pristine, immaculate glacial valley was a magnificent sight, but this had been Kyurem's home for thousands of years.

"Your power is so overwhelming, incredibly alluring. Even Reshiram and Zekrom have left behind legends passed down through the generations."

"As the strongest dragon, isn't it a shame that no one acknowledges you, Kyurem?" Dylan spoke slowly to Kyurem.

In response, Kyurem merely scoffed.

"What a ridiculous statement."

Kyurem's golden dragon eyes stared at the human chosen by the White Dragon.

"Why would I need validation from weak ants like humans? Only the worthless care about such things."

"The White Dragon and the Black Dragon are both fools. What truth? What ideals?"

Kyurem slowly straightened up, and its hoarse, resentful telepathic voice grew more dangerous.

It held nothing but contempt for the Black and White Dragons, who were constantly at odds over laughable concepts like truth and ideals. It was almost enough to make a dragon laugh.

"Weak White Dragon, weak Black Dragon, and their feeble principles and existence."

"Relying on frail humans to affirm their value—what right do those worthless beings have to be compared with me?"

Frost began to emanate from Kyurem's body, and its presence grew heavier.

"Pathetic creatures. Then again, I have let them be for over two thousand years. Perhaps it is time to bring them back."

In the end, as far as Kyurem was concerned, the so-called White and Black Dragons were no better than this.

"Reshiram, it seems Kyurem holds you in low regard."

Dylan narrowed his eyes slightly and pulled Lillie back, jokingly teasing his White Dragon in his mind.

"That's just how Kyurem is. But it's actually the most pitiful one," Reshiram replied calmly.

"It mocks us for having the purpose of truth and ideals, but Kyurem itself is merely an empty shell."

Kyurem despised the Black and White Dragons, but to both of them, the strongest Ice Dragon was equally pitiful.

"Having lost its inner self, all that's left is a hollow shell with nothing but power. It doesn't even have a purpose for its existence," Reshiram said faintly.

Hearing Kyurem's mockery of it and the Black Dragon, Reshiram remained unexpectedly composed.

The divine dragon had split due to ideological differences, resulting in two dragons born with principles of truth and ideals. But what of the remaining shell?

Nothing but emptiness—it had nothing but power because its heart was already hollow. Thus, it was waiting to fill the void with truth and ideals.

"Tsk..."

Dylan clicked his tongue. Goodness, while Kyurem's insults were harsh, Reshiram was no slouch either.

Without uttering a single curse, Reshiram's disdain for Kyurem was evident—every word was a critical hit. Truly worthy of being the White Dragon of truth, it was a verbal onslaught.

If Kyurem heard this, wouldn't it go berserk?

Oh, right—why didn't Reshiram just say it to Kyurem's face?

"Human boy, bring out your White Dragon! After more than two thousand years, it's time to settle the score."

Kyurem didn't know what Dylan and Reshiram were discussing, but now it felt there was no point in continuing the conversation.

Since Reshiram had come here, Kyurem wouldn't let the White Dragon go so easily!

"Kyurem, why so aggressive? Why don't we keep talking? Look, isn't it boring just lying here and sleeping? How about I show you the outside world?"

"Human! I won't repeat myself."

Kyurem's voice was nearly growling, barely suppressing its anger. If it weren't for the inexplicable feeling that this human's aura wasn't entirely repugnant, Kyurem would have already unleashed a freezing beam.

It had little patience.

"In that case, Reshiram!"

Dylan tossed Reshiram's Poké Ball, and with an ethereal, resonant cry, the graceful and magnificent White Dragon spread its wings and floated in the air. Its azure eyes stared intently at Kyurem.

Kyurem's golden eyes suddenly gleamed fiercely, and the pressure it exuded intensified.

"White Dragon!!!"

The Ice Dragon's roar echoed through the icy canyon, and a flame instantly ignited on its back, spreading across its entire body.

In the blink of an eye, a larger, colder yet paradoxically searing grayish-white dragon appeared.

"This is...?" Lillie was stunned.

Its upper body rose, standing on its two legs, with some features resembling Reshiram, including a similar heat-emitting organ on its tail.

This was White Kyurem!

'Beep~ White Kyurem, Dragon/Ice-type, boundary Pokémon.

Ability: Turboblaze. Moves: Glaciate, Sheer Cold, Outrage, Blizzard, Fusion Flare, Ice Beam, Freeze-Dry...

Description: It has foreseen the arrival of a true world for Pokémon and humans, and seeks to protect such a future! (White Kyurem)'

So powerful, so powerful!!

"Indeed, this Ice Dragon can freely change forms just like in the movies." Dylan's eyes glimmered as he looked at the awe-inspiring White Kyurem.

"White Dragon!"

White Kyurem slowly floated upward, its savage dragon eyes locked onto the elegant Reshiram. A vicious smile appeared on its menacing face.

"I have seen the true world coming, White Dragon. In that true world... there is no place for you!!"

Reshiram suddenly felt a chill run through its body, its white feathers standing on end.

This Ice Dragon was too much! It actually tried to scare it!

'Dylan, you'd better make sure Necrozma keeps an eye on things! This Kyurem seems serious!'

'Don't worry, don't worry, Reshiram. Just go for it—we've got your back.'

'Then why are you all moving farther away?'

(End of chapter)
