# 149: Chapter 150:  Save Me!!

Dylan didn't pay any attention to Reshiram. Of course, with Reshiram locked in battle against White Kyurem, he had to keep his distance. Otherwise, what if he got caught up in the crossfire?

Not only did he move farther away, but Dylan also released Articuno, allowing himself and Lillie to sit on its back. If the shockwaves from the battle came their way, Articuno could dodge or use Protect to shield them.

"White Dragon!!"

White Kyurem's roar echoed through the air as its massive body charged straight at Reshiram with a powerful and imposing momentum.

Meanwhile, the wild Cryogonal that had been floating nearby scattered in a panic, fleeing in disorganized confusion.

"Reshiram, use Fusion Flare!"

Dylan commanded Reshiram through their telepathic connection.

The main purpose of confronting Kyurem was to gauge the power of both Reshiram and Kyurem. If they could capture Kyurem, that would be ideal.

If not, at least they would have a better understanding of its strength, preparing for a future attempt.

"Rrrr~"

Reshiram continued to retreat, the turbine on its tail igniting with a bright red glow. The tips of its two long white locks behind its head also lit up with the same red hue, as intense heat radiated from its body.

Overdrive Mode!

This wasn't a special form but rather a sign that Reshiram was giving its all.

A massive fireball formed above Reshiram, flames swirling around it in rings.

This was one of Reshiram's signature moves, the Fire-type Fusion Flare! Apart from Reshiram, White Kyurem could also use this attack.

Noticing Reshiram's actions, White Kyurem's turbine also glowed red, and a massive fireball formed above it.

"Because it has absorbed the same genes as Reshiram, it can also use Fusion Flare," Lillie thought to herself as she watched from Dylan's arms.

And it seemed that White Kyurem's fireball was significantly larger than Reshiram's.

Fusion Flare x2!

Boom!

The two enormous, scorching Fusion Flares collided in mid-air, and waves of searing heat rippled outward in all directions.

The ancient, unmelting ice of the surrounding glaciers began to melt under the intense heat.

However, after a brief clash, Reshiram's Fusion Flare started to falter and was soon overwhelmed by White Kyurem's, which continued its onslaught toward Reshiram.

"How can they be the same move, but with such different results?" Lillie exclaimed in surprise.

"The difference lies in their power. Reshiram isn't as strong as White Kyurem, and the gap is quite significant," Dylan noted, raising an eyebrow. Even though they were using the same move, the intensity differed between the two Pokémon.

Reshiram naturally evaded the incoming Fusion Flare, but in the next instant, the massive gray-white form of White Kyurem closed in.

It slammed directly into Reshiram, grabbing it with its claws and pinning it against the canyon wall.

Bang!

Reshiram groaned in pain; White Kyurem was using a lot of force.

"White Dragon! Look at me!!"

White Kyurem gripped Reshiram's head with its right claw, its golden, ferocious eyes staring intently at Reshiram. Its raspy telepathic voice echoed in Reshiram's mind.

Reshiram locked eyes with White Kyurem.

"Look at yourself! Your truth is so fragile! Tell me, where is your truth??"

Reshiram didn't respond, instead gathering a pure blue-green draconic light in its mouth.

But before it could unleash the attack, White Kyurem clamped its dragon claw over Reshiram's mouth, sealing it shut, forcing the Dragon Pulse to dissipate.

"You think I didn't know? You and the Black Dragon have always avoided me, always choosing to run away. Did you really think you could hide from me? I simply didn't care to hunt you down," White Kyurem's voice was menacing.

It lifted Reshiram high, its sinister words echoing.

"How laughable! The hero you chose has brought you right to me! Do you understand, White Dragon?"

"You will all return to me. We will become one again! That is the true world!!"

Boom!

A Dragon Pulse blasted directly onto White Kyurem, erupting in a surge of energy.

Taking advantage of the opportunity, Reshiram broke free from Kyurem's grasp and quickly retreated.

"Dylan! This thing is too strong! Hurry and help me!"

Reshiram anxiously called out to Dylan.

White Kyurem was terrifying. Its gaze made Reshiram feel incredibly uneasy, as though the ice dragon was eyeing its beautiful form, wanting to "consume" it. It was terrifying!

"Don't be afraid. Face your fear, and you can overcome it! The battle has only just begun," Dylan encouraged Reshiram. How deeply was this white dragon scarred by the ice dragon to be calling for help after just one move?

"???"

Reshiram hesitated for a moment.

That made sense—it was embarrassing to call for help after just one move. At the very least, it should hold on for three moves, right?

But at that moment, White Kyurem was already charging again, unleashing a fierce blizzard from its mouth.

An Ice-type Blizzard!

The sky turned white with the storm, and everything within sight was covered in swirling snow.

The ice that had melted from the earlier Fusion Flares began to freeze again under the blizzard's cold.

"Reshiram, use Blue Flare."

The turbine on Reshiram's tail ignited with intense red light, and blue flames gathered in its mouth.

In the next instant, a massive blue flame burst forth, meeting the incoming blizzard head-on.

Reshiram's strongest signature move, Blue Flare!

The Blue Flare and the icy blizzard clashed fiercely, with blue flames and ice-blue snow battering against each other.

But alarmingly, even Reshiram's most powerful Blue Flare couldn't withstand the blizzard.

The icy storm visibly consumed the brilliant flames.

Roar!!

With a roar filled with hostility, the blizzard expanded, swallowing up the Blue Flare and continuing its assault toward Reshiram.

Reshiram's eyes widened!

"Dylan! Save me!"

The raging blizzard completely engulfed Reshiram, who was unable to retreat in time. Although as a White Dragon, Reshiram wasn't weak to Ice-type moves, the blizzard still dealt regular damage.

And this was no ordinary blizzard—it was White Kyurem's blizzard!

The blizzard howled past, with the sky completely covered by snow and ice. Snowflakes descended beautifully, painting a mesmerizing scene.

However, Lillie didn't find it beautiful at all. One powerful move actually changed the entire area's weather! What kind of legendary Pokémon was this?

When the blizzard subsided, Reshiram, frozen stiff, nearly fell to the ground. Its once soft and beautiful white feathers were now covered in frost, and the dragon itself was in bad shape.

Boom!

The next second, White Kyurem's massive body flew forward, slamming Reshiram into the mountain wall for the second time.

"Weak! You're even weaker than I expected, White Dragon!"

White Kyurem's fierce eyes stared at Reshiram, and the oppressive telepathic voice sent chills down Reshiram's spine.

But White Kyurem's strength was overwhelming—Reshiram couldn't break free!

"Dylan! If you don't act now, you'll lose your beautiful White Dragon forever!"

In a panic, Reshiram frantically called out to its trainer through telepathy. Kyurem's enormous face was right in front of it, terrifyingly close! "This Ice Dragon might really eat me alive!"

"As expected of you, Reshiram," Dylan sighed helplessly. He had hoped to learn more about the dynamics between the White Dragon and Ice Dragon, expecting Reshiram to put up a fight against Kyurem, but he didn't anticipate Reshiram to desperately call for help after just two moves.

"Necrozma."

Dylan took out the Ultra Ball from his chest and enlarged it before releasing it.

While White Kyurem was contemplating whether to devour Reshiram then and there, thinking that absorbing Reshiram's full genes could make it even stronger despite its ability to shift forms freely...

"Hm?"

As White Kyurem pondered, a golden beam of light suddenly shot toward it at lightning speed.

A Photon Geyser from a Psychic-type!

"Who's there?!"

White Kyurem's eyes narrowed fiercely as it gathered icy energy in its mouth, launching a winding Ice Beam to intercept the Photon Geyser.

Boom!

The Photon Geyser and Ice Beam clashed violently in mid-air, exploding with a tremendous force.

The shockwave swept across the area, causing White Kyurem's dragon eyes to flicker. That was a powerful attack.

As the dust settled, a strange pink bat covered in deep blue crystals spread its wings and hovered silently—it was Dawn Wings Necrozma!

White Kyurem released its grip on Reshiram, turning slowly to face Dawn Wings Necrozma, its violent and savage aura gradually intensifying.

Finally free, Reshiram breathed a sigh of relief and quickly flew behind Dawn Wings Necrozma. It felt so reassuring to have a powerful ally for protection!

"Huh? Brother, is that Necrozma? Why does it look like that?" Lillie asked in confusion, staring at the now completely different Dawn Wings Necrozma. In just one day, Necrozma had changed so much.

"It obtained new power, so it looks different," Dylan explained with a smile, gesturing for the Rotom Pokédex to give more information.

Indeed, during the simulation training last night, he had successfully obtained Lunala and fused it with Necrozma, transforming it into Dawn Wings Necrozma.

'Beep~ Necrozma (Dawn Wings), Psychic/Ghost-type, Prism Pokémon

Ability: Prism Armor Signature moves: Prismatic Laser, Photon Geyser, Shadow Beam, Power Gem, Psychic, Wide Power, Dragon Pulse...

Description: The form it takes when attached to Lunala, becoming even more aggressive and viewing everything as an enemy. It uses laser beams to burn everything away. (Dawn Wings)'

Wait a minute!

Lillie froze, having caught something in the description—"the form attached to Lunala"? Was this really Necrozma fused with Lunala? No way! Then where did Lunala come from? Lillie was starting to feel dizzy.

White Kyurem stared at Dawn Wings Necrozma, its eyes narrowing especially upon seeing Reshiram hiding behind it. Its aura grew even colder and more ferocious.

That useless White Dragon! It couldn't fight and had to call for backup?!

Fine, fine!

"Lima~!"

Dawn Wings Necrozma let out a hoarse cry, signaling for Reshiram to retreat and leave the fight to it.

Reshiram immediately pulled back to maintain a safe distance, cheering quietly to Dawn Wings Necrozma.

"Go for it, big bro! Smash that wretched Ice Dragon!"

# 150: Chapter 151: A legal 2v1!

At this moment, somewhere in Unova.

"Father has been captured by the League members in Icirrus City?"

N's vacant eyes stared at the Plasma Grunt before him, and he spoke each word slowly.

How could this happen? N pressed his lips together and furrowed his brows. This shouldn't be right.

"Yes, King. It happened around dusk yesterday. The sage was spreading the organization's ideals in Icirrus City and was arrested by Officer Jenny," the Plasma Grunt reported carefully.

Everyone knew that Ghetsis, the sage, was N's adoptive father, and N had great respect for him.

Standing nearby, Colress adjusted his glasses and raised an eyebrow, his expression amused.

"I understand. Is Father still being held at the Icirrus City Police Station?" N asked after a moment of silence, realizing it was true.

But this didn't make sense. Although his father wasn't as strong as him, he was definitely not weak. Could Officer Jenny in Icirrus City really be that strong?

"Yes, but I've heard they plan to transfer him to Opelucid City soon."

"You may leave."

N fell silent. Opelucid City was the headquarters of the Unova League. The fact that Ghetsis was being transferred there showed how seriously the League took him.

The atmosphere grew tense.

"N, are you going to rescue Ghetsis?" Colress asked. It was less of a question and more of an assumption. N deeply respected Ghetsis and wouldn't just let him grow old in prison.

"Yes," N nodded.

"Then you'd better act fast. If Ghetsis reaches Opelucid City, it will be much more difficult to rescue him," Colress reminded him.

Opelucid City was to Unova what Indigo Plateau was to Kanto—a sacred city and the headquarters of the Unova League.

If N stormed into Opelucid City to rescue Ghetsis, it would be a direct slap in the face to the Unova League, and a loud one at that.

The League would undoubtedly be furious and launch an all-out manhunt for Team Plasma, which was no laughing matter.

Rescuing Ghetsis while he was still being transported would anger the League, but it wouldn't lead to such an all-out pursuit.

"I understand." N was smart and knew this well.

"How's the progress with reviving Genesect?" N asked, turning to Colress.

"There are two complete ones, and they're quite powerful," Colress said with a slight smile as he spoke of his masterpiece. Joining Team Plasma had greatly advanced his research.

"Only two?" N's lifeless eyes fixed on Colress.

"Of course, why would I hide that?" Colress shrugged.

"Then bring those two Genesect and come with me," N said calmly, no longer pressing Colress.

Colress was a genius and a smart man, and N trusted that as long as he led Team Plasma, Colress wouldn't act recklessly.

"N, you want me to help you break a criminal out from the Unova League?" Colress's glasses gleamed, hiding his eyes.

"That's not a criminal. That's my father, a sage of Team Plasma, and your collaborator," N replied, his dull, gray eyes staring at Colress, clearly displeased by his words.

"Colress, please remember that it was my father who took you in when you were homeless. That's why you have the luxury of conducting your research now."

"Alright, alright, no need to remind me. I know," Colress replied casually. Indeed, it was Ghetsis who found him when the Unova League had put him on a wanted list and offered a partnership, which led him to join Team Plasma.

But Colress didn't feel he owed Team Plasma. Many of his experiments had benefited the organization, and besides, he had given them two Genesect, hadn't he?

"Let's go."

After warning Colress, N headed for the door.

"Seriously, I don't want to completely antagonize the Pokémon League," Colress muttered, frowning. Ghetsis was truly useless, always spreading that ridiculous ideology. Who in their right mind would listen to such cultish ideas?

And to top it off, he got caught by Officer Jenny. What a waste.

"No, I need to plan a way out," Colress thought to himself. He wasn't about to follow Team Plasma into ruin. He still wanted to continue his research.

Meanwhile, in the icy canyon of Giant Chasm...

"Roar~!"

White Kyurem let out a dragon roar, unleashing a fierce snowstorm from its mouth. The biting wind and snow swirled violently.

An Ice-type Blizzard!

"Necrozma, use Photon Geyser!"

Necrozma, in its Dawn Wings form, shifted back, small golden lights gathering at the tips of its crescent wings.

Necrozma's signature move—Photon Geyser!

A golden orb formed and then two massive beams of golden light shot through the air.

Boom!

The Psychic-type Photon Geyser collided with the Blizzard. It barely held off the snowstorm's onslaught, but just barely.

"This Kyurem must be over level 90!" Dylan's eyes flickered. When he had first caught Necrozma, it was level 65. After two days of intense training, Necrozma had reached level 83, which was already impressive.

But there was no way to re-challenge the Elite Four, and the trainers in the post-game didn't have higher levels. He couldn't even use Rare Candies to level up.

Relying on wild Pokémon for experience made leveling up increasingly slow, especially since Necrozma required more experience than regular Pokémon.

Boom!

The collision of Photon Geyser and Blizzard caused a massive explosion, the shockwaves distorting the sky.

"Necrozma is amazing!!" Reshiram had already transformed into a cheerleader, marveling at how Necrozma managed to withstand White Kyurem's Blizzard.

Even Reshiram's strongest move, Blue Flare, couldn't stand up to White Kyurem's Blizzard.

"You're one to talk."

Dylan glanced at the magnificent white dragon. Now that it wasn't fighting, it had instantly used its fire to melt the frost off its body. It looked as pristine as ever, with no sign of injury, and its energy was high.

Dylan seriously suspected that its earlier cry for help was just for show.

"Dylan, I really can't beat the Ice Dragon," Reshiram said innocently.

"But I didn't expect you to be struggling after just two moves. As long as Kyurem doesn't try to absorb you, you don't need to be afraid," Dylan said, exasperated.

If Kyurem really did try to absorb Reshiram, Dylan would immediately have Necrozma intervene.

"If you could hold out a little longer and deal some real damage, with Necrozma's help, we'd have a much better chance of catching Kyurem today."

Dylan had a clear reason for sending White Dragon into battle first. He wanted to see how powerful it was and hoped that if White Dragon could hold its own against Kyurem for a while, it could gather useful information about Kyurem's strengths and weaknesses.

However, Dylan didn't expect that without the DNA Splicers, Reshiram couldn't even last a few moves against Kyurem.

It was clear that the gap between even legendary Pokémon like Reshiram and top-tier legendaries like Kyurem was far greater than Dylan had imagined.

"It's okay, Dylan. Even though I can't beat Kyurem, next time we face Zekrom, leave it to me!" Reshiram said, awkwardly turning its head. Although its condition wasn't bad, it could still hold out for a bit longer in the fight if necessary.

But it really didn't want to battle Kyurem—there was no point in seeking a beating!

Dylan's eyelid twitched. He decided to lower his expectations for his ride and backup. He would have to rely on the real main force.

At that moment, the aftermath of Photon Geyser and Blizzard was still surging violently.

"So powerful!"

Even from a safe distance, the powerful wind blew toward Dylan and Lillie, leaving Lillie speechless in awe.

What they saw was a strange scene where golden and icy blue energy tides clashed, shattering the cliffs below due to the impact of the energy waves.

Terrifying!

"Roar!"

Clearly, White Kyurem wasn't satisfied with the situation. Its violent roar echoed as it charged into the sky.

In a flash, a streak of blue lightning passed over its body, and in the blink of an eye, a black-gray dragon Pokémon appeared.

Its massive, muscular body had arms and wings that were part ice and part black. It was none other than Black Kyurem!

'Beep~ Black Kyurem, Dragon/Ice-type, Boundary Pokémon.

Ability: Teravolt

Signature Moves: Freeze Shock, Outrage, Blizzard, Fusion Bolt, Dragon Claw, Scale Shot, Avalanche, Shadow Claw...

Description: It is said to fight for the ideal world that Pokémon and humans will achieve in the future. (Black Kyurem)'

Dylan's Rotom Dex dutifully explained Black Kyurem's information.

"Kyurem has this form too?"

Lillie was shocked. This meant that Kyurem had three forms, including its normal state!

"A Kyurem that can transform at will is truly amazing," Dylan couldn't help but marvel. This was why it was called the strongest dragon on Earth.

Normally, in each generation of Pokémon, the third legendary could easily overwhelm the other two.

For example, Rayquaza could defeat both the land and sea titans without much effort. Giratina could suppress Dialga and Palkia, and Kyurem could effortlessly crush Reshiram and Zekrom. Even Zygarde's Pokédex entry mentioned its power surpassing Xerneas and Yveltal, and its ability Aura Break signified its advantage over the two.

Necrozma could also easily overpower the sun and moon gods, and Eternatus—well, Eternatus was an exception.

The Sword and Shield kings were particularly strong against Eternatus, especially Zacian, whose strength was specifically geared towards Eternatus.

Steel-types are immune to Poison, Fairy-types are immune to Dragon, and coupled with the equally strong Zamazenta, what could Eternatus do?

Then there's the ninth generation's Terapagos, with Koraidon and Miraidon clearly not on par with it. Although Terapagos didn't perform outstandingly, it was still the "source of Terastallization."

It's hard to judge the strength of third legendaries across regions, but a Kyurem that can transform freely is definitely not weak. If Kyurem could absorb both Zekrom and Reshiram to return to its true dragon form, it would at least be on par with Ultra Necrozma.

But for now, it wasn't there yet, and besides, Necrozma wasn't in its ultimate form either.

"We still have to fight. Even though there's a level gap, Kyurem isn't in its perfect form yet, having not fully absorbed Zekrom and Reshiram," Dylan thought, narrowing his eyes. This ice dragon's greatest strength was its ability to freely switch between its Black and White forms. Black Kyurem excelled at close combat, while White Kyurem specialized in ranged attacks.

But Kyurem wasn't in its fused form with Zekrom and Reshiram, so Dawn Wings Necrozma might still stand a chance—since it was fully fused with Lunala.

A legal 2v1!

(End of chapter)
